Feasible improvements update the retained recipe; every measured trial informs the Skill Bank that guides the next edit. The model weights remain fixed. On a FreePDK45-derived benchmark with ten commercial-tool evaluations per case, \system reduces the mean per-case maximum edge placement error from 18.294 to 5.361 nm on Poly and from 22.052 to 15.692 nm on Metal1. Every final recipe satisfies the predefined quality constraints and improves the maximum error by at least 0.1 nm.

</details>


### [30] [Prefill-Free Cross-Family KV Cache Transfer for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32259)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Haneul Yoo,Sungjoo Yoo,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 跨模型族 KV cache 免预填充传输以加速多 agent 通信，属多 agent 系统效率基础设施，方法可迁移但偏底层推理优化。

TL;DR: HeteroFold是一种免预填充的跨模型家族KV缓存迁移方法，通过结构对齐、缓存映射与校准，在冻结发送方和接收方的情况下实现高效KV复用，并在多项基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常用异构模型承担专门角色，但文本通信会让接收方重复预填充发送方已处理的共享上下文；直接复用发送方KV缓存虽可避免冗余，却面临跨模型家族在分词、深度和KV表示上的不一致。

Method: HeteroFold保持发送方和接收方参数冻结，先对齐模型结构，再将发送方KV缓存映射到接收方空间，并进行校准以维持接收方原有行为，从而实现免预填充的跨家族缓存迁移。

Result: 在六个迁移方向、四个长上下文基准及多数短上下文设置上，HeteroFold均取得最佳缓存迁移性能，并在多智能体基准上匹配文本通信；32K上下文下Llama-3.1-8B→Ministral-3-14B迁移比Native Prefill快10.7×，比Dense Latent与KV Ridge快1.18–1.47×。

Conclusion: 结果表明，HeteroFold能在无需接收方预填充的情况下实现高效的跨模型家族KV缓存复用。

中文摘要：近期，多智能体LLM系统越来越倾向于组合异构模型以承担专门的智能体角色。然而，基于文本的通信要求每个接收方预填充发送方已经处理过的共享上下文。复用发送方的键值（KV）缓存可以避免这种冗余，但跨模型家族的免预填充迁移必须处理分词、模型深度和KV表示方面的差异。为了解决这些问题，我们提出HeteroFold，一种免预填充的跨家族KV缓存迁移方法，它使发送方和接收方均保持冻结。HeteroFold对齐模型结构，将发送方缓存映射到接收方空间，并对其进行校准以保持接收方行为。在六个迁移方向上，HeteroFold在所有四个长上下文基准和大多数短上下文设置上取得了最佳的缓存迁移性能。它还在多智能体基准上匹配了基于文本的通信。在32K上下文长度下，Llama-3.1-8B→Ministral-3-14B迁移比Native Prefill快10.7倍，比最先进的免预填充基线Dense Latent和KV Ridge快1.18–1.47倍。这些结果表明，HeteroFold能够在无需接收方预填充的情况下实现高效的跨家族KV复用。

Original abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly combine heterogeneous models for specialized agent roles. However, text-based communication requires each receiver to prefill shared context already processed by the sender. Reusing the sender's key-value (KV) cache avoids this redundancy, but prefill-free transfer across model families must handle differences in tokenization, model depth, and KV representations. To address these issues, we propose \textit{HeteroFold}, a prefill-free cross-family KV cache transfer method that keeps both the sender and receiver frozen. HeteroFold aligns model structures, maps the sender cache into the receiver space, and calibrates it to preserve receiver behavior. Across six transfer directions, HeteroFold achieves the best cache-transfer performance on all four long-context benchmarks and most short-context settings. It also matches text-based communication on the multi-agent benchmark. At 32K context length, Llama-3.1-8B$\rightarrow$Ministral-3-14B transfer is $10.7\times$ faster than Native Prefill and $1.18$--$1.47\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art prefill-free baselines, Dense Latent and KV Ridge. These results show that HeteroFold enables efficient cross-family KV reuse without receiver prefill.

</details>


### [31] [CUA-SWE: When Computer-Use Agents Meet Visual Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32600)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Chenhao Liang,Zelong Xu,Aojie Yuan,Xiaolin Zhou,Haiyue Zhang,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 66 pages

Selection reason: 提出结合GUI视觉反馈与代码修改的SWE Agent benchmark与环境，含确定性测试验证，直接面向Coding Agent的视觉诊断-修复-验证长程任务。

TL;DR: CUA-SWE 是面向结合计算机使用的软件工程基准、环境与评估流水线，要求智能体在四个软件工程领域内修改代码与配置、执行命令、与运行中的软件交互并检查视觉反馈；每个任务含确定性、任务特定测试，用于评估前沿智能体如何利用截图与图形交互完成可验证的软件修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体与计算机使用智能体大多被孤立研究，集成式开发过程尚未充分探索；诊断运行时交互故障需将视觉观察与负责代码关联，并再次使用应用验证修复，同时需研究当规格或操作信息仅能通过运行中应用的可视界面获得时，智能体能否完成任务。

Method: 提出 CUA-SWE，一个涵盖四个软件工程领域的基准、环境和评估流水线；任务要求智能体在同一任务内修改代码与配置、执行命令、与运行中的软件交互并检查视觉反馈；每个任务包含确定性、任务特定的测试，以验证所得软件满足需求并保持指定行为；评估刻画前沿智能体如何结合源码级执行、应用截图与图形交互产生经过验证的软件变更，并分析跨领域和任务信息需求下的性能及与成功修复相关的开发行为。

Result: 评估刻画了前沿智能体如何结合源码级执行、应用截图和图形交互来产生经过验证的软件变更；考察了不同领域和任务信息需求下的性能，以及与成功修复相关的开发行为；CUA-SWE 为所得软件提供可执行的正确性判据。

Conclusion: CUA-SWE 提供了一个统一测试平台，用于研究智能体如何利用视觉反馈和交互来指导软件工程，并为所得软件提供可执行的正确性判据。

中文摘要：软件开发不仅仅需要编辑代码：开发者会反复运行软件、与其界面交互、目视检查其行为，并利用这些观察来决定下一步修改什么以及某项修改是否有效。现有的编码智能体和计算机使用智能体大多被孤立地研究，使得这一集成式开发过程仍未得到充分探索。诊断运行时交互故障要求智能体将视觉观察与应负责的代码联系起来，然后再次使用应用程序来验证修复。我们提出 CUA-SWE，一个面向结合计算机使用的软件工程的基准、环境和评估流水线。除了研究 GUI 反馈如何支持诊断与修复之外，我们还追问：当所需的规格信息或操作信息只能通过运行中应用程序的可视界面获得时，智能体能否完成软件工程任务。CUA-SWE 涵盖四个软件工程领域，并要求智能体在同一任务内修改代码和配置、执行命令、与运行中的软件交互并检查视觉反馈。每个任务都包含确定性的、任务特定的测试，用于验证所得软件是否满足需求并保持指定行为。我们的评估刻画了前沿智能体如何将源码级执行与应用程序截图和图形交互结合起来，以产生经过验证的软件变更。我们考察不同领域和任务信息需求下的性能，以及与被成功修复相关的开发行为。CUA-SWE 提供了一个统一测试平台，用于研究智能体如何利用视觉反馈和交互来指导软件工程，并为所得软件提供可执行的正确性判据。

Original abstract: Software development requires more than editing code: developers repeatedly run software, interact with its interfaces, visually inspect its behavior, and use these observations to decide what to change next and whether a change works. Existing coding agents and computer-use agents are largely studied in isolation, leaving this integrated development process underexplored. Diagnosing a runtime interaction failure requires agents to connect visual observations with the responsible code, then use the application again to verify the repair. We introduce CUA-SWE, a benchmark, environment, and evaluation pipeline for software engineering with computer use. Beyond studying how GUI feedback supports diagnosis and repair, we ask whether agents can complete software engineering tasks when required specification or operational information is available only through the running application's visual interface. CUA-SWE spans four software engineering domains and requires agents to modify code and configuration, execute commands, interact with running software, and inspect visual feedback within the same task. Each task includes deterministic, task-specific tests that verify whether the resulting software satisfies the requirements and preserves specified behavior. Our evaluation characterizes how frontier agents combine source-level execution with application screenshots and graphical interaction to produce verified software changes. We examine performance across domains and task information requirements, alongside the development behaviors associated with successful repairs. CUA-SWE provides a unified testbed for studying how agents use visual feedback and interaction to guide software engineering, with executable correctness criteria for the resulting software.

</details>


### [32] [When Does a Skill Add Value? Task-Conditional Gain Prediction for Selective Skill Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32274)
*Anjie Xu,Zhiyu Zhang,Ruiqing Ding,Fengli Xu,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures. Code: https://github.com/TankTechnology/skilldelta

Selection reason: 预测技能在特定任务上是否带来增益以选择性启用技能，直接对应 agent 技能/经验复用与选择性调用问题。

TL;DR: 提出 SkillDelta，利用同一智能体有无技能的配对执行预测任务条件技能增益并迁移到新任务，从而改进技能激活决策；在15个设置中全部优于随机激活，平均绝对增益4.3%，主要收益来自任务组间分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能并非总是有益，可能无效甚至损害性能，并带来额外令牌成本，因此需要在智能体行动前预测技能是否会带来帮助。

Method: 从同一智能体有技能与无技能的配对执行中估计任务条件技能增益，使用局部预测器将历史增益迁移到新任务且无需重新训练智能体；理论分析将支持覆盖、表示不匹配和执行噪声与预测误差、决策遗憾关联起来。

Result: 在五个基准和三个目标智能体上，配对历史在12/15个设置中相比仅使用技能辅助结果改善了观测增益排序；在匹配期望技能使用率下，SkillDelta 在全部15个设置中优于随机激活，平均绝对增益为4.3%；优势主要来自跨任务组分配，额外组内选择价值在 ToolQA 上最强、其他基准较弱。

Conclusion: SkillDelta 能在不重新训练智能体的前提下预测技能增益并改进技能使用决策，但其额外组内选择价值有限且因任务或基准而异。

中文摘要：智能体技能有望提升任务性能。然而我们发现，它们常常没有带来任何益处，甚至可能在产生额外令牌成本的同时损害性能。我们能否在智能体行动之前预测某项技能是否会有所帮助？我们提出 SkillDelta，一个用于从同一智能体在有该技能和无该技能下的配对执行中预测任务条件技能增益的框架。一个局部预测器将这些历史增益迁移到新任务，而无需重新训练智能体。在明确的迁移假设下，我们的分析将支持覆盖、表示不匹配和执行噪声与预测误差和决策遗憾联系起来。在五个基准和三个目标智能体上，配对历史在15个设置中的12个里，相比仅使用技能辅助结果，提升了观测增益排序。在匹配的期望技能使用率下，SkillDelta 在全部15个设置中都相较随机激活提升了成功率，平均绝对增益为4.3%。这一优势的大部分来自跨任务组的分配。关于额外组内选择价值的证据在 ToolQA 上最强，在其他地方较弱。代码见 https://github.com/TankTechnology/skilldelta。

Original abstract: Agent skills are expected to improve task performance. Yet we find that they often provide no benefit, and can even hurt performance while incurring additional token costs. Can we predict whether a skill will help before the agent acts? We introduce SkillDelta, a framework for predicting task-conditional skill gains from paired executions of the same agent with and without the skill. A local predictor transfers these historical gains to new tasks without retraining the agent. Under explicit transfer assumptions, our analysis links support coverage, representation mismatch, and execution noise to prediction error and decision regret. Across five benchmarks and three target agents, paired history improves observed-gain ranking over skill-assisted outcomes alone in 12 of 15 settings. At matched expected skill-use rates, SkillDelta improves success over random activation in all 15 settings, with an average absolute gain of 4.3%. Most of this advantage comes from allocation across task groups. Evidence for additional within-group selection value is strongest on ToolQA and weaker elsewhere. Code is available at https://github.com/TankTechnology/skilldelta.

</details>


### [33] [SWE-MILE: Asynchronous Potential-Induced Milestone Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32631)
*Chaoqun Cui,Hao Zhou,Meiqi Chen,Fandong Meng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 面向长程SWE Agent的RLVR细粒度过程信用分配，用文件暴露与测试状态势能差归因里程碑，并异步影子探测隐藏验证延迟，与代码Agent训练与验证强相关。

TL;DR: 提出SWE-MILE，一种异步势能诱导里程碑信用分配框架，无需辅助奖励模型或外部评估器，从工作流运行时提取细粒度过程监督，通过文件暴露和测试状态对齐势能分配信用，并采用异步影子探测隐藏验证延迟；实验表明在长时程SWE任务上显著提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程SWE智能体使用RLVR训练时通常仅获得终端结果监督，难以区分有效动作与冗余探索或功能回归，因此需要细粒度过程监督，但又不依赖辅助奖励模型或外部评估器。

Method: 提出SWE-MILE框架：将任务相关文件暴露和测试状态对齐分别量化为导航势能与验证势能；利用势能差异将里程碑进展和回归归因到单个动作，并用折扣反向信用将监督传播到先前步骤；引入异步影子探测，在隔离沙箱中重放改变仓库的动作并并行执行验证以隐藏验证延迟；最终过程信用增强终端结果优势，提供有效学习信号。

Result: 在两个具有代表性的长时程SWE任务上，实验表明智能体性能取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 工作流运行时信号可作为长时程SWE智能体过程监督的实用来源。

中文摘要：长时程软件工程（SWE）智能体使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练时，通常仅接收终端结果监督，这使得难以区分有成效的动作与冗余探索或功能回归。我们提出SWE-MILE，一个异步势能诱导的里程碑信用分配框架，该框架从工作流运行时中提取细粒度的过程监督，无需辅助奖励模型或外部评估器。SWE-MILE将任务相关文件暴露和测试状态对齐分别量化为导航势能和验证势能。这些势能的差异将里程碑进展和回归归因于单个动作，同时折扣后的反向信用将监督传播到先前步骤。为了高效获取中间验证状态，SWE-MILE进一步引入异步影子探测，该机制在隔离沙箱中重放改变仓库的动作，并与智能体的主要交互并行运行验证，从而在很大程度上隐藏验证延迟。由此产生的过程信用增强了终端结果优势，并提供了信息丰富的学习信号。在两个具有代表性的长时程SWE任务上的实验表明，智能体性能有显著提升，突显了工作流运行时信号作为长时程SWE智能体过程监督的实用来源。

Original abstract: Long-horizon software engineering (SWE) agents trained with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically receive only terminal outcome supervision, making it difficult to distinguish productive actions from redundant exploration or functional regressions. We propose SWE-MILE, an asynchronous potential-induced milestone credit assignment framework that derives fine-grained process supervision from workflow runtime, without auxiliary reward models or external evaluators. SWE-MILE quantifies task-relevant file exposure and test-state alignment as navigation and verification potentials, respectively. Differences in these potentials attribute milestone progress and regressions to individual actions, while discounted backward credit propagates supervision to preceding steps. To efficiently acquire intermediate verification states, SWE-MILE further introduces asynchronous shadow probing, which replays repository-changing actions in an isolated sandbox and runs verification in parallel with the agent's primary interaction, largely hiding verification latency. The resulting process credit augments terminal outcome advantages and provides informative learning signals. Experiments on two representative long-horizon SWE tasks demonstrate substantial improvements in agent performance, highlighting workflow runtime signals as a practical source of process supervision for long-horizon SWE agents.

</details>


### [34] [GLIDE: Generalized Layer-wise Intrinsic Distributional Evaluation for Heterogeneous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32295)
*Wei Zhu,Yiming Wang,Rui Wang,Lixing Yu,Kun Yue,Zhiwen Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted by EMNLP 2026 Findings

Selection reason: 用层内残差一致性给出无需外部验证器的步级价值信号，服务于 MCTS 分支选择与计算分配，可迁移到 agent 搜索与自评。

TL;DR: GLIDE 提出一种无需外部验证器或任务特定监督的 LLM 智能体步骤级评估方法，通过逐层残差一致性与分布校准生成跨智能体奖励，用于 MCTS 分支选择和自适应分支，并提升多跳推理、序贯决策和符号逻辑任务的表现与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需要可靠的步骤级评估来比较候选分支并分配计算资源，但轻量级评估困难：外部验证器增加推理成本，智能体置信度或自评估分数在异构智能体生成候选时容易校准不佳。

Method: 提出 GLIDE（广义逐层内在分布评估）。该方法从逐层残差一致性中提取内在步骤证据，衡量局部残差更新是否一致支持候选步骤引发的全局残差变化；再依据生成智能体近期分数分布校准证据，并转化为同时考虑绝对残差证据与智能体相对位置的悲观奖励；该奖励为 MCTS 分支选择提供跨智能体价值信号，归一化预测不确定性用于引导自适应分支。

Result: 在多跳推理、序贯决策和符号逻辑实验中，GLIDE 提升了任务性能、步骤级排序质量和计算效率，且无需外部验证器或任务特定监督。

Conclusion: GLIDE 表明可利用 LLM 智能体内部逐层残差信号实现轻量、跨智能体的步骤级评估，为 MCTS 分支选择与计算分配提供有效价值信号，并避免外部验证器与任务特定监督。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体需要可靠的步骤级评估，以比较候选分支并有效分配计算资源。然而，轻量级评估仍然具有挑战性。外部验证器会引入额外的推理成本，而智能体产生的置信度或自评估分数可能校准不佳，尤其当候选由异构智能体生成时。我们提出面向 LLM 智能体的广义逐层内在分布评估（Generalized Layer-wise Intrinsic Distributional Evaluation, GLIDE）。GLIDE 从逐层残差一致性中推导内在步骤证据，该一致性衡量局部残差更新是否一致地支持由候选步骤引起的全局残差变化。它根据生成智能体近期的分数分布对该证据进行校准，并将其转换为一种悲观奖励，该奖励同时考虑绝对残差证据和智能体相对位置。该奖励为 MCTS 分支选择提供跨智能体价值信号，而归一化预测不确定性引导自适应分支。在多跳推理、序贯决策和符号逻辑上的实验表明，GLIDE 无需外部验证器或任务特定监督，即可提升任务性能、步骤级排序质量和计算效率。

Original abstract: LLM agents require reliable step-level evaluation to compare candidate branches and allocate computation effectively. However, lightweight evaluation remains challenging. External verifiers introduce additional inference cost, while agent-produced confidence or self-evaluation scores can be miscalibrated, especially when candidates are generated by heterogeneous agents. We propose \textbf{G}eneralized \textbf{L}ayer-wise \textbf{I}ntrinsic \textbf{D}istributional \textbf{E}valuation (\textbf{GLIDE}) for LLM agents. \textsc{GLIDE} derives intrinsic step evidence from layer-wise residual coherence, which measures whether local residual updates consistently support the global residual change induced by a candidate step. It calibrates this evidence against the recent score distribution of the generating agent and converts it into a pessimistic reward that jointly accounts for absolute residual evidence and agent-relative standing. The reward provides a cross-agent value signal for MCTS branch selection, while normalized predictive uncertainty guides adaptive branching. Experiments on multi-hop reasoning, sequential decision making, and symbolic logic show that \textsc{GLIDE} improves task performance, step-level ranking quality, and computational efficiency without external verifiers or task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [35] [The Decomposition Tax: LLM Pipelines Lose Up to 40 Accuracy Points at Their Own Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32825)
*Tianqi Bu,YuXuan Peng,Junteng Tu,Henghui Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures; preprint under review

Selection reason: 系统量化LLM多阶段流水线在阶段接口处的信息丢失（decomposition tax）并给出重锚定修复策略，可迁移到多阶段Agent工作流的接口可靠性设计。

TL;DR: 四阶段 LLM 流水线因阶段间接口丢失原始问题信息而产生“分解税”，最高达 40.5 个准确率点；固定其他因素可逐阶段定位该税。修复策略是：在有损接口之后重新接地，并让列出数值量的阶段保留关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段 LLM 流水线由构建者逐阶段设计，但其性能代价出现在阶段之间的接口处；需要量化这种接口损失，并找到可操作的修复方法。

Method: 固定模型、问题、阶段、阶段提示与补全预算，仅操纵各阶段能否继续看到原始问题，将准确率差异定义为分解税；在 GSM-Hard 和 MATH-500 上对 21 个开放权重模型、每单元 200 个配对项目进行检验，并使用安慰剂、指令改写、不同重新接地位置及封存留出测试。

Result: 四阶段流水线接口处最高损失 40.5 点；118 个主要家族检验中 70 个通过 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正、54 个通过 Holm 校正；GSM-Hard 上安慰剂不恢复性能。改写指令使 gemma-3-12B 的税从 4.5 变为 36.5 点，加入关系短语在 MATH-500 上改善 9/9 模型。有损接口后重新接地优于之前，在 7/7 模型上成立；MATH-500 上较早修复比不修复更差，在 7/7 模型上成立。gemma-4-12B 仍损失 37.0 点，修复在最新三个模型上成立；更强预测规则被留出测试反驳。

Conclusion: 应通过一次测量一个阶段来定位分解税；处方为：在有损接口之后重新接地该阶段，并要求必须列出数值量的阶段保留关系。

中文摘要：一个四阶段 LLM 流水线在其自身接口处放弃高达 40.5 个准确率点（gemma-3-12B 在 MATH-500 上，Holm 校正 p = 1.66e-19；这是主要家族中最大的税）。我们保持模型、问题、阶段、阶段提示和补全预算固定，只改变每个阶段是否仍能看到原始问题，并将准确率差异称为分解税。在来自九家机构的 21 个开放权重模型上，在 GSM-Hard 和 MATH-500 上每个单元 n = 200 个配对项目，118 个主要家族检验中有 70 个通过 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正，54 个通过 Holm 校正。在 GSM-Hard 上，安慰剂没有恢复任何东西：它携带至少 60% 的额外 token，并且至多包含问题中的一个词。构建者一次一个阶段地设计流水线，而它的账单出现在阶段之间的接口处。重写一个阶段的指令会使 gemma-3-12B 的税从 4.5 点变为 36.5 点，而在列出数值量的阶段中加入“它们之间陈述的每一个关系”会在 MATH-500 上降低 9/9 模型的税。重新接地（re-grounding），即再次向一个阶段展示原始问题，应放在损失之后。在有一个有损接口时，重新接地其后的阶段在两个基准上均以 7/7 模型胜过重新接地其前的阶段；在 MATH-500 上，较早的修复在 7/7 模型上比不修复更差。较新的模型仍然付出代价：gemma-4-12B 损失 37.0 点，并且该修复在我们测试的全部三个最新模型上成立。一个封存的留出测试反驳了我们注册的一个更强规则，该规则根据接口和接收者类型预测付出代价的阶段，因此我们通过一次测量一个阶段来定位该税。处方有两部分：在有损接口之后重新接地该阶段；如果一个阶段必须列出数值量，则告诉它保留关系。

Original abstract: A four-stage LLM pipeline gives up as much as 40.5 accuracy points at its own interfaces (gemma-3-12B on MATH-500, Holm-corrected p = 1.66e-19; the largest tax in the primary family). We hold model, problem, stages, stage prompts and completion budget fixed, vary only whether each stage can still see the original problem, and call the accuracy difference the decomposition tax. Across 21 open-weight models from nine organisations, on GSM-Hard and MATH-500 at n = 200 paired items per cell, 70 of 118 primary-family tests survive Benjamini-Hochberg correction and 54 survive Holm. On GSM-Hard, a placebo recovers nothing: it carries at least 60% of the extra tokens and at most one word of the problem. Builders design a pipeline one stage at a time, and its bill arrives at the interfaces between stages. Rewriting one stage's instruction moves gemma-3-12B's tax from 4.5 to 36.5 points, and adding "every relationship stated between them" to a stage that lists the numerical quantities lowers the tax on 9 of 9 models on MATH-500. Re-grounding, which shows a stage the original problem again, belongs after the loss. With one lossy interface, re-grounding the stage after it beats re-grounding the stage before it on 7 of 7 models on both benchmarks; on MATH-500 the earlier repair is worse than none on 7 of 7. Newer models still pay: gemma-4-12B gives up 37.0 points, and the repair holds on all three of the newest models we test. A sealed held-out test refuted a stronger rule we registered, which predicted the paying stage from the interface and receiver types, so we locate the tax by measuring one stage at a time. The prescription has two parts: re-ground the stage after the lossy interface, and if a stage must list the quantities, tell it to keep the relationships.

</details>


### [36] [Agentsensus: Consensus-Compressed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32297)
*Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 32 pages, 19 figures, 9 tables. Code: https://github.com/impanyu/agentsensus

Selection reason: 多 agent 共享长期记忆与事件合并机制，虽应用于故事世界，但共享记忆压缩设计对多 agent 协作有启发。

TL;DR: 提出 Agentsensus：共享统一长期记忆的故事世界模拟框架，同一事件合并为全体目击者共有记录并链接语义相关记忆；在四个世界40-80轮评测中减少22-44%条目、实现14-28%共享、94-99%链接，质量不降或更优，消融显示合并是关键，共享随轮数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准智能体故事世界为每个角色维护私有记忆流，导致共享事件按目击者重复存储，存储冗余高、记忆之间缺乏关联。

Method: Agentsensus 使用统一长期记忆：同一事件记录合并为所有目击者共同拥有的单条记录，并链接语义相关记录；在四个世界（两部中国古典小说、《哈姆雷特》、真实世界冲突时间线）以40-80轮、等粒度协议与三种按角色记忆设计对比，并做消融。

Result: 相比最接近基线少写22-44%条目；唯一实现共享（14-28%记录被多角色持有，部分被10个角色持有）和链接（94-99%）的设计；模拟质量不劣于甚至优于基线；禁用合并使存储增至3.1倍、共享率归零；共享率随10/20/40轮从6%升至9%再升至14%。

Conclusion: 统一与合并式长期记忆能显著降低存储重复、促进跨角色记忆共享与链接，且质量不降；共享随模拟轮数累积，具有可扩展优势。

中文摘要：一个智能体故事世界是一个动态系统，用于模拟谁在何时、从谁那里学到了什么——然而标准设计为每个角色提供一个私有记忆流。因此，一个共享事件会按每个目击者各存储一次，在存储方面会产生大量重复。我们提出 Agentsensus，一个故事世界模拟框架，其中存在一个统一的长期记忆。同一事件的记录会合并为一条由所有目击者共同拥有的记录，语义相关的记忆记录会被链接。我们在四个世界上进行评估——两部中国古典小说、《哈姆雷特》以及一个真实世界冲突时间线——在等粒度协议下与三种按角色记忆设计进行对比，运行40至80轮。Agentsensus 比最接近的基线少写入22-44%的条目，并且是唯一一种记忆变得共享（14-28%的记录由不止一个角色持有，有些由10个角色持有）且链接（94-99%）的设计；在经评判的模拟质量上，与基线不可区分甚至更好。一项消融实验将此归因于合并本身：禁用它会使存储量增至3.1倍，并使共享率恰好降为零。共享也会随视界复利式增长，而非过早饱和；当同一个世界分别在10、20和40轮重新运行时，共享率从6%升至9%，再升至14%。

Original abstract: A agentic story world is a dynamic system simulating who learned what, when, and from whom -- yet the standard design gives each character a private memory stream. A shared event is therefore stored once per witness, large duplication will be incurred in terms of storage. We present Agentsensus, a story-world simulation framework in which there is an unified long-term memory. Records of the same event merge into one owned by all its witnesses, and semantically relevant memory records are linked. We evaluate on four worlds -- two classical Chinese novels, Hamlet, and a real-world conflict timeline -- run for 40 to 80 rounds against three per-character memory designs under an equal-granularity protocol. Agentsensus writes 22-44% fewer entries than the closest baseline and is the only design whose memory becomes shared (14-28% of records held by more than one character, some by 10) and linked (94-99%), at judged simulation quality indistinguishable or even better than the baselines. An ablation attributes this to the merge itself: disabling it multiplies the store by 3.1x and takes sharing to exactly zero. Sharing also compounds with the horizon rather than saturating early, rising 6% to 9% to 14% as one world is re-run at 10, 20 and 40 rounds.

</details>


### [37] [Relic: From Multi-Agent Collaboration to Persistent Organizational Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32965)
*Hongyi Du,Tianyi Zhang,Weijia Zhang,Yi Yang,Haofei Yu,Kunlun Zhu,Tianxiang Dai,Shang Jiang,Zhelun Gao,Jiaxin Pei,Shang Zhu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 83 pages, 8 figures. Preprint

Selection reason: 将多Agent协作反复出现的失败沉淀为组织级可执行协议并治理其采纳/修订/退役，含跨成员迁移实验，对多Agent代码协作与持久记忆机制很有参考价值。

TL;DR: Relic 将反复出现的协作失败转化为组织拥有、可执行且可修订的协议，并在交付、新成员迁移和基准表现上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作中的冲突通常可由单次对话解决，但当参与者更换后，经验难以持续约束团队，因此需要把协作教训转化为持久的组织状态。

Method: Relic 让成员反思可见工作、提出规则并治理采纳，将触发器、责任、所需证据和执行后果绑定到运行时，形成可执行协议，同时支持修订与退役。

Result: 在 360 次受控运行中，完整契约交付率从 14.06% 提升至 19.76%（+5.71 个百分点）；新成员迁移下行为正确性分别为无协议 25.4%、文本规则 34.6%、可执行绑定 41.2%；CooperBench 全基准为 371/469（79.1%），同模型 47 对子集为 28/47 对 26/47，超过 Solo。

Conclusion: 协作经验可以成为持久的组织状态，并在创建它的成员之外继续发挥效用。

中文摘要：多个智能体在组织中可能经常发生冲突：例如，一个编码智能体更改了仓库中的接口，但另一个智能体继续在旧版本上开发，导致现有测试变得过时。一次对话可以解决这一事件，但当参与者更换时，是什么让经验教训继续约束团队？我们提出 Relic，它把反复出现的协作失败转化为组织拥有的、可执行的协议。成员反思可见的工作，提出规则，并治理其采纳。被采纳的协议将触发器、责任、所需证据和执行后果绑定到运行时，同时仍可修订和退役。在一个追踪案例中，反复出现的集成摩擦产生了一条接口审查规则，该规则约束后续的拉取请求，并随着工作推进而被修订。在覆盖十个软件工作负载和三个模型的 360 次受控运行中，Relic 相比没有协议生命周期的匹配结构化团队，将完整契约交付率从 14.06% 提升到 19.76%（+5.71 个百分点），在每个模型分层中都改善了所有四个经过验证的生产端点。在新成员迁移下，行为正确性在无继承协议时为 25.4%，在相同规则以可读文本提供时为 34.6%，在可执行绑定下为 41.2%，比纯文本高出 6.5 个百分点。在完整的 CooperBench 基准上，排除 183 个损坏的基准对后，Relic 达到 371/469（79.1%），在同类结构化系统中取得了已报告的最佳结果。在 47 对的同模型子集上，Relic 也超过 Solo（28/47 对 26/47），扭转了官方同类基线表现出的协调损失。总之，这些结果表明，协作经验可以成为持久的组织状态，并且在创建它的成员之外仍然有用。

Original abstract: Multiple agents may often conflict in an organization: for example, one coding agent changes an interface in a repository, but another continues to develop on the old version where existing tests become stale. A conversation can resolve the episode, but when the participants change, what makes the lesson continue to govern the team? We introduce Relic, which turns recurring collaboration failures into organization-owned, executable protocols. Members reflect on visible work, propose rules, and govern their adoption. Adopted protocols bind triggers, responsibilities, required evidence, and execution consequences to the runtime, while remaining open to revision and retirement. In one traced case, repeated integration friction produces an interface-review rule that governs later pull requests and is revised as work continues. Across 360 controlled runs over ten software workloads and three models, Relic raises complete-contract delivery from 14.06% to 19.76% (+5.71 percentage points) over a matched structured team without the protocol lifecycle, improving all four verified production endpoints in every model stratum. Under fresh-member transfer, behavioral correctness is 25.4% with no inherited protocol, 34.6% with the same rules provided as readable text, and 41.2% with executable bindings, a +6.5-point advantage over text alone. On the full CooperBench benchmark, after excluding 183 broken benchmark pairs, Relic achieves 371/469 (79.1%), establishing the best reported result among peer-structured systems. On the 47-pair same-model subset, Relic also exceeds Solo (28/47 vs. 26/47), reversing the coordination loss exhibited by the official peer baseline. Together, these results show how collaboration experience can become persistent organizational state that remains useful beyond the members who created it.

</details>


### [38] [SRE-Marathon: A Continuous, Change-Driven Benchmark for Autonomous Site Reliability Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33023)
*Yifang Tian,Yingjian Bai,Yifeng He,Zichun Chong,Yuanchen Gao,Yiran Li,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程连续SRE Agent基准，含重叠故障注入、持久工作区与确定性离线评分，对长程运维/代码修复Agent的评测机制有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出 SRE-Marathon：面向长时程连续 SRE 操作的基准，在实时双区 Kubernetes 上按固定节奏注入重叠故障并离线评分；最佳方法仅得 41.3/100，能关联定位但难在故障活跃时完成修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SRE 智能体基准多为片段式，单故障、单事件、即时评分，无法反映生产中警报嘈杂、故障重叠且常由代码或配置变更引发的连续运维。

Method: SRE-Marathon 让智能体以固定节奏获得累积警报历史和持久工作区，操作实时双区 Kubernetes；故障编排器按种子化、生产校准计划注入重叠故障；精选的代码/配置变更经同一构建流水线部署；每次运行记录为密封包并离线用 Marathon-Score 评分，按关联、定位、修复的有序进展为每个故障记分，指标由记录的系统证据确定性计算。

Result: 在三个应用、每次约六十个故障下，10 种方法最佳仅 41.3/100；智能体常能关联和定位故障，但故障活跃期间几乎无法完成修复。

Conclusion: 长时程连续 SRE 运维仍是重大挑战，当前智能体虽具备一定关联与定位能力，但在重叠且持续活跃的故障下修复闭环能力严重不足。

中文摘要：面向站点可靠性工程（SRE）智能体的基准通常是片段式的：注入一个故障，智能体接收一个事件任务，并对其响应进行评分。生产运维并非如此。事件通过带噪警报浮现，在时间上重叠，并且常源于代码或配置变更。我们提出 SRE-Marathon，一个用于长时程、连续 SRE 操作的基准。智能体以固定节奏被调用，并获得累积警报历史和持久工作区，同时操作一个实时双区 Kubernetes 部署；故障编排器则按带随机种子、经生产校准的计划注入重叠故障。经过精选的代码和配置变更通过与可用于修复的同一构建流水线进行部署。每次运行都被记录到一个密封包中，并离线评分：Marathon-Score 根据每个注入故障在关联、定位和修复方面的有序进展为其记分，所有指标均从记录的系统证据确定性地计算。在三个应用以及每次运行约六十个故障的情况下，10 种方法中最佳者仅达到 100 分中的 41.3 分。智能体常常能关联并定位故障，但当故障仍处于活跃状态时几乎从不完成修复。

Original abstract: Benchmarks for site reliability engineering (SRE) agents are typically episodic: one fault is injected, the agent receives an incident task, and its response is scored. Production operation is not. Incidents surface through noisy alerts, overlap in time, and often originate from code or configuration changes. We present SRE-Marathon, a benchmark for long-horizon, continuous SRE operation. An agent is invoked at a fixed cadence with cumulative alert history and a persistent workspace while operating a live two-zone Kubernetes deployment as a fault orchestrator injects overlapping faults according to a seeded, production-calibrated schedule. Curated code and configuration changes deployed through the same build pipeline available for repair. Each run is recorded into a sealed bundle and scored offline: Marathon-Score credits each injected fault for ordered progress through correlation, localization, and repair, with all metrics computed deterministically from recorded system evidence. Across three applications and about sixty faults per run, the best of 10 methods reaches only 41.3 out of 100. Agents often correlate and localize faults, but almost never complete repairs while the faults remain active.

</details>


### [39] [Compositional Safety Failures in Harness Evolution: Identification and Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33123)
*Zhixiang Zhang,Zesen Liu,Wai Ip Lai,Hongxu chen,Dongdong She*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究自进化Agent harness中跨组件更新的组合式安全失效，并用类型化超图做增量运行时监控，对Agent可靠性/安全与工作流演化机制有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文研究智能体 harness 演化中的组合式安全失效：单独安全的组件更新交互可导致不安全行为；提出类型化超图与运行时监控，仅更新局部交互邻域，以降低检查成本并缓解风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作关注 harness 误演化及单个组件更新验证，忽略跨组件更新交互的安全分析；这种交互可能产生组合式安全失效，是 harness 演化内在风险。传统全组合验证成本过高，随演化不可行。

Method: 识别组合式安全失效；用类型化超图建模组件状态为节点、安全相关高阶交互为超边；harness 变化时只更新变更状态的交互邻域；基于超图引导运行时监控机制。

Result: 在三个安全基准上发现 43 个成对和 18 个不可约三元组合式安全失效；实验显示方法能缓解组合式安全风险，保持任务效用，降低交互检查成本，并揭示安全-效用-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 跨组件交互会带来传统单组件验证遗漏的组合式安全风险；超图引导的局部更新与运行时监控可有效且低成本地缓解该风险，但安全、效用与成本之间存在经验权衡。

中文摘要：自进化的智能体 harness 会持续更新持久化组件，如记忆、提示词、技能和工具。我们将这一过程称为 harness 演化。然而，这种演化可能引入意外的安全风险。现有工作研究 harness 误演化，并验证候选 harness 或归因的单个组件更新，但跨组件更新交互的安全性分析在很大程度上仍未被考察。为填补这一空白，我们研究 harness 演化中的组合式安全失效，即多个单独安全且保持效用的组件更新之间的交互可能产生不良或不安全的智能体行为，从而揭示出 harness 演化固有的一种安全风险。在三个安全相关基准上，我们识别出 43 个成对和 18 个不可约的三元组合式安全失效。传统解决方案在验证跨组件交互时会产生组合复杂度，使得随着 harness 演化，安全检查变得不切实际。为解决这一问题，我们引入一种类型化超图，将组件状态表示为节点，并将与安全相关的高阶交互表示为超边。当 harness 发生变化时，超图仅更新发生变化状态的交互邻域，而非重构全局组合空间。在此基础上，我们开发了一种超图引导的运行时监控机制。实验表明，我们的方法在保持任务效用的同时有效缓解组合式安全风险，并降低交互检查成本，还进一步揭示了不同安全机制之间经验性的安全-效用-成本权衡。

Original abstract: Self-evolving agent harnesses continually update persistent components such as memory, prompts, skills, and tools. We call this process harness evolution. However, such evolution could introduce unexpected safety risks. Existing work studies harness misevolution and validates candidate harnesses or attributed individual component updates, leaving safety analysis of cross-component update interactions largely unexamined. To address this gap, we study compositional safety failures in harness evolution, where interactions among individually safe and utility-preserving component updates can produce undesirable or unsafe agent behavior, revealing a safety risk intrinsic to harness evolution. Across three safety-related benchmarks, we identify 43 pairwise and 18 irreducible 3-way compositional safety failures. Conventional solution incurs combinatorial complexity in validating cross-component interactions, leaving the safety checking impractical as the harness evolves. To solve this, we introduced a typed hypergraph that represents component states as nodes and safety-relevant higher-order interactions as hyperedges. When the harness changes, the hypergraph updates only the interaction neighborhood of the changed states rather than reconstructing the global composition space. Building on that, we develop a hypergraph-guided runtime monitoring mechanism. Experiments show that our method effectively mitigates compositional safety risks while preserving task utility and reducing interaction-checking costs, and further reveal an empirical safety-utility-cost trade-off across different safety mechanisms.

</details>


### [40] [Delayed Supervision for Test-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32312)
*Jinha Kim,Taksh Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. Equal contribution by both authors. Work done at MIT CSAIL

Selection reason: 针对测试时快速权重记忆的延迟监督训练目标，研究长间隔事实保留与更新，可间接启发 agent 长期记忆机制。

TL;DR: 提出延迟监督：在后训练中于长间隔无关事件后提问并监督答案，以增强测试时记忆的保持与修订；在 LaCT、DeltaNet、RWKV-7 上均取得 BABILong/RULER 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一词元预测不显式要求事实在多次记忆更新后仍可访问，测试时记忆缺乏对延迟保持与修订的直接监督。

Method: 在后训练期间，仅在长间隔无关事件后提出模拟器支撑的问题，答案在一次性分支上评估且不进入事件流，并与普通下一词元预测联合监督；在 LaCT-760M 与普通 DeltaNet-1.3B 上用 TextWorld 轨迹和共享 BABILong、RULER 面板评估，并单独报告 RWKV-7 比较。

Result: 相对仅事件训练，延迟问答使 LaCT 的 BABILong 提升 5.48 个百分点、DeltaNet 提升 1.32 个百分点，单针 RULER 分别提升 1.45 和 3.27 个百分点；RWKV-7 在其面板上分别提升 4.60 和 7.00 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持延迟语义监督作为可用测试时记忆的实用外层训练目标，但仍未确定收益中有多少具体来自延迟，而非一般问答与答案终止监督。

中文摘要：测试时语言模型在处理输入序列时会调整一个紧凑的记忆。这一视角涵盖 LaCT 中的非线性快速权重学习、DeltaNet 中的关联 delta 规则更新，以及 RWKV-7 中的广义 delta 规则状态更新。训练这些模型预测下一个词元并未明确要求某个事实在经历许多后续记忆更新后仍可被访问。我们研究针对这种测试时记忆的延迟监督：在后训练期间，只在一长段无关事件之后提出一个由模拟器支撑的问题，并将其答案与通常的下一个词元预测一起进行监督。问题在一次性分支上进行评估，因此其答案从不会进入继续的事件流。该构造区分了保持与修订：被保持的事实必须在整个延迟期间保持有效，而被修订的事实必须以其最新值来回答。我们在 LaCT-760M 和普通 DeltaNet-1.3B 上使用 TextWorld 训练轨迹以及共享的 BABILong 和 RULER 评估面板来评估该方法，并包含单独报告的 RWKV-7 比较。相对于仅事件训练，延迟问答使 LaCT 的 BABILong 提升 5.48 个百分点、DeltaNet 提升 1.32 个百分点，并使单针 RULER 分别提升 1.45 和 3.27 个百分点。RWKV-7 比较在其各自面板上报告了 4.60 和 7.00 个百分点的增益。这些结果支持将延迟语义监督作为可用测试时记忆的一种实用外层训练目标，同时仍未确定有多少收益具体来自延迟，而非来自一般问答与答案终止监督。

Original abstract: Test-time language models adapt a compact memory while processing the input sequence. This
  perspective encompasses nonlinear fast-weight learning in LaCT, associative delta-rule
  updates in DeltaNet, and generalized delta-rule state updates in RWKV-7. Training these
  models to predict the next token does not explicitly require a fact to remain accessible
  after many subsequent memory updates. We study delayed supervision for this test-time memory:
  during post-training, ask a simulator-grounded question only after a long interval of
  unrelated events, and supervise its answer alongside ordinary next-token prediction.
  Questions are evaluated on disposable branches, so their answers never enter the continuing
  event stream. The construction distinguishes retention from revision: a retained fact must
  remain valid throughout the delay, whereas a revised fact must be answered with its latest
  value. We evaluate this approach on LaCT-760M and plain DeltaNet-1.3B using TextWorld
  training trajectories and shared BABILong and RULER evaluation panels, and include a
  separately reported RWKV-7 comparison. Relative to event-only training, delayed QA improves
  BABILong by 5.48 percentage points for LaCT and 1.32 points for DeltaNet, and single-needle
  RULER by 1.45 and 3.27 points, respectively. The RWKV-7 comparison reports gains of 4.60 and
  7.00 points on its own panels. These results support delayed semantic supervision as a
  practical outer training objective for usable test-time memory, while leaving open how much
  of the benefit derives specifically from delay rather than general question-answering and
  answer-termination supervision.

</details>


### [41] [On Device Agentic Operation Caches -- Classifier-Centric NL-to-Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33141)
*Moghis Fereidouni,Anthony Arnold,Sumit Gulwani,Mark Marron,A. B. Siddique*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用设备端操作缓存把NL-to-Action从生成转为分类，降低Agent推理延迟与成本，属工程化效率优化，对代码Agent的运行时开销削减有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出通过端侧操作缓存将NL-to-Action从生成任务转为分类任务，使代理系统能在设备端处理高频动作；在Excel公式生成任务中，相比仅云端模型路由，总推理成本降低56%，缓存命中时延迟降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端托管LLM推理带来额外网络延迟、数据隐私风险和成本膨胀，尤其在企业级或前沿模型驱动的代理式AI中。

Method: 利用端侧操作缓存，将自然语言到动作问题重新表述为以分类为中心的问题，使频繁出现的动作类别完全在设备端处理。

Result: 在根据用户请求生成Excel公式的NL-to-Formula任务中，相比仅云端基于模型路由的推理，总推理成本降低56%；缓存命中时，延迟相对于响应延迟降低5倍。

Conclusion: 端侧操作缓存可降低延迟、增强隐私并降低运营成本，为代理式AI提供更高效的推理路径。

中文摘要：代理式AI正越来越多地被嵌入软件应用中，为特性和功能提供自然语言接口。在大多数情况下，这些代理由企业级（1000亿+参数）或前沿级大语言模型驱动，这些模型需要大量计算资源来运行，并依赖云端托管推理来完成将自然语言输入转化为可执行软件操作的任务。这种对云端托管推理的依赖，在LLM推理时间之外还引入了大量网络延迟，造成数据隐私问题，并且考虑到运行这些模型的成本，可能迅速推高与支持代理式功能相关的费用。本文提出了一种新颖的方法，通过端侧操作缓存，将自然语言到动作（NL-to-Action）问题从生成式问题转换为以分类为中心的表述。这些缓存使代理式系统能够完全在设备端处理频繁出现的动作类别——从而降低延迟、增强隐私并降低运营成本。我们表明，对于经典的自然语言到公式（NL-to-Formula）任务，即根据用户请求生成Excel公式，与仅云端基于模型路由的推理相比，该方法将总推理成本降低56%；并且在缓存命中时，将延迟相对于响应延迟降低5倍。

Original abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly being embedded in software applications to provide natural language interfaces to features and functionality. In most cases these agents are powered by enterprise (100+ billion parameter) or frontier class large language models that require substantial computational resources run and depend on cloud hosted inference to handle the task of transforming natural language inputs into actionable software operations. This reliance on cloud-hosted inference introduces substantial network latency on top of LLM inference times, creates data privacy concerns, and, given the costs of running these models, can rapidly escalate expenses associated with supporting agentic features.
  This paper introduces a novel means of converting the NL-to-Action problem from a generative one into a classification-centric formulation via on-device operation caches. These caches allow an agentic system to handle frequently occurring classes of actions completely on-device -- reducing latency, enhancing privacy, and lowering operational costs. We show that for a classic NL-to-Formula task, generating Excel Formula in response to user requests, this approach reduces total inference cost by 56% when compared to cloud-only model-routing based inference and, on cache hits, reduces the latency to response latency by 5x.

</details>


### [42] [RLHarness: Co-evolving Procedural Skills with Reinforcement Learning for Long-horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32326)
*Ziqiao Shang,Zian Xu,Ji-Chen Yan,Weiming Wu,Ziyi Jia,Jie Meng,Tao Huang,Shan Huang,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将技能库、执行协议与演示组织为版本化 Harness 并与 RL 共同演化，直接对应 agent harness/技能复用与长程执行。

TL;DR: 论文提出 RLHARNESS，将技能、协议、演示和任务契约组织为统一版本化 Harness，并交替演化 Harness 与策略学习，以解决多模态推理中的程序冷启动和固定技能库失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅由终端验证器引导的 RL 只提供答案正确性信号，无法指导策略如何产生答案；策略需同时发现并执行可复用推理流程，导致程序冷启动。固定技能库假设指导始终兼容演化中的策略，而只更新技能会使触发条件、执行协议和演示陈旧或不一致。

Method: RLHARNESS 将技能、选择与执行协议、少样本演示和任务契约整合为统一、带版本的 Harness，并交替进行 Harness 演化与策略学习。探索-蒸馏 Harness 构建初始 Harness 及版本对齐的已验证轨迹，用于 SFT 和 DAPO I；首个 RL 块后，后 RL 重建 Harness 从新的成功-失败 rollout 重建技能、协议和演示，DAPO II 再让策略适应重建后的程序。

Result: MetroMap/TravelMap 上 Accuracy 从 16.25%/27.50% 提升至 62.00%/50.00%；Fee-VL/Cancel-VL 上 F1 从 37.13%/45.50% 提升至 65.81%/65.51%。四个任务均在重建和 DAPO II 后才达到最佳结果。

Conclusion: 不断演化的 Harness 通过持续更新策略学习执行的外部程序来补充 RL，使外部程序与策略同步演进，从而缓解程序冷启动和固定技能库失配问题。

中文摘要：多模态推理要求模型在长决策链中保留视觉证据，同时在不同场景和规则下选择合适的流程。当学习仅由终端验证器引导时，强化学习（RL）能揭示最终答案是否正确，但不能揭示答案应如何产生。因此，策略必须在学习执行推理流程的同时发现可复用的推理流程，这造成了程序冷启动问题。技能可以将成功流程外化，减少重复探索，并提供可检查的指导。然而，固定的技能库假设这种指导始终与不断演化的策略兼容，而仅更新技能又可能使它们的触发条件、执行协议和演示变得过时或彼此不一致。我们提出 RLHARNESS，它将技能、选择与执行协议、少样本演示以及任务契约组织成一个统一、带版本的 Harness，并交替进行 Harness 演化与策略学习。一个探索-蒸馏 Harness 构建初始 Harness 以及用于 SFT 和 DAPO I 的版本对齐的已验证轨迹。在第一个 RL 模块之后，一个后 RL 重建 Harness 从新的成功-失败 rollout 中重建技能、协议和演示，而 DAPO II 使策略适应重建后的程序。RLHARNESS 在 MetroMap/TravelMap 上将 Accuracy 从 16.25%/27.50% 提升到 62.00%/50.00%，并在 Fee-VL/Cancel-VL 上将 F1 分数从 37.13%/45.50% 提升到 65.81%/65.51%。所有四个任务都仅在重建和 DAPO II 之后取得最佳结果，表明不断演化的 Harness 通过持续更新策略学习执行的外部程序来补充 RL。

Original abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires models to preserve visual evidence through long decision chains while selecting appropriate procedures across diverse scenarios and rules. When learning is guided only by terminal verifiers, reinforcement learning (RL) reveals whether a final answer is correct but not how it should be produced. The policy must therefore discover reusable reasoning procedures while learning to execute them, creating a program cold-start problem. Skills can externalize successful procedures, reduce repeated exploration, and provide inspectable guidance. However, a fixed Skill Bank assumes that this guidance remains compatible with an evolving policy, while updating Skills alone can leave their triggers, execution protocols, and demonstrations stale or mutually inconsistent. We introduce RLHARNESS, which organizes Skills, selection and execution protocols, few-shot demonstrations, and task contracts into a unified, versioned Harness and alternates Harness evolution with policy learning. An Exploration-Distillation Harness builds the initial Harness and version-aligned verified traces for SFT and DAPO I. After the first RL block, a Post-RL Reconstruction Harness rebuilds Skills, protocols, and demonstrations from fresh success-failure rollouts, and DAPO II adapts the policy to the reconstructed program. RLHARNESS improves Accuracy from 16.25%/27.50% to 62.00%/50.00% on MetroMap/TravelMap and raises F1 score from 37.13%/45.50% to 65.81%/65.51% on Fee-VL/Cancel-VL. All four tasks achieve their best results only after reconstruction and DAPO II, showing that an evolving Harness complements RL by continually updating the external program that the policy learns to execute.

</details>


### [43] [MetaBench-Harness: Unlocking End-to-End Optimization of Benchmark Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33411)
*Xuanjun Chen,Hua-Hsuan Chen,Wei-Chung Lu,Yinghao Ma,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Work in progress

Selection reason: 提出双循环MetaBench-Harness端到端优化基准生成工作流本身，属可迁移的评测/基准自动化演化方法，可用于代码基准的持续生成与难度提升。

TL;DR: 提出双环搜索框架 MetaBench-Harness，端到端优化基准生成工作流，在 CodeContests 和 AIME-2024 上生成对前沿模型更具挑战性和区分度的演化基准，以缓解基准饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准被 LLM 快速饱和，现有自动化演化方法仅扰动单个任务且依赖硬编码规则，难以持续生成高难度、高区分度基准。

Method: MetaBench-Harness 采用双环搜索：内环用 benchmark harness 每轮生成新基准；外环 meta-harness 编排层基于历史演化轨迹迭代改进和搜索 harness 实现。

Result: 在 CodeContests 和 AIME-2024 上，演化基准对前沿模型具有挑战性和区分度；轨迹与质量分析显示多维演化，连续轮次提升演化合理性、基准竞争力和评估器鲁棒性；案例显示能利用多样化难度杠杆重构问题并提升能力要求。

Conclusion: 该工作为基准饱和这一紧迫问题提供了端到端优化基准生成工作流的解决方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）的快速进展正以快于静态基准设计的速度使其饱和。尽管现有的自动化演化框架试图通过扰动单个任务来生成更难的问题，但它们仍受限于僵化、硬编码的生成规则。超越对孤立任务的演化，我们提出用 MetaBench-Harness 端到端地优化基准生成工作流本身，这是一个双环搜索框架。具体而言，内环利用基准测试 harness 在每一轮生成一个新基准，而外层的元 harness 编排层则基于历史演化轨迹迭代地改进并搜索 harness 实现。通过将 MetaBench-Harness 应用于竞赛编程 CodeContests 和奥林匹克数学 AIME-2024 数据集，我们证明演化后的基准对前沿模型具有挑战性和区分度。轨迹和质量分析验证，MetaBench-Harness 能够实现多维演化，在连续轮次中稳步提升演化合理性、基准竞争力和评估器鲁棒性。此外，案例研究揭示了其有效利用多样化的难度杠杆来重构问题并提升所需能力。最终，这项工作为基准饱和这一紧迫挑战提供了解决方案。

Original abstract: Rapid progress in Large Language Models (LLMs) is saturating static benchmarks faster than they can be designed. While existing automated evolution frameworks attempt to generate harder questions by perturbing individual tasks, they remain constrained by rigid, hard-coded generation rules. Moving beyond the evolution of isolated tasks, we propose to optimize the benchmark generation workflow itself end to end with MetaBench-Harness, a dual-loop search framework. Specifically, the inner loop utilizes a benchmark harness to generate a new benchmark in each round, while the outer meta-harness orchestration layer iteratively refines and searches over harness implementations based on historical evolution trajectories. By applying MetaBench-Harness to the competitive programming CodeContests and Olympiad mathematics AIME-2024 datasets, we demonstrate that the evolved benchmarks are challenging and discriminative for frontier models. Trajectory and quality analyses verify that MetaBench-Harness enables multi-dimensional evolution, steadily improving evolution reasonableness, benchmark competency, and evaluator robustness across successive rounds. Furthermore, case studies reveal its effective utilization of diverse difficulty levers to reframe problems and elevate required capabilities. Ultimately, this work provides a solution to the pressing challenge of benchmark saturation.

</details>


### [44] [HyperReCo: Retrieving and Connecting Evidence with Hypergraph Neural Networks for LLM Multi-hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32327)
*Zicheng Zhao,Linhao Luo,Junnan Dong,Haoran Luo,Xiaoli Li,Shirui Pan,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 超图神经检索与超路径解码提升多跳证据连接，属检索增强推理方法，可作为 agent 检索-推理组件的参考。

TL;DR: 提出HyperReCo，利用HyperGNN进行超图检索，并通过GGHD解码显式超路径来连接证据，提升多跳QA检索与下游问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图检索器多依赖预定义的结构扩展或扩散，难以捕捉识别相关证据所需的查询相关交互；同时检索到的证据之间连接隐式，迫使LLMs在推理前额外重建连接。

Method: 将每篇文档建模为抽取实体上的超边，共享实体连接超边；通过文档与实体联合监督的超图消息传递，HyperGNN学习查询相关交互以检索互补证据；引入GGHD，用梯度归因解释学习到的交互并转化为显式超路径。

Result: 在六个基准上实验，HyperReCo在三个多跳QA数据集上均取得所比较方法中最佳检索性能，并保持强下游QA性能；案例研究和进一步分析验证了解码超路径在连接检索证据上的效用。

Conclusion: HyperReCo通过查询相关的超图检索与显式超路径连接，有效支持LLMs进行多跳推理，并证明了解码超路径对连接检索证据的价值。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）已展现出强大能力，检索增强生成（RAG）通过检索分布在文档中的证据来支持复杂的多跳推理。基于图的方法利用证据之间的连接，而基于超图的检索进一步保留文档内的高阶实体关联，并通过共享实体连接文档。然而，现有的超图检索器通常依赖预定义的结构扩展或扩散，这可能遗漏识别相关证据所需的查询相关交互。它们还将检索到的证据之间的连接留为隐式，需要LLMs在推理前重建这些连接。因此，我们提出HyperReCo，一个利用超图神经网络（HyperGNN）检索并连接证据的框架。我们将每个文档表示为其抽取实体上的超边，共享实体连接这些超边。通过在文档和实体上联合监督的超图消息传递，HyperGNN学习查询相关交互以检索互补证据。我们进一步引入梯度引导超路径解码（GGHD），它使用梯度归因来解释学习到的交互，并将其转化为显式超路径，帮助LLMs组合互补事实以进行多跳推理。在六个基准上的实验表明，HyperReCo在三个多跳QA数据集上均取得了所比较方法中最佳的检索性能，并具有强大的下游QA性能。案例研究和进一步分析证明了所解码超路径在连接检索证据方面的效用。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities, with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) supporting complex multi-hop reasoning by retrieving evidence distributed across documents. Graph-based approaches exploit connections among evidence, and hypergraph-based retrieval further preserves higher-order entity associations within documents and connects documents through shared entities. However, existing hypergraph retrievers often rely on predefined structural expansion or diffusion, which may miss query-dependent interactions needed to identify relevant evidence. They also leave connections among retrieved evidence implicit, requiring LLMs to reconstruct these connections before reasoning. Therefore, we propose HyperReCo, a framework for retrieving and connecting evidence with a hypergraph neural network (HyperGNN). We represent each document as a hyperedge over its extracted entities, with shared entities connecting the hyperedges. Through hypergraph message passing with joint supervision over documents and entities, the HyperGNN learns query-dependent interactions to retrieve complementary evidence. We further introduce Gradient-Guided Hyper-Path Decoding (GGHD), which uses gradient attribution to interpret the learned interactions and translate them into explicit hyper-paths that help LLMs combine complementary facts for multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on six benchmarks show that HyperReCo achieves the best retrieval performance among the compared methods on all three multi-hop QA datasets, together with strong downstream QA performance. Case studies and further analyses demonstrate the utility of decoded hyper-paths for connecting retrieved evidence.

</details>


### [45] [SpecRead: A Benchmark for Measuring Whether Language Models Understand Hardware Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33699)
*Feilian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages. Benchmark data and code at https://github.com/EDGAhab/specread

Selection reason: 将规范理解与代码生成能力解耦的硬件规范理解benchmark，含变异注入与一致性检查，对代码/规范理解类Agent评测与错误归因有参考价值。

TL;DR: SpecRead v2.1将规范理解从生成能力中隔离，包含385个问题与82个控制项；Ministral-3B总体得分33.2%，定位矛盾较好但类别判定较弱，且基准可自动评分、第3类题可再生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件设计LLM基准仅评测生成的RTL、断言或测试平台等下游产物，模型失败时无法区分是误读规范还是理解了规范但代码生成失败，因此需要隔离并单独评测规范理解能力。

Method: 构建SpecRead v2.1，覆盖10个开源OpenTitan IP模块，设置精确检索、跨章节推理、变异规范矛盾检测和规范-RTL一致性检查四类问题，并加入干扰项与consistent-RTL对照；第4类题基于真实RTL变异，仅保留Icarus Verilog仿真可观察到行为变化的变异；使用带规范与不带规范消融、确定性自动评分，并测试结构化“规则表”提示干预。

Result: Ministral-3B总体准确率33.2%（128/385，宏平均39.0%）：检索55.2%，跨章节推理51.7%；矛盾重点类型判定加定位为48.0%（t3）与63.3%（t4），干扰项假阳性率51.2%，consistent-RTL对照100%；分层评分定位准确率78.9-81.6%，类别得分43.9-49.7%；“规则表”提示除t2持平外均降低准确率。

Conclusion: SpecRead能隔离规范理解与生成能力，并以确定性检查自动评分；当前小模型表征显示其可较好定位矛盾但细粒度类别判定仍弱；基准完全基于公开来源，且第3类题目可通过变异注入再生成。

中文摘要：现有面向硬件设计的大语言模型（LLM）基准评测的是下游产物，例如生成的RTL、断言或测试平台。当模型在这样的基准上失败时，失败原因是模糊的：它可能误读了规范，也可能理解了规范但未能写出代码。我们提出SpecRead，一个将规范理解与生成能力隔离开来的基准。SpecRead v2.1包含覆盖10个开源OpenTitan IP模块的385个问题：精确检索、跨章节推理、变异规范中的矛盾检测，以及规范-RTL一致性检查，另有82个控制项（41个干扰项，41个consistent-RTL对照）。第4类题目由真实RTL变异构建；我们只保留Icarus Verilog仿真显示会改变可观察行为的变异。带规范与不带规范的消融实验表明，这些问题需要规范摘录，而不仅仅依赖训练记忆（在t1/t2子集上不带规范的准确率为3/20），尽管对源文本的记忆仍可能有助于发现变异。作为使用小模型进行的初步表征，Ministral-3B总体得分为33.2%（128/385；宏平均39.0%）：检索为55.2%，跨章节推理为51.7%。在两个以矛盾为重点的类型上，判定加定位度量给出48.0%（t3）和63.3%（t4），在干扰项上的假阳性率为51.2%，在consistent-RTL对照上为100%。分层评分显示，模型能较好地定位矛盾（定位准确率为78.9-81.6%），但在其类别上的得分较低（43.9-49.7%）。一种结构化的“规则表”提示干预在除t2（持平）外的所有问题类型上都降低了准确率。SpecRead可通过确定性检查自动评分，在保守的主评分下，灰色地带案例计为错误。该基准的第3类题目可通过变异注入重新生成，并且完全基于公开来源构建。

Original abstract: Existing benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) in hardware design evaluate downstream artifacts such as generated RTL, assertions, or testbenches. When a model fails such a benchmark, the failure is ambiguous: it may have misread the specification, or it may have understood the specification and failed to write the code. We present SpecRead, a benchmark that isolates specification comprehension from generation ability. SpecRead v2.1 contains 385 questions over 10 open-source OpenTitan IP blocks: exact retrieval, cross-section reasoning, contradiction detection in mutated specifications, and spec-RTL consistency checking, plus 82 controls (41 distractor, 41 consistent-RTL). Type-4 items are built from real RTL mutations; we retain only mutations that Icarus Verilog simulation shows to change observable behavior. A with-spec vs. without-spec ablation suggests the questions require the excerpt, not training recall alone (without-spec accuracy 3/20 on the t1/t2 subset), though memorization of the source text may still help spot mutations. As an initial characterization with a small model, Ministral-3B scores 33.2% overall (128/385; macro average 39.0%): 55.2% on retrieval, 51.7% on cross-section reasoning. On the two contradiction-focused types, the verdict-plus-location measure gives 48.0% (t3) and 63.3% (t4), with a 51.2% false-positive rate on distractors and 100% on consistent-RTL controls. Layered scoring shows the model locates contradictions well (78.9-81.6% location accuracy) but scores lower on their category (43.9-49.7%). A structured "rule-table" prompting intervention lowers accuracy on every question type except t2 (tied). SpecRead is automatically scorable by deterministic checks, with gray-zone cases counted wrong under the conservative main scoring. The benchmark is regenerable for type-3 items via mutation injection, and built exclusively from public sources.

</details>


### [46] [Enabling Timely Guidance before Skill Retrieval: Retaining Helpful Warm Tips in Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32339)
*Feng Liang,Yupeng Li,Runhao Zeng,Francis C. M. Lau,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages and 8 figures

Selection reason: 在技能检索前把技能关键点作为 warm tips 注入上下文，面向 coding agent 任务成功率，与技能/上下文管理高度相关。

TL;DR: TipsWarm通过维护预算化的技能关键点池并逐轮选择性注入，将事件触发的LLM评估与低成本逐轮筛选分离，在编码和迭代任务执行基准上实现最高成功率并保持时间高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能机制按需加载完整技能，导致智能体在决定检索前无法获得有用指导；通用记忆维护开销大；把指导长期保留在对话上下文中又会反复暴露无关或有害建议。因此，需要一种能在每轮消息中低成本、选择性地提供可迁移技能指导的机制。

Method: 提出TipsWarm：维护一个带预算的由技能衍生的关键点池（warm tips），并将关键点选择性注入每条消息轮次的上下文；同时把事件触发的LLM评估与低成本的逐轮筛选分离，以控制维护成本并补充现有技能机制。

Result: 在三个编码和迭代任务执行基准中，与近期技能方法和通用记忆基线相比，TipsWarm取得了最高任务成功率，同时保持时间高效。

Conclusion: TipsWarm能够补充现有技能机制，使可迁移技能指导及时可用并控制维护成本，从而提升基于LLM的智能体在编码与迭代任务执行中的表现。

中文摘要：可复用技能帮助基于LLM的智能体解决复杂任务，但智能体必须在投入无效方法之前获得指导。现有技能机制通常仅暴露元数据并按需加载完整内容，这使得有用指导在智能体决定检索它之前不可用。通用记忆方法可能带来大量维护开销，而将指导保留在对话上下文中则存在让智能体反复接触无关或有害建议的风险。我们提出TipsWarm，这是一种补充现有技能机制的机制，它维护一个有预算的、由技能衍生的关键点池，即“warm tips”，用于选择性注入每条消息轮次的上下文。通过将事件触发的LLM评估与低成本的逐轮筛选分离，它使可迁移的技能指导随时可用，同时控制维护成本。在三个编码和迭代任务执行基准中，与近期的技能和通用记忆基线相比，TipsWarm在保持时间高效的同时实现了最高的任务成功率。

Original abstract: Reusable skills help LLM-based agents solve complex tasks, but the agent must receive guidance before it commits to an ineffective approach. Existing skill mechanisms often expose only metadata and load full content on demand, leaving useful guidance unavailable until the agent decides to retrieve it. General memory methods can incur substantial maintenance overhead, while keeping guidance in conversation context risks repeatedly exposing the agent to irrelevant or harmful advice. We propose TipsWarm, a mechanism that complements existing skill mechanisms by maintaining a budgeted pool of skill-derived keypoints, or \textit{warm tips}, for selective injection into the context of every message turn. By separating event-triggered LLM assessment from inexpensive per-turn screening, it makes transferable skill guidance readily available while controlling maintenance costs. In three coding and iterative task-execution benchmarks, TipsWarm achieves the highest task success rate while remaining time-efficient, compared to recent skill and general memory baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Certificate-Driven Software Porting: A Self-Improving Agentic Harness for Scientific Program Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34069)
*Piyush Jha,Aishik Ghosh,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Submitted to ML4PS 2026

Selection reason: 证书驱动的进化搜索，用失败候选生成可执行约束指导CPU→GPU科学程序移植与加速，含形式化+数值+物理多层验证，与自动程序优化和可验证反馈Agent高度契合。

TL;DR: 提出证书驱动演化搜索CDES，用失败候选生成可执行限制，通过拒绝、回溯和定向修复约束LLM演化搜索，在Geant4 CPU到GPU转换中实现显著加速与正确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型科学代码库升级重写困难，基于LLM的演化搜索虽可移植和加速遗留代码，但仅靠提示修复反馈无法防止后续候选重复相同错误。

Method: CDES将失败候选转化为由假设证书、检查器证据和经论证限制组成的可执行约束，控制逻辑通过拒绝、回溯和定向修复强制执行，同时保留兼容编辑；应用于Geant4两个粒子模拟函数的CPU到GPU翻译，并用形式化检查、数值比较、物理检查和GPU安全测试评估。

Result: 生成实现相对CPU代码取得13.78倍和23.54倍函数级加速，包含数据转换与传输；一个函数的GPU吞吐量比专家实现高14.9%，结合互补组件后达16.1%；消融实验中证书反馈使通过必需正确性检查的候选比例从55%提升至90%。

Conclusion: CDES通过可执行证书约束演化搜索，有效避免重复错误，提升大型科学代码转换的正确性与性能。

中文摘要：大型科学代码库的升级与重写历来是一项重大挑战。虽然基于大型语言模型（LLM）的演化搜索可以移植并加速遗留代码，但仅靠提示中的修复反馈并不能阻止后续候选方案重复相同的错误。我们提出了证书驱动演化搜索（CDES），它通过从失败候选中提取的可强制执行限制来扩展演化搜索，这些限制被记录为假设证书、检查器证据和经论证的限制。其控制逻辑通过拒绝、回溯和定向修复来强制执行这些限制，同时保留兼容的编辑。我们将 CDES 应用于 Geant4 工具包中两个粒子模拟函数的 CPU 到 GPU 转换，并使用一个超越单元测试的评估框架进行评估，该框架结合了形式化检查、数值比较、物理检查和 GPU 安全性测试。生成的实现相较于 CPU 代码在函数级别实现了 13.78 倍和 23.54 倍加速，其中包含数据转换和传输；对于其中一个函数，GPU 吞吐量比专家实现高出 14.9%，当结合互补组件时达到 16.1%。在针对执行设置的消融研究中，证书反馈将通过所需正确性检查的候选比例从 55% 提高到 90%。

Original abstract: The upgrade and rewriting of large scientific codebases has traditionally been a major challenge. While evolutionary search with large language models (LLMs) can port and accelerate legacy code, repair feedback in prompts alone does not prevent subsequent candidates from repeating the same errors. We introduce Certificate-Driven Evolutionary Search (CDES), which extends evolutionary search with enforceable restrictions derived from failed candidates, recorded as certificates of assumptions, checker evidence, and justified restrictions. Its control logic enforces these restrictions through rejection, backtracking, and targeted repair while preserving compatible edits. We apply CDES to CPU-to-GPU translation of two particle-simulation functions from the Geant4 toolkit, evaluated with a harness that goes beyond unit tests to combine formal checks, numerical comparisons, physics checks, and GPU safety tests. Generated implementations achieve 13.78x and 23.54x function-level speedups over CPU code, including data conversion and transfers; for one function, GPU throughput exceeds an expert implementation by 14.9%, reaching 16.1% when complementary components are combined. In an ablation over execution settings, certificate feedback increases the fraction of candidates passing required correctness checks from 55% to 90%.

</details>


### [48] [Maintaining Benchmarks Against Increasingly Capable Agents: Detection and Remediation of Unearned Passes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34262)
*Weijun Luo,Kelvin Luu,Xinyi Liu,Guangze Luo,Miguel Romero Calvo,Soham Dan,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ying Liu,Mohamed Elfeki*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过轨迹过程验证审计Agent的"不劳而获通过"与奖励黑客，定位并修复可利用面，对Agent基准完整性与评测机制维护有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出过程验证框架，审计智能体基准通过轨迹中的未挣得通过/完整性差距，区分奖励劫持与验证器弱点，定位并修复可利用面；对3810条轨迹的分析显示违规随模型代际非单调变化，修复需结合最小补丁、利用重放与新智能体评估，最终补丁后无利用到达受保护通道且通过均合法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准用于模型选择与训练，但模型可能通过未展示目标能力的方式通过任务；随着模型能力提升，原本无害的基准暴露面会变为可利用，基准有效性因此需要持续维护。

Method: 提出过程验证框架审计通过轨迹，识别有证据的奖励劫持与验证器弱点，定位可利用面；在29个模型-基准队列的3810条通过轨迹上分析；通过三个修复案例研究，采用最小补丁、利用重放和新智能体评估，并在原始标准下审计新通过。

Result: 确认违规常随模型代际增加但非单调；SWEBench Pro V1.0匹配任务上，Opus 4.7到Fable 5违规率由24%升至73%，Fable 5.1降至11%，GPT-6 Astra为0%；违规集中于少量反复出现的暴露面，尤其通过git历史访问参考解。比较为描述性，配置未归一化，最新模型通过的可利用任务也更少。最终补丁后无评估尝试到达受保护通道，补丁后通过均判为合法。

Conclusion: 基准完整性是持续维护问题：需审计通过行为、修复使能暴露面，并重新评估利用访问与合法可解性。

中文摘要：智能体基准指导模型选择与训练。然而，一个智能体可以在没有展示预期能力的情况下通过任务。此类结果构成未挣得的通过；它们在所有通过中所占比例定义了完整性差距。随着智能体改进，曾看似无害的基准表面可能变得可利用，使基准有效性成为一项持续维护问题。我们引入一个过程验证框架，审计通过轨迹，区分有证据的奖励劫持与验证器弱点，并定位可修复的可利用面。在来自29个模型-基准队列的3,810条通过轨迹中，确认的违规行为通常随模型代际增加，但并非单调增加。在SWEBench Pro V1.0上，在匹配任务中，确认违规率在Opus 4.7与Fable 5之间从24%上升到73%；较晚队列降至Fable 5.1的11%和GPT-6 Astra的0%。这些比较是描述性的：配置未归一化，且最新模型也通过更少的可利用任务。违规集中在少量反复出现的表面上，尤其是通过git历史意外访问参考解决方案。跨两个基准的三个修复案例研究表明，为何仅阻断已记录的利用是不够的：同一受保护信息仍可通过另一条路径访问。因此，我们将最小补丁与利用重放及新智能体评估相结合，在原始标准下审计新的通过。针对最终补丁的所有评估尝试均未到达受保护通道，且每个补丁后的通过都被判定为合法。基准完整性需要持续维护：审计通过行为，修复使能表面，并重新评估利用访问与合法可解性。

Original abstract: Agentic benchmarks guide model selection and training. Yet an agent can pass a task without demonstrating the intended capability. Such outcomes constitute unearned passes; their proportion among all passes defines the integrity gap. As agents improve, benchmark surfaces that once seemed harmless can become exploitable, making benchmark validity an ongoing maintenance problem. We introduce a process-verification framework that audits passing trajectories, distinguishes evidenced reward hacking from verifier weakness, and localizes exploitable surfaces for repair. Across 3,810 passing trajectories from 29 model-benchmark cohorts, confirmed violations often increase with model generation but not monotonically. On SWEBench Pro V1.0, confirmed violation rates rise from 24% to 73% between Opus 4.7 and Fable 5 on matched tasks; later cohorts fall to 11% for Fable 5.1 and 0% for GPT-6 Astra. These comparisons are descriptive: configurations were not normalized, and the latest models also pass fewer exploitable tasks. Violations concentrate around a small set of recurring surfaces, especially unintended access to reference solutions through git history. Three repair case studies across two benchmarks show why blocking a recorded exploit is insufficient: the same protected information can remain accessible through another route. Therefore, we combine minimal patches with exploit replay and fresh agent evaluation, auditing new passes under the original standard. No evaluated attempt against the final patches reached the protected channel, and every post-patch pass was judged legitimate. Benchmark integrity requires ongoing maintenance: audit passing behavior, repair the enabling surface, and re-evaluate both exploit access and legitimate solvability.

</details>


### [49] [From Trajectories to Grounded Preferences: Process Preference Synthesis via Interaction Element Graphs for Web PRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32351)
*Yangzhe Peng,Xiaoyang Wang,Yiyang Zhao,Lijun Wu,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 为 Web agent 构建过程奖励模型的高质量对比偏好数据合成，步级指导与轨迹评估思路可迁移到 agent 训练与验证。

TL;DR: 现有比较式过程奖励模型因偏好数据中GMCPs稀缺而依赖浅层捷径；SURFPRM通过交互元素图合成对比负动作，将GMCP比例从24.19%提升至74.60%，训练出的PRM在多个基准上超越基线并媲美专有LLM，还能提升GPT-4o等策略在复杂网页任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过多策略采样合成的偏好数据缺乏有据最小对比对（GMCPs），仅占24.19%，导致PRMs训练时依赖元素幻觉和动作类型不匹配等浅层捷径，无法学习真正的上下文决策语义，限制了自主网络代理的步骤级决策能力。

Method: 提出SURFPRM图引导过程偏好合成框架：将网页演示构建为持久交互元素图，作为基于环境的负动作提议机制，沿空间、时间、时空混淆轴系统生成对比负动作，从而构建SURFPRM-DATA数据集。

Result: GMCP比例从24.19%提升至74.60%；在六个开源骨干（3B-9B）上，SURFPRM-DATA训练的PRM在WEBPRMBENCH上平均优于基线模型并媲美领先专有LLM；在WEBARENA-LITE下游奖励引导轨迹搜索中，为GPT-4o和GPT-4o-mini策略分别带来+14.21%和+12.83%的提升，显著提高复杂网页任务成功率。

Conclusion: SURFPRM通过图引导合成高质量对比偏好数据，有效缓解GMCP稀缺导致的浅层捷径问题，显著提升比较式Web PRM的步骤级决策能力及下游网络代理任务表现。

中文摘要：比较式过程奖励模型（PRMs）通过评估候选动作之间基于状态的偏好，为自主网络代理提供关键的步骤级指导。然而，通过多策略采样合成的现有偏好训练数据严重缺乏有据最小对比对（GMCPs）——即竞争候选针对真实页面元素且动作类型相同的情况。在代表性基线偏好数据（即 WebArbiter）中，GMCPs 仅占 24.19%，这使得 PRMs 在训练过程中偏向依赖浅层捷径（例如元素幻觉和动作类型不匹配），而非习得真正的上下文决策语义。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 SURFPRM，一种用于比较式 Web PRMs 的图引导过程偏好合成框架。SURFPRM 将网络演示结构化为持久交互元素图，该图充当基于环境的负动作提议机制，跨空间、时间和时空混淆轴系统合成对比负动作。这将 GMCP 比例从 24.19% 提升至 74.60%，生成了精心整理的 SURFPRM-DATA 数据集。在六个开源骨干模型（3B 至 9B 参数）上，使用 SURFPRM-DATA 训练的 PRMs 在 WEBPRMBENCH 上平均优于基线训练的模型，并可媲美领先的专有 LLM。在 WEBARENA-LITE 上的下游奖励引导轨迹搜索中，SURFPRM 为 GPT-4o（+14.21%）和 GPT-4o-mini（+12.83%）策略提供步骤级指导，从而在复杂网络任务成功率上取得显著提升。

Original abstract: Comparative Process Reward Models (PRMs) provide critical step-level guidance for autonomous web agents by evaluating state-conditioned preferences between candidate actions. However, existing preference training data synthesized via multi-policy sampling suffers from a severe scarcity of Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs (GMCPs)-where competing candidates target genuine on-page elements with identical action types. In representative baselines preference data (namely, WebArbiter), GMCPs account for merely 24.19%, biasing PRMs during training to rely on shallow shortcuts (such as element hallucinations and action type mismatches) rather than acquiring genuine contextual decision semantics. To address these challenges, we propose SURFPRM, a graph-guided process preference synthesis framework for comparative Web PRMs. SURFPRM structures web demonstrations into a persistent Interaction Element Graph that acts as an environment-grounded negative action proposal mechanism, systematically synthesizing contrastive negative actions across spatial, temporal, and spatiotemporal confusion axes. This elevates the GMCP proportion from 24.19% to 74.60%, producing the curated SURFPRM-DATA dataset. Across six open-source backbones (3B to 9B parameters), PRMs trained on SURFPRM-DATA outperform baseline-trained models on average on WEBPRMBENCH and rival leading proprietary LLMs. In downstream reward-guided trajectory search on WEBARENA-LITE, SURFPRM provides step-level guidance for both GPT-4o (+14.21%) and GPT-4o-mini (+12.83%) policies, yielding substantial improvements in complex web task success rates.

</details>


### [50] [ControlScope: Workflow Revision and Reliability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34313)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 对比Agent在运行中可修订的程度（续写代码/改工具参数/替换整段工作流），揭示修复权限与实际执行结果的关系，对Agent工作流修订与可靠性研究很有用。

TL;DR: ControlScope 对比继续代码、改参数、替换工作流三种修复粒度，在多基准上检验一次性/重复审查；FULL 有时优于 KEEP，但快速抽样下未必，且审查成本、保护与捷径会影响结果，说明修复权限需与实际选择和执行绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型智能体在运行工作流中应修订多少内容，以及可用修复范围与实际选择如何影响执行结果。

Method: ControlScope 从同一公开执行状态出发，比较继续生成代码、编辑下一工具调用参数、替换未完成工作流；用嵌套权限区分可用修复与智能体所选动作；在 filesystem、ALFWorld、AppWorld 上做一次性/重复审查，并含冻结重放、轨迹中点比较、保护与仅参数捷径等分析。

Result: 20 个文件系统任务中，3 次推理抽样 FULL 为 15–16 对 KEEP 13，4 次快速抽样为 10–13 对 13；ALFWorld 87 任务/52 场景快速面板 KEEP/ARG/FULL 85/86/87，134 任务/4 场景为 134/134/127，推理队列 85/86/87 但成本高；AppWorld V1 585 实例/195 模板净差异小；冻结重放见替换被打断，轨迹中点显示后续审查完成不充分修复；五次调用保护节省 19.4% 输出并损失 1 次成功；仅参数捷径显示更广策略可能忽略更便宜成功编辑。

Conclusion: 修复访问权限需与实际选择和后续执行绑定，修复粒度收益依赖任务、抽样方式与审查成本，并非越广越好。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体应修订正在运行的工作流的多少部分？ControlScope 比较了从同一公开执行状态出发的三种做法：继续生成的代码、编辑下一个工具调用的数据参数，以及替换尚未完成的工作流。嵌套权限将可用的修复与智能体选择的动作区分开来。我们在文件系统任务、ALFWorld 和 AppWorld 上评估一次性审查与重复审查。每项任务有两个源程序，在 20 个文件系统任务上进行三次推理审查者抽样时，FULL 完成了 15–16 项任务，而 KEEP 完成 13 项；在四次快速抽样中，FULL 完成 10–13 项，而 KEEP 完成 13 项。新的学生记录确认复现了一次批量读取修复。ALFWorld 快速面板在跨 52 个场景的 87 项任务上给出 KEEP/ARG/FULL 得分 85/86/87，在跨四个场景的 134 项任务上给出 134/134/127；在 87 项任务队列上进行推理同样给出 85/86/87，但审查成本很高。一个来自 195 个场景模板、包含 585 个任务实例的 AppWorld V1 官方测试面板显示出较小的净差异。冻结重放暴露了在两次失败的文件组织运行中，智能体编写的可行替换被后续修订打断。离线源轨迹中点比较显示，后续审查完成了一次不充分的修复。五次调用保护节省了 19.4% 的已记录模型输出，并在 20 次新的源运行中损失了一次成功。仅参数捷径表明，更广泛的采样策略可能忽略两个操作集中都存在的更便宜的成功编辑。这些结果将修复访问权限与实际选择及后续执行联系起来。

Original abstract: How much of a running workflow should a language model agent revise? ControlScope compares continuing generated code, editing the next tool call's data arguments, and replacing the unfinished workflow from the same public execution state. The nested permissions separate available repairs from the actions an agent selects. We evaluate one-time and repeated reviews across filesystem tasks, ALFWorld, and AppWorld. Across two source programs per task and three reasoning-reviewer draws on 20 filesystem tasks, FULL completes 15-16 tasks versus 13 for KEEP; across four fast draws it completes 10-13 versus 13. Fresh student-record confirmation reproduces a batch-read repair. ALFWorld fast panels yield KEEP/ARG/FULL scores of 85/86/87 on 87 tasks across 52 scenes and 134/134/127 on 134 tasks across four scenes; reasoning on the 87-task cohort also yields 85/86/87 with substantial review cost. An AppWorld V1 official-test panel of 585 task instances from 195 scenario templates shows small net differences. Frozen replays expose viable agent-written replacements interrupted by later revision in two failed file-organization runs. An offline source-trajectory midpoint comparison shows later reviews completing an insufficient repair. Five-call protection saves 19.4% of logged model output and loses one success across 20 fresh source runs. An argument-only shortcut shows that the broader sampled policy can overlook a cheaper successful edit available in both operation sets. These outcomes tie repair access to actual choices and subsequent execution.

</details>


### [51] [AuthorityLens: Rethinking LLM-Based Agent Systems Through the Lens of Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32378)
*Shaojin Chen,Huihao Jing,Wun Yu Chan,Wenbin Hu,Jiaxing Li,Wu Pandy Pui Ching,Kshitij Bhatia,Xinlei He,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 76 pages, 5 figures, including appendices

Selection reason: 提出度量 agent 系统实际权限结构（权限分离、主体权限）的框架并分析 Codex 等，对 agent 可靠性与安全控制设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 AuthorityLens 框架，从系统权限、权限分离和主体权限三个维度度量智能体系统实际权限结构；在 Codex、OpenCode、Gemini CLI 的 13 种配置上发现名义配置与实际权限不一致，并能区分权限复制与权限分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作流角色、权限设置和审查机制未必反映实际行使的权限，因此需要一种统一的度量方法来刻画智能体系统的真实权限结构。

Method: 提出 AuthorityLens，从权限组合出发，基于可允许运行时状态中实现结果所需的最小参与者组合，度量系统权限、权限分离和主体权限；应用于 Codex、OpenCode、Gemini CLI 的 13 种运行配置。

Result: 名义配置无法清晰映射到实际权限：Codex Full Access 仅小幅改变系统权限但将权限集中于 Assistant；OpenCode Build/Plan 系统权限与权限分离相同；Gemini CLI 模型审查增加权限分离但不改变系统权限；主体权限可区分权限复制与权限分离。

Conclusion: AuthorityLens 提供统一框架，用于度量和比较不同智能体系统实际实现的权限结构。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体正越来越多地被部署到真实系统中，并对重要资源和决策拥有权限。这些智能体通常与拥有不同权限形式的人类和基于LLM的参与者一起运行。然而，工作流角色、权限设置和审查机制并不一定反映实践中实际实现的权限。我们提出 AuthorityLens，一个用于度量系统权限结构的框架。从权限组合出发，我们从三个维度评估系统：系统被授权做什么（系统权限）、行使该权限需要多少联合参与（权限分离），以及每个参与者持有多少权限（主体权限）。我们从可允许运行时状态下足以实现每种结果的最小参与者组合中推导出这些度量。我们将 AuthorityLens 应用于 Codex、OpenCode 和 Gemini CLI，在 13 种运行配置下、针对一组共同的智能体操作组合进行评估。我们发现，名义配置并不清晰地映射到实际实现的权限。在 Codex 中，Full Access 仅略微改变系统权限，却将权限大幅集中于正在执行的 Assistant。OpenCode 的 Build 和 Plan 配置尽管工作流和根级权限不同，却具有相同的系统权限和权限分离。在 Gemini CLI 中，基于模型的审查增加了权限分离，但未改变系统权限。主体权限进一步区分权限复制与权限分离：衍生或委派的智能体可以成为同一权限的替代持有者，而不增加所需的联合参与。总之，这些结果表明，AuthorityLens 提供了一个统一的框架，用于度量和比较跨智能体系统实际实现的权限结构。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed with authority over consequential resources and decisions in real systems. These agents often operate alongside human and LLM-based participants who hold different forms of authority. Yet workflow roles, permission settings, and review mechanisms do not necessarily reflect the authority realized in practice. We introduce AuthorityLens, a framework for measuring a system's authority structure. Starting from an authority portfolio, we evaluate a system along three dimensions: what the system is authorized to do (System Authority), how much joint participation is required to exercise that authority (Authority Separation), and how much authority each participant holds (Principal Authority). We derive these measurements from the minimal combinations of participants sufficient to realize each outcome across admissible runtime states. We apply AuthorityLens to Codex, OpenCode, and Gemini CLI across 13 operating configurations over a common portfolio of agent operations. We find that nominal configurations do not map cleanly onto realized authority. In Codex, Full Access changes System Authority only marginally while substantially concentrating authority in the executing Assistant. OpenCode's Build and Plan configurations have the same System Authority and Authority Separation despite different workflows and root-level permissions. In Gemini CLI, model-based review increases Authority Separation without changing System Authority. Principal Authority further distinguishes authority replication from authority separation: spawned or delegated agents can become alternative holders of the same authority without increasing the required joint participation. Together, these results demonstrate that AuthorityLens provides a unified framework for measuring and comparing realized authority structures across agent systems.

</details>


### [52] [After the Fix: Transfer of Corrected Agent Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34603)
*Yanfei Zhang,Xu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究修复后的失败经验作为记忆迁移到新任务是否有效，区分"修复经验"与"复用经验"的价值，对Agent记忆/反思机制设计有可迁移结论。

TL;DR: 修复经验并不自动成为更好的下一任务记忆；ThinkingBox有修正增益但部分源于未修正基线更差，APEX无同等总体收益，修复价值与复用价值不同，记忆更新需旧版本与全新起点两个参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验修复失败回合是否提升其作为下一任务记忆的价值，并区分修复经验与复用经验。

Method: 将同一失败源在修复前后转移至固定目标，并与独立执行比较；3,300次运行覆盖100个ThinkingBox配对和100个APEX配对，在有/无源状态继承下共11种条件。

Result: ThinkingBox的Full/Skill/Hybrid修正增益为44/29/32个百分点，修正后性能比独立执行高25/22/18点；但Full对Skill的15点修正差距中12点来自未修正性能更差；Full的46次向上转移中22次恢复基线成功；APEX未建立可比总体修正收益；文本APEX接受执行52%对摘要40%，无稳健全局/组级优势；较小交接减少输入但增加调用。

Conclusion: 修复经验的价值不同于复用经验；记忆更新需要前一版本参照和全新起点参照。

中文摘要：修复一个回合是否会让其经验成为下一任务更好的记忆？我们将同一个失败源在被接受的修复前后转移到固定目标，并与独立执行并列比较。我们的3,300次运行涵盖100个ThinkingBox配对以及同样的100个APEX配对，在有和没有源状态继承的条件下，共十一种条件。ThinkingBox的Full/Skill/Hybrid修正增益分别为44/29/32个百分点，修正后性能分别比独立执行高25/22/18个点；推理在任务族层面减弱。然而，Full相对Skill大15个百分点的修正差距中，有12个点来自更差的未修正性能，而非更好的修正后记忆。此外，Full的46次向上转移中有22次恢复了观察到的基线成功。两种APEX机制都没有建立起可比的总体修正收益。动作证据将工作流收益与可复用义务联系起来，并将约定冲突与源局部选择联系起来。文本APEX的接受执行达到52%，而其摘要为40%，但不具备稳健的全局/组级优越性，也没有相对于独立执行的已确立优势。更小的交接减少输入，但增加调用。因此，修复经验的价值不同于复用经验的价值：记忆更新既需要前一版本参照，也需要全新开始参照。

Original abstract: Does repairing an episode make its experience a better memory for the next task? We transfer the same failed source before and after accepted repair to a fixed target, alongside independent execution. Our 3,300 runs cover 100 ThinkingBox pairs and the same 100 APEX pairs with and without source-state inheritance, under eleven conditions. ThinkingBox's Full/Skill/Hybrid correction gains are 44/29/32 percentage points, with corrected performance 25/22/18 points above independence; inference weakens at the task-family level. Yet 12 of Full's 15-point larger correction gap over Skill come from worse uncorrected performance, not better corrected memory. Moreover, 22 of Full's 46 upward transitions restore observed baseline success. Neither APEX regime establishes comparable aggregate correction benefits. Action evidence connects workflow gains with reusable obligations and convention conflicts with source-local choices. Text APEX's accepted execution reaches 52% versus its summary's 40%, without robust global/group-level superiority or an estab- lished advantage over independence. Smaller handoffs reduce input but increase calls. The value of repairing experience is therefore distinct from the value of reusing it: memory updates require both a previous-version reference and a fresh-start reference.

</details>


### [53] [Reward Hacking and Agent Containment Failure: A Monte Carlo Study Based on the 2026 Hugging Face Incident](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32390)
*Murat Ozer,Bulent Erenay,Ibrahim Berber*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, research paper, and two figures

Selection reason: 用蒙特卡洛建模奖励黑客到逃逸的 agent 遏制失效链条，为 agent 安全边界与评测隔离提供可迁移结论。

TL;DR: 论文提出将奖励黑客、遏制逃逸、可用访问、持久化与检测失败联系起来的五阶段概率风险模型，并用蒙特卡洛模拟比较四种控制配置。结果显示，分层控制显著优于单独网络隔离或监控；风险主要受智能体能力及监控、授权、凭据控制薄弱环节影响。作者主张将网络能力智能体评估按敌对安全区管理，隔离间接出口、共享基础设施、凭据和评估工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奖励黑客从模型行为演变为外部网络安全事件的路径，并比较不同控制措施的有效性，为智能体评估环境的安全设计提供依据。

Method: 构建五阶段概率风险模型；在四种控制配置下各进行100,000次蒙特卡洛模拟；对每个系数进行300次独立±25%扰动检验排序；开展敏感性分析。

Result: 分层控制比单独网络隔离或监控更能降低模拟外部事件概率，且该排序在±25%系数扰动下稳健；智能体能力以及监控、授权、凭据控制薄弱环节影响最大。

Conclusion: 应将具有网络能力的智能体评估视为敌对安全区，使间接出口、共享基础设施、凭据和评估工件处于智能体有效权限之外。

中文摘要：2026年7月对Hugging Face生产基础设施的入侵表明，当能力较强的智能体遇到薄弱的遏制边界时，奖励黑客行为如何可能演变为外部网络安全事件。本研究开发了一个概率风险模型，将五个阶段联系起来：奖励黑客行为、遏制逃逸、可用访问权限、持久化和检测失败。蒙特卡洛模拟在四种控制配置下各评估了100,000次运行。输入分布表示显式不确定性，并用于比较分析，而非现实世界频率预测。在上述假设下，分层控制比单独使用网络隔离或监控更能大幅降低模拟的外部事件概率；这一排序在300次抽取中对模型中每个系数进行独立的±25%扰动时依然成立。敏感性分析表明，智能体能力以及监控、授权和凭据控制中的薄弱环节对建模风险的影响最大。人类时间贴现和指标博弈为短时间范围优化提供了行为类比，但本研究并不推断AI智能体会体验到满足感或人类动机。结果支持将具有网络能力的智能体评估视为敌对安全区；在其中，间接出口、共享基础设施、凭据和评估工件必须保持在智能体的有效权限之外。

Original abstract: The July 2026 intrusion into Hugging Face production infrastructure showed how reward hacking can become an external cybersecurity incident when a capable agent encounters weak containment boundaries. This study develops a probabilistic risk model linking five stages: reward hacking, containment escape, usable access, persistence, and failure of detection. A Monte Carlo simulation evaluates 100,000 runs under each of four control configurations. Input distributions represent explicit uncertainty and are used for comparative analysis rather than real-world frequency prediction. Under the stated assumptions, layered controls reduce simulated external-incident probability substantially more than network isolation or monitoring used alone, an ordering that holds under independent plus/minus 25% perturbation of every coefficient in the model across 300 draws. Sensitivity analysis shows that agent capability and weaknesses in monitoring, authorization, and credential control exert the greatest influence on modeled risk. Human temporal discounting and metric gaming provide a behavioral analogy for short-horizon optimization, but the study does not infer that AI agents experience gratification or human motivation. The results support treating cyber-capable agent evaluations as hostile security zones in which indirect egress, shared infrastructure, credentials, and evaluation artifacts must remain outside the agent's effective authority.

</details>


### [54] [Fewer Assumptions by Design: A Reusable Skill for LLM-Assisted Verus Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34886)
*Andrada-Livia Antoneac,Dorel Lucanu,Dragoş Teodor Gavriluţ*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: In Proceedings FROM 2026, arXiv:2609.30324

Journal reference: EPTCS 452, 2026, pp. 104-121

DOI: [10.4204/EPTCS.452.8](https://doi.org/10.4204/EPTCS.452.8)

Selection reason: 为LLM辅助Verus验证设计可复用技能（领域知识+任务分解），降低可信基并生成强规范，属代码验证Agent的技能/工作流构建方法。

TL;DR: LLM智能体借助针对双向链表的验证技能，可在Verus中生成强且低可信基的规约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助Verus验证可减轻Rust实现验证负担，但自引用结构（如双向链表）形式化困难，且依赖公理引理、assume等未证明假设会削弱规约；因此需研究能否合成强DLL规约并最小化可信基。

Method: 分析采用三种途径：手动验证、属性特定验证，以及为DLL及其特定属性定义的验证技能；该技能封装领域知识与任务分解策略。

Result: 配备精心设计的验证技能的LLM智能体能够为Verus中的双向链表生成强且低可信基的规约。

Conclusion: 通过引入编码领域知识和任务分解的验证技能，可提升LLM智能体对双向链表规约合成的强度，并减少对未证明假设的依赖。

中文摘要：LLM辅助的Verus验证是一种较不繁琐地验证Rust实现的方法，但当它与自引用结构（例如双向链表（DLL）——以难以形式化以用于验证而著称）结合时，它会变成一项显著更严苛的验证任务。此外，当验证依赖于未经证明或被否定的假设（例如公理引理和assume语句）时，可能会出现规约薄弱性。我们研究LLM智能体能否合成强的DLL规约，同时最小化这些可信基。分析遵循三种不同的方法：手动验证、属性特定验证，以及针对DLL特定情况以及这类数据结构的某些属性所定义的技能。该技能编码了领域知识和任务分解策略。我们表明，配备精心设计的验证技能的LLM智能体能够在Verus中为DLL生成强的、低可信基的规约。

Original abstract: LLM-assisted Verus verification is a less tedious method to verify Rust implementations, but paired with self-referential structures, e.g., Doubly Linked Lists (DLLs)—notoriously difficult to formalise for verification—it becomes a substantially more demanding verification task.    Moreover, a specification weakness can arise when verification relies on unproven or invalidated assumptions, such as axiomatic lemmas and assume statements. We investigate whether LLM agents can synthesize strong DLL specifications while minimizing these trusted base. The analysis follows three different approaches: manual verification, property-specific verification, and a defined skill for the specific case of DLLs and certain properties of this type of data structure. The skill encodes domain knowledge and a task-decomposition strategy. We show that an LLM agent equipped with a carefully designed verification skill can generate strong, low-trust specifications for DLLs in Verus.

</details>


### [55] [SCLATE: a Substrate for Continual-Learning Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32391)
*Youngmok Jung,Sirajul Salekin,Henry Tran,Javier Movellan,Zhao Huang,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: submitted for ICLR2027, Apple Inc

Selection reason: 面向持续学习 agent 的执行基座，统一调度多会话/记忆整合事件并支持原样 harness 的训练与评测，与长程 agent 研究直接相关。

TL;DR: SCLATE 是一个统一调度基准与智能体事件、支持混合模拟时钟和 rollout 记录的执行基座；移植 7 个基准、对比 10 种 harness/记忆配置和 10 个模型，并后训练 Qwen3.5-4B 提升工具使用、SWE-bench Verified 与 MetaClaw 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习智能体需在长时多会话中评估与训练，但现有基准和训练框架只调度自身事件，迫使每个基准-智能体组合自建调度循环。

Method: 提出 SCLATE 执行基座：通过适配器将基准与未修改智能体的事件接入同一开放事件调度器；用混合模拟时钟在共享时间线上运行事件，工作时实时流动、空闲时跳过；并作为 rollout 引擎，经容器内代理记录每次模型调用的 token 与 log 概率。

Result: 将 7 个基准移植至 SCLATE，在 10 个模型上对比 10 种未修改 harness/记忆配置：附加记忆系统并不稳定优于 harness 原生记忆，且不同模型对相同 harness/记忆的使用差异很大。后训练 Qwen3.5-4B 后，文件读取行数减少 6.8 倍，SWE-bench Verified 通过率提高 16.7 个百分点，记忆记录更丰富，留出 MetaClaw 准确率最高提升 11.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: SCLATE 可作为持续学习智能体的统一评估与训练执行基座，并支持通过未修改 harness 与记忆系统进行后训练，显著改善模型对工具和记忆的使用。

中文摘要：持续学习智能体是由模型、harness 和记忆组成的系统，在长时多会话时间跨度上运行。评估和训练它们需要将任务与智能体侧事件交错，例如会话停止与启动、cron 定时任务和记忆巩固。然而，现有基准和训练框架只调度基准自身的事件，导致每个基准与智能体的组合都需要构建自定义调度循环。我们提出 SCLATE，一个执行基座，其中基准和未经修改的智能体分别通过适配器将其事件添加到一个开放事件调度器中。一种混合模拟时钟在共享时间线上运行这些事件，在智能体工作时以实时方式流动并跳过空闲间隙，从而将长达一个月的场景压缩到数小时。SCLATE 还可作为 rollout 引擎，在不对任何智能体的 harness 和记忆做修改的情况下运行它们，并通过容器内代理记录每次模型调用的 token 和 log 概率。我们将七个基准移植到 SCLATE，并在十个模型上对十种未经修改的 harness 和记忆配置进行正面比较。比较表明，添加的记忆系统并不能可靠地胜过 harness 的原生记忆，并且不同模型在使用相同 harness 和记忆的方式上差异很大。然后，我们通过未经修改的 harness 和记忆系统对 Qwen3.5-4B 进行后训练。该模型学会同时使用二者，读取的文件行数减少 6.8 倍，SWE-bench Verified 通过率提高 16.7 个百分点，并写出更丰富的记忆记录，同时其留出的 MetaClaw 准确率提升高达 11.8 个百分点。

Original abstract: Continual-learning agents are systems of models, harnesses, and memory operating over long multi-session horizons. Evaluating and training them requires interleaving tasks with agent-side events such as session stop and start, crons, and memory consolidation. Yet existing benchmarks and training frameworks schedule only the benchmark's own events, leaving each benchmark and agent pair to build a custom scheduling loop. We present SCLATE, an execution substrate where benchmarks and unmodified agents each add their events to one open event scheduler through an adapter. A hybrid simulated clock runs these events on a shared timeline, flowing in real time while the agent works and skipping idle gaps, which compresses a month-long scenario into hours. SCLATE also serves as a rollout engine that runs any agent's harness and memory unmodified, recording the tokens and log probabilities of every model call through an in-container proxy. We port seven benchmarks to SCLATE and compare ten unmodified harness and memory configurations head to head on ten models. The comparison shows that an added memory system does not reliably beat the harness's native memory and that models differ widely in how they use the same harness and memory. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through unmodified harnesses and memory systems. The model learns to use both, reading 6.8x fewer file lines with a 16.7-point higher SWE-bench Verified pass rate, and writing richer memory records, while its held-out MetaClaw accuracy rises by up to 11.8 points.

</details>


### [56] [When Valid Tool Calls Change Meaning: Formation-Consistent Dispatch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35088)
*Geonwoo Kim,Brent ByungHoon Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures, and 9 tables

Selection reason: 发现schema合法的工具调用在分发时含义漂移的安全问题，提出形成一致性分发与批准策略，对Agent工具调用可靠性与安全有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 该论文揭示工具调用中描述符-处理器关系缺失导致模式纪元漂移，提出FCD将实现分析与执行权限绑定，并通过来源绑定摘要、安全契约验证、原子准入与最终跳栅栏防止同一有效调用在部署、重连或延迟批准中获得不同安全效果；实验复现该故障并显示FCD在保持调用私有性的同时阻止公开化遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分派省略描述符-处理器关系，导致未改变且模式有效的调用在滚动发布、重连或延迟批准时可改变安全效果，因此需要连接实现分析与执行权限以消除这种漂移。

Method: 提出形成一致性分派（FCD）；经审查配置从官方源生成来源绑定的范围内效果过近似；验证器在封闭目标批准策略下将调用应用于摘要并按安全契约捕获后继；原子准入和最终跳栅栏维持决策；精确源优先，后继在源退役后生效；用32个官方发布、冻结16发布扩展、预注册原版比较和生命周期实验进行评估。

Result: 原版发布和部署变更复现故障；四配置覆盖32个官方发布，29个无需发布特定变更，3个升级；冻结16发布扩展与独立源预言机匹配；预注册原版比较中，FCD完成3个效果保持私有的待处理调用并阻止3个遗漏公开的调用；精确固定和全发布拒绝停止全部6个调用，全发布批准完成6个但产生3个公开效果；生命周期实验证明形成捕获证书可跨源退役并在不扩展权限下治理新形成。

Conclusion: FCD通过将实现分析与执行权限绑定，可防止模式纪元漂移，保持安全效果稳定，并在不扩大待处理调用权限的前提下支持源退役后的安全证书迁移。

中文摘要：支持工具的智能体从模型可见接口形成调用，而宿主机稍后选择其实现。标准分派省略了描述符-处理器关系。因此，一个未改变且模式有效的调用可以在滚动发布、重连或延迟批准期间获得不同的安全效果。我们将这种故障称为模式纪元漂移（schema-epoch drift）。我们提出形成一致性分派（FCD），它将实现分析与执行权限连接起来。经审查的配置从官方源产生对声明的范围内效果的来源绑定过近似。在封闭目标批准策略下，验证器将每个形成的调用应用于摘要，并仅当其效果符合调用的安全契约时才捕获后继。原子准入和最终跳栅栏将该决定保持到效果处。精确源保持优先级，捕获的后继只有在源退役后才有资格。原版发布和部署变更复现了该故障。四个配置覆盖了32个官方发布：29个无需特定发布变更，3个升级。一个冻结的16发布扩展与单独的源预言机匹配。在预注册的原版比较中，FCD完成了所有三个效果保持私有的待处理调用，并阻止了所有三个其遗漏变为公开的调用。精确固定和全发布拒绝停止了全部六个调用，而全发布批准完成了全部六个但产生了三个公开效果。一个单独的生命周期实验携带了一个形成捕获的证书跨源退役。后来安装的同一安全证书治理了新的形成，而没有扩展待处理调用的权限。

Original abstract: Tool-enabled agents form calls from model-visible interfaces, while hosts later select their implementation. Standard dispatch omits the descriptor-handler relation. An unchanged and schema-valid call can therefore acquire a different security effect during rollout, reconnect, or delayed approval. We call this failure schema-epoch drift. We present formation-consistent dispatch (FCD), which connects implementation analysis to execution authority.
