Reviewed profiles produce provenance-bound over-approximations of declared in-scope effects from official source. Under a closed-target approval policy, a verifier applies each formed call to a summary and captures a successor only when its effects fit the call's security contract. Atomic admission and a final-hop fence preserve this decision to the effect. The exact source retains priority, and the captured successor becomes eligible only after source retirement. Stock releases and deployment changes reproduced the failure. Four profiles covered 32 official releases: 29 required no release-specific change and three escalated. A frozen 16-release expansion matched a separate source oracle. In a preregistered stock comparison, FCD completed all three pending calls whose effect remained private and blocked all three whose omission became public. Exact pinning and release-wide denial stopped all six calls, while release-wide approval completed all six but produced three public effects. A separate lifecycle experiment carried a formation-captured certificate across source retirement. The same safe certificate installed later governed new formations without expanding the pending call's authority.

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Scripted Search: Sample-Efficient Reward Discovery via Agentic Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32394)
*Minghao Li,Rui Tan,Ruihang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM agent 依据评测历史与自身信念动态构建黑盒搜索策略，体现 agent 化迭代优化与工具反馈复用，可迁移到自动优化 agent。

TL;DR: 提出ARBO框架，让LLM智能体基于持久评估历史在运行时构建奖励搜索策略，从而提升复杂控制任务中奖励搜索的样本效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 底层RL控制中的稠密奖励设计仍很困难；现有LLM奖励搜索方法评估每个候选都需完整RL训练，导致样本效率成为核心瓶颈。

Method: 将评估历史作为LLM智能体的持久工作区，包含评估预言机观测（候选分数、分项训练曲线、错误回溯）和智能体信念（诊断、预期下一步）；智能体用工具查询历史并批量生成下一批奖励候选，而非从固定提示一次性生成。

Result: 在四个控制领域、共享评估预算下，ARBO相较基线均值将操作成功率提升29.9%、电网得分提升192.8%；消融实验分析各组件贡献及对骨干选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: ARBO通过让LLM智能体在运行时依据评估历史动态构建搜索策略，提高了奖励搜索的样本效率，并在复杂控制任务上显著优于基线。

中文摘要：为底层强化学习（RL）控制设计稠密奖励函数仍然很困难。近期工作使用大语言模型（LLM），在脚本化搜索算法中利用策略训练反馈来迭代生成并精炼奖励函数。然而，评估每个候选方案都需要一次完整的RL训练运行，这使得样本效率成为复杂控制任务上奖励搜索的核心挑战。为应对这一局限，我们提出智能体式奖励黑盒优化（ARBO）框架，其中LLM智能体在运行时根据作为其持久工作区维护的评估历史来构建搜索策略。评估历史包含两个组成部分：由评估预言机维护的观测，包括候选分数、分项训练曲线和错误回溯；以及由智能体维护的信念，记录诊断和预期下一步。智能体使用工具查询二者，并生成下一批奖励候选，而不是根据固定提示一次性生成它们。在四个控制领域中，在共享评估预算下，ARBO相较于基线均值在操作成功率上取得29.9%的提升，在电网得分上取得192.8%的提升。消融实验考察了每个组件的贡献以及对骨干选择的敏感性。

Original abstract: Designing dense reward functions for low-level reinforcement learning (RL) control remains difficult. Recent work uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate and refine reward functions using policy-training feedback within scripted search algorithms. However, evaluating each candidate requires a full RL training run, making sample efficiency a central challenge for reward search on complex control tasks. To address this limitation, we propose an Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization (ARBO) framework, in which an LLM agent builds the search strategy at run time from an evaluation history maintained as its persistent workspace. The evaluation history comprises two components: observations maintained by the evaluation oracle, including candidate scores, per-term training curves, and error tracebacks; and an agent-maintained belief that records diagnoses and intended next steps. The agent queries both with tools and generates the next batch of reward candidates, rather than generating them in a single pass from a fixed prompt. Across four control domains, ARBO achieves gains of 29.9% in manipulation success rate and 192.8% in power-grid score over baseline means under a shared evaluation budget. Ablations examine each component's contribution and sensitivity to backbone choice.

</details>


### [58] [From Migration to Calibration: Preserving Agent Capabilities across Models, Jurisdictions, and Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35149)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages, 7 figures. Methodological proposal: three-layer agent calibration framework; no empirical results reported

Selection reason: 提出Agent跨模型/司法辖区/规模迁移时的三层校准框架与held-out评测建议，目前为方法学提案，但思路可迁移到Agent能力保持与评测设计。

TL;DR: 面向部署条件变化，提出包含信息保留、harness适应与用户接受三层交互的智能体校准框架，并给出目标原生记录、留出评估、因子测试和分组报告等验证方案；当前仅为方法论提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署条件变化（模型替换、跨境、跨市场/来源扩展）时，仅接口兼容无法保证能力保持与目标契约满足，需要系统化的校准与验证。

Method: 将智能体校准形式化为受约束行为适应：信息校准保留已验证且适用的源内容；harness校准在语义检查点对齐并通过迭代、工具替换、局部重规划修复产物；用户校准执行接收方输出契约。区分可训练策略与冻结骨干/控制器优化、证据验证与相对判断/DPO/GRPO优化，并提出留出评估、目标原生执行记录、任务有效性与近似平局排序审计、匹配优化控制、源证据与检查点修复因子测试及分组级报告。

Result: 提出方法论框架与评估协议，未报告实现或实证结果；以全球电子商务示例说明共享标准与站点/市场特定适配器及验证共存。

Conclusion: 智能体校准需覆盖信息、harness和用户三层，并以目标原生评估防止总体收益掩盖局部失败；该工作为方法论提案，实现和实证验证留待未来。

中文摘要：智能体在部署条件变化时需要校准：替换驱动模型，包括从基础模型到后训练模型的转换；跨越司法管辖区；或跨异构市场和来源进行扩展。仅接口兼容并不能确立能力保持或目标契约满足。我们将智能体校准形式化为跨三个相互作用层的受约束行为适应：信息保留、harness适应和用户接受；这些层适用于每个场景，而不是与三种场景一一对应。基本目标是在满足目标要求的同时，在预先指定的能力度量上不出现退化；总体改进是更强的目标。信息校准保留经独立验证且仍适用于目标任务的源内容。Harness校准在语义检查点对齐可观察产物，并在明确预算内通过迭代、工具替换或局部重规划来修复它们。用户校准强制执行接收方特定的输出契约：模板、模式和章节级偏好。一个全球电子商务示例展示了共享标准如何与站点和市场特定的适配器及验证共存。我们区分可训练策略与冻结骨干配置或控制器优化，并区分证据验证与相对判断以及DPO/GRPO优化。近期关于harness迁移和评判者有效性的研究推动了目标原生执行记录、对任务有效性和近似平局排序的单独审计，以及匹配的目标原生优化控制。我们提出针对模型更换、跨境适应和规模化的留出评估，包括对源证据与检查点修复的因子测试，以及分组级报告，以防止总体收益掩盖局部失败。这是一个方法论提案；实现和实证验证仍属未来工作。

Original abstract: Agents need calibration when deployment conditions change: replacing a driving model, including a foundation-to-post-trained transition; crossing jurisdictions; or scaling across heterogeneous markets and sources. Interface compatibility alone does not establish capability retention or target-contract satisfaction. We formulate agent calibration as constrained behavioral adaptation across three interacting layers: information preservation, harness adaptation, and user acceptance; the layers apply to every scenario, not one-to-one to the three. The basic objective is non-degradation on prespecified capability measures while satisfying target requirements; aggregate improvement is stronger. Information calibration preserves independently validated source content still applicable to the target task. Harness calibration aligns observable artifacts at semantic checkpoints and repairs them through iteration, tool substitution, or local replanning within explicit budgets. User calibration enforces recipient-specific output contracts: templates, schemas, and section-level preferences. A global e-commerce example shows how shared standards coexist with site- and market-specific adapters and validation. We distinguish trainable policies from frozen-backbone configuration or controller optimization, and evidence verification from relative judgment and DPO/GRPO optimization. Recent harness-transfer and judge-validity studies motivate target-native execution records, separate audits of task validity and near-tie ranking, and matched target-native optimization controls. We propose held-out evaluations for model changes, cross-border adaptation, and scale, including a factorial test of source evidence and checkpoint repair and group-level reporting to prevent aggregate gains from masking local failures. This is a methodological proposal; implementation and empirical validation remain future work.

</details>


### [59] [Memory as a cache: Exact context reuse and deletion by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32395)
*Shengyao Wang,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以块级可组合、可精确删除的上下文记忆架构替代纠缠 KV cache，对长上下文 agent 的记忆复用与编辑有潜在启发。

TL;DR: SMem 通过块局部编码器与交叉注意力读取器，使上下文表示天然成为可精确组合、删除且与编辑路径无关的缓存，并在长上下文检索、解码效率与删除成本上取得优势，同时保持困惑度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的 KV 缓存与整个前缀纠缠，导致编码后的文本无法在不同前缀下复用，也无法低成本删除或编辑，精确缓存复用仅限于共享前缀。

Method: SMem 使用块局部编码器独立地将每个块映射为记忆行，并由读取器通过交叉注意力在记忆行并集上条件生成；记忆在固定块索引处精确组合，删除 b-token 块为 O(b) 更新，且状态与编辑路径无关；还可与 RoPE 组合。

Result: 在 4 倍训练上下文下，SMem 在 31/63 块距离的植入针检索中精确匹配 0.14-0.28，而基线至多 0.02；完全缓存上下文仅需计算一个块，耗时 3.1-6.2 ms；批量解码少存 34-38% KV 行，带宽受限时快 1.4-1.7 倍；删除在 512/4096 块时分别快 8.5/452 倍；在 160M-1.5B、FineWeb-Edu 上困惑度差距为 -4.7% 到 +2.8%；与 RoPE 组合后在 160M/410M 匹配或领先，并缩小 29-59% 差距。

Conclusion: 消除前缀纠缠可在保持困惑度与参数匹配 Transformer 相当的同时，使 KV 缓存具备精确可组合性与可编辑性，并显著改善长上下文检索、解码存储与删除成本。

中文摘要：Transformer 的 KV 缓存将每个 token 的表示与其整个前缀纠缠在一起：一段文本一旦被编码，就不能在不同前缀下复用，也不能在不重算其后所有内容的情况下删除，因此精确缓存复用仅限于共享前缀。我们提出 SMem，一种其上下文表示按构造即为缓存的架构。块局部编码器独立于其他块，将每个块映射为记忆行，而读取器通过交叉注意力将生成条件建立在这些记忆行的并集上。对于每种参数设置，记忆在固定块索引处精确组合，删除一个块对于 b-token 块是精确的 O(b) 更新，且记忆状态与编辑路径无关。在训练上下文的 4 倍长度下，在共享配方下，SMem 检索到超出任何训练长度窗口的植入针（在距离为 31 和 63 个块时精确匹配为 0.14-0.28），而学习位置、RoPE 和 Block-Attention 风格 Transformer 的得分均至多 0.02。完全缓存的上下文通过仅计算一个块即可服务，耗时近乎恒定为 3.1-6.2 ms，而冷预填充随上下文增长；批量解码存储的 KV 行少 34-38%，在带宽受限时运行快 1.4-1.7 倍；删除在 512 个块时比后缀重计算快 8.5 倍，在 4096 个块时快 452 倍（为训练长度的 32-256 倍，探测的是成本模型而非服务场景）。代价是困惑度差距为 -4.7% 到 +2.8%（负值有利于 SMem），对比对象是使用相同位置方案且参数量匹配的 Transformer，在 FineWeb-Edu 上、参数规模 160M-1.5B、两种配方和学习率搜索下。SMem 也可与 RoPE 组合：在 160M 和 410M 规模下，组合模型匹配或领先于匹配的 Transformer，并缩小 SMem 与 RoPE Transformer 差距的 29-59%。因此，消除前缀纠缠可在保持困惑度相当的同时，使缓存精确可组合和可编辑。

Original abstract: The KV cache of a transformer entangles every token's representation with its entire prefix: a passage encoded once cannot be reused under a different prefix or removed without recomputing everything after it, so exact cache reuse is limited to shared prefixes. We present SMem, an architecture whose context representation is a cache by construction. A block-local encoder maps each block to memory rows independently of other blocks, and a reader conditions generation on their union through cross-attention. For every parameter setting, memory composes exactly at fixed block indices, deleting a block is an exact $O(b)$ update for $b$-token blocks, and the memory state is independent of the edit path. At $4\times$ the training context, under the shared recipe, SMem retrieves planted needles beyond any trained-length window (exact match 0.14-0.28 at distances of 31 and 63 blocks), where learned-position, RoPE, and Block-Attention-style transformers all score at most 0.02. A fully cached context is served by computing one block alone at a near-constant 3.1-6.2 ms, whereas cold prefill grows with context; batched decode stores 34-38% fewer KV rows and runs 1.4-1.7$\times$ faster when bandwidth-bound; and deletion beats suffix recomputation by 8.5$\times$ at 512 blocks and 452$\times$ at 4096 blocks (32-256$\times$ the trained length, probing the cost model rather than a served regime). The cost is a perplexity gap of -4.7% to +2.8% (negative favors SMem) against a parameter-matched transformer with the same positional scheme, at 160M-1.5B on FineWeb-Edu across two recipes and a learning-rate search. SMem also composes with RoPE: at 160M and 410M the composite matches or leads the matched transformer and closes 29-59% of SMem's gap to a RoPE transformer. Dropping prefix entanglement thus keeps perplexity comparable while making the cache exactly composable and editable.

</details>


### [60] [Opening LLM Judges: Recovering Preference Signals Beyond the Final Verdict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32407)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 揭示 LLM judge 的偏好信号可从内部激活恢复，可用于 agent 结果验证与评测可靠性诊断。

TL;DR: 在64个开放权重评判器和14个数据集上，研究发现LLM评判器常因位置、长度等表面特征出错，但正确答案信息往往存在于激活中；小型探针可恢复该信号，且表面特征可预测性可诊断何时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器广泛用于评估，但判定易受位置、长度等表面特征影响；核心问题是错误判定源于信息缺失，还是信息存在于内部表示却未被输出反映。

Method: 跨64个开放权重评估器、14个数据集；对41个评判器进行因果干预，运行中途编辑激活；在LLMBar等基准上用小型探针读取激活，并残差化表面特征；比较rubric任务、八个基准及模型家族；计算表面特征预测人类标签的得分以预测增益；在相同标签预算下评估标记错误和偏好学习标签。

Result: LLMBar上50个评判器与人类标签一致率仅0.456；同激活探针达0.846，残差化后0.686，打乱标签0.507；差距跨八个基准和模型家族，但非普遍；表面特征预测得分与增益大小相关（Spearman rho=0.90）；rubric任务中读取内部无优势；编辑网络中途激活可改变判定并定位位置/长度偏差通路；恢复信号可标记可能错误并改进偏好学习标签。

Conclusion: 错误判定不必然意味着评判器缺少信息；内部激活常包含可恢复的正确信号，简单诊断可判断何时值得恢复，从而提升评判可靠性与偏好学习标签质量。

中文摘要：LLM 评判器被广泛用于评估模型输出，但其判定可能不可靠：评判器可能因为位置、长度或其他表面特征而偏向更差的答案。当评判器出错时，正确判断所需的信息是根本不存在于模型中，还是存在于其内部表示中但未反映在输出里？我们在 64 个开放权重评估器和 14 个数据集上研究这一问题，包括对 41 个评判器进行因果干预（在运行中途编辑激活值，观察判定是否改变）。在 LLMBar 上——该数据集被构建为表面更好的答案实际上是更差的答案——50 个评判器的判定与人类标签的一致率仅为 0.456，即使对两种答案顺序取平均后也是如此。然而，对同一批评判器的激活值使用一个小型探针，无需更新权重，即可达到 0.846；在将长度和位置等表面特征残差化剔除后为 0.686（打乱标签时为 0.507）。这一差距在八个基准和模型家族中均存在，但并非普遍适用：仅凭表面特征预测人类标签的效果得分，在训练任何探针之前计算，可预测增益大小（Spearman rho = 0.90）。在每次只对一个答案评分的 rubric 任务中，由于没有可利用的表面线索，读取内部信息没有优势。干预还表明，在网络中途编辑激活值就已经会改变判定，甚至在能够直接读取该判定之前，并定位了携带位置和长度偏差的通路。在相同标签预算下，恢复出的信号使评判器能够标记其可能出错的情况，并为偏好学习生成更好的标签。因此，错误的判定并不意味着评判器缺乏所需信息，而一个简单的诊断可以显示何时值得恢复该信息。

Original abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate model outputs, but their verdicts can be unreliable: a judge may favor the worse answer for its position, length, or other surface features. When a judge is wrong, is the information needed to judge correctly absent from the model, or present in its internal representations but not reflected in the output? We study this across 64 open-weight evaluators and 14 datasets, including causal interventions on 41 judges (editing activations mid-run to see whether the verdict changes). On LLMBar, built so the superficially better answer is the worse one, the verdicts of 50 judges agree with human labels only 0.456 of the time, even after averaging both answer orders. Yet a small probe on the same judges' activations, with no weight updates, reaches 0.846, and 0.686 once surface features such as length and position are residualized out (0.507 with shuffled labels). The gap holds across eight benchmarks and model families, but is not universal: a score of how well surface features alone predict the human label, computed before any probe is trained, predicts the size of the gain (Spearman rho = 0.90). On rubric tasks that score one answer at a time, leaving no surface cue to exploit, reading the internals gives no advantage. The interventions also show that editing activations mid-network already changes the verdict, before it can be read off directly, and locate the pathways carrying position and length bias. At the same label budget, the recovered signal lets a judge flag cases where it is likely wrong and yields better labels for preference learning. A wrong verdict, then, does not mean the judge lacks the information, and a simple diagnostic shows when it is worth recovering.

</details>


### [61] [PluginRSI: Recursive Improvement of Agent Harnesses with Reusable Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32423)
*Yaorui Shi,Yuchun Miao,Yuxin Chen,Jiayuan Zhang,Yueqing Sun,Xierui Song,Xiang Wang,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 agent harness 拆解为可复用插件并递归自改进，直接对应 harness/workflow 优化与经验复用。

TL;DR: PluginRSI 将 harness 拆解为可独立演化和复用的原子化插件，通过共享插件库组合生成新 harness，在多种任务上优于现有方法，并具备迁移与加速优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness 优化方法在完整程序空间搜索，导致单个机制难以隔离、复用和持续积累，从而限制优化效率与泛化。

Method: PluginRSI 将 harness 表示为原子化插件的组合；独立改进单个插件并存入共享库，每次迭代再将插件重新组合成新的 harness。

Result: 在软件工程、命令行交互和问答任务上，PluginRSI 优于现有 harness 优化方法；所得 harness 无需进一步优化即可迁移到其他求解器模型并保持优势；演化出的插件库还能从初始 harness 加速后续优化，在未见任务上实现更快、更高收敛。

Conclusion: 积累可复用机制是持续改进 harness 的有效基础。

中文摘要：围绕语言模型的 harness 是决定智能体性能的核心因素。近期方法通过对完整程序进行搜索来优化 harness，其中单个机制难以被隔离和复用。我们提出 PluginRSI，将 harness 表示为原子化插件的组合，并围绕这些插件组织 harness 演化。单个插件被独立改进并积累在共享库中，然后在每次迭代时重新组合成新的 harness。PluginRSI 在软件工程、命令行交互和问答任务上优于现有的 harness 优化方法。所得 harness 在无需进一步优化的情况下迁移到其他求解器模型时仍保持其优势。演化出的插件库从初始 harness 加速后续优化，这有助于在未见任务上更快、更高地收敛。这些结果表明，积累可复用机制为持续改进 harness 提供了有效基础。

Original abstract: The harness surrounding a language model is a central determinant of agent performance. Recent methods optimize harnesses by searching over complete programs, where individual mechanisms are difficult to isolate and reuse. We introduce PluginRSI, which represents a harness as a composition of atomized plugins and organizes harness evolution around these plugins. Individual plugins are improved independently and accumulated in a shared library, then recombined into new harnesses at each iteration. PluginRSI improves over existing harness optimization methods across software engineering, command-line interaction, and question-answering tasks. The resulting harnesses retain their advantage when transferred to other solver models without further optimization. The evolved plugin library accelerates subsequent optimization from the initial harness, which helps faster and higher convergence on unseen tasks. These results show that accumulating reusable mechanisms provides an effective basis for continued harness improvement.

</details>


### [62] [Authorization Closure Graph: Minimal Repair for LLM Agents with Evolving User Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32428)
*Qingzhuo Wang,CaiYi Wang,Jinglu Meng,Ruiyang Qin,Kunyu Peng,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 8 figures, 6 tables. Preprint under review

Selection reason: 面向指令演化的授权闭包图与最小修复，为工具型 agent 的权限/状态更新提供通用机制。

TL;DR: 提出基于授权闭包图（ACG）的框架，将授权表示为带版本的演化状态，支持部分指令变更时的选择性失效与最小修复，从而提升动作安全率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法在仅部分指令发生变化时，有原则地选择性地更新先前的授权，可能导致过时权限和不必要的授权请求。

Method: 提出ACG框架，将授权及其依赖关系建模为不断演化、带版本的状态；选择性使受修订影响的权限失效，保留未受影响部分，并计算最小修复以识别执行所需的缺失证据或权限。

Result: 在两个自然任务中跨三个先进LLM评估ACG，动作安全率和任务成功率均一致提升。

Conclusion: ACG使智能体能够适应修订后的指令，同时避免过时权限和不必要的授权请求。代码已开源。

中文摘要：使用工具的大语言模型（LLM）智能体日益执行需要用户授权的状态变更操作。然而，现有方法并未提供一种有原则的机制，用于在仅部分指令发生变化时选择性地更新先前的授权。为此，我们提出了一种基于授权闭包图（Authorization-Closure-Graph，ACG）的框架，将授权及其依赖关系表示为不断演化、带版本的状态。ACG会选择性地使受修订影响的权限失效，同时保留授权状态中未受影响的部分，并计算最小修复，仅识别执行所需的缺失证据或权限。这使智能体能够适应修订后的指令，同时避免过时权限和不必要的授权请求。我们在两个自然任务中跨三个先进LLM评估ACG，ACG一致地提升了动作安全率和任务成功率。代码见 https://github.com/weiliang822/ACG。

Original abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents increasingly perform state-changing actions that require user authorization. Yet existing approaches do not provide a principled mechanism for selectively updating prior authorization when only part of an instruction changes. To this end, we propose an Authorization-Closure-Graph (ACG)-based framework that represents authorization and its dependencies as an evolving, versioned state. ACG selectively invalidates authority affected by a revision while preserving unaffected portions of the authorization state, and computes a minimal repair that identifies only the missing evidence or authority required for execution. This enables agents to adapt to revised instructions while avoiding stale authority and unnecessary authorization requests. We evaluate ACG across three advanced LLMs in two natural tasks, and ACG consistently improves action safety rate and task success rate. Code is available at https://github.com/weiliang822/ACG.

</details>


### [63] [Multi-Agent System Search via Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32430)
*Beicheng Xu,Bowen Fan,Weitong Qian,Lingching Tung,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用 RL 搜索多 agent 系统结构，含子结构级奖励与查询选择机制，可迁移到 agent 架构搜索。

TL;DR: 提出 ASPO，一种查询级 MAS 搜索强化学习框架，通过自适应查询选择、困难查询探索和子结构级奖励学习有效多智能体系统；在六个基准上均优于十二个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计多智能体系统的角色、提示和通信结构成本高，而现有基于固定查询集和工作流级奖励的训练忽略了查询学习潜力的变化，也无法识别提升解质量的子结构。

Method: ASPO 使用 AQSM 聚焦处于能力边界的查询，辅以发现机制扩大困难查询的架构探索，并采用子结构级奖励衡量动作后代子图的输出质量增益，再通过近端策略优化强化有效架构改进、抑制冗余或有害计算。

Result: 在数学推理、通用问答和代码生成等六个基准上，ASPO 均排名第一，超过十二个基线。

Conclusion: ASPO 通过优先学习可查询并利用细粒度反馈，有效搜索查询级多智能体系统，在多种任务上取得领先性能。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）使多智能体系统（MAS）能够处理复杂任务，但手动设计智能体角色、提示和通信结构需要大量专业知识与努力。这促使人们学习能够根据执行奖励构建针对查询的 MAS 的策略。现有方法通常通过反复遍历固定的训练查询集，并在工作流层面分配奖励来训练这些策略。然而，这忽略了查询不断变化的学习潜力的差异，并模糊了哪些子结构提升了解决方案质量。在本文中，我们提出主动子结构感知策略优化（ASPO），一个用于查询级 MAS 搜索的强化学习框架。ASPO 引入自适应查询选择机制（AQSM），将训练集中在处于策略能力边界的查询上：即那些它能够解决但尚不能可靠解决的查询。一个互补的发现机制拓宽了针对困难查询的架构探索，有助于区分探索不足与算子能力限制。除查询选择外，ASPO 还引入子结构级奖励，用于衡量每个动作的后代子图内的输出质量增益。这些奖励指导近端策略优化，以强化有用的架构改进，并抑制冗余或有害计算。这些机制共同优先考虑可学习的查询，并为学习有效的 MAS 提供细粒度反馈。在涵盖数学推理、通用问答和代码生成的六个基准上，ASPO 在对抗十二个基线时于每个基准上均排名第一。

Original abstract: LLMs enable multi-agent systems (MAS) to tackle complex tasks, but manually designing agent roles, prompts, and communication structures requires substantial expertise and effort. This motivates learning policies that construct query-specific MAS from execution reward. Existing approaches typically train these policies by repeatedly traversing a fixed set of training queries and assigning rewards at the workflow level. However, this overlooks differences in queries' evolving learning potential and obscures which substructures improve solution quality. In this paper, we propose Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization (ASPO), a RL framework for query-level MAS search. ASPO introduces an Adaptive Query-Selection Mechanism (AQSM) that focuses training on queries at the policy's competence boundary: those it can solve but not yet reliably. A complementary discovery mechanism widens architectural exploration for hard queries, helping distinguish insufficient exploration from operator capability limits. Beyond query selection, ASPO introduces substructure-level rewards that measure output-quality gains within each action's descendant subgraph. These rewards guide proximal policy optimization to reinforce useful architectural refinements and discourage redundant or harmful computation. Together, these mechanisms prioritize learnable queries and provide fine-grained feedback for learning effective MASs. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, general question answering, and code generation, ASPO ranks first on every benchmark against twelve baselines.

</details>


### [64] [PULSE: Identifying Demonstration-Utility Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32469)
*Chenduo Hao,Chuanbao Gao,Pinjun Zeng,Jingze Zhu,Chonghan Liu,Zidong Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 24 pages

Selection reason: 用 SAE 内部特征度量示例效用并做选择，思路可迁移到 agent 记忆/经验检索与上下文选择。

TL;DR: PULSE 利用 SAE 识别与示例效用相关的模型内部特征，构建稀疏效用定位向量，并用于完整集排序和池级检索，在分类、生成和推理任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有示例选择多依赖外部查询-示例相似度，容易忽略模型特定信号：相似示例可能激活不同的内部特征与下游行为。因此需要直接识别与示例效用相关的模型内部特征。

Method: 采用基于 SAE 的 PULSE 框架：用小型带标注发现集采样候选示例集，在目标模型下测量其零样本相对效用，按激活差异与效用差异的对齐程度评分 SAE 特征，取正负坐标构成稀疏效用定位向量；该向量可作为有符号分数进行受控完整集排序，也可通过 PULSE-Retriever 将幅度转为特征相关性掩码实现可扩展池级检索。

Result: 在分类、生成和推理基准上，PULSE-Retriever 分别比最强基线提升 2-3 个准确率点、0.6-0.9 BLEU-4 和 3.2 个精确匹配点；受控排序验证所识别特征编码可预测的集合级效用信号。特征检查与跨数据集实验显示，识别出的特征捕捉任务相关、数据集条件化模式，同时保留部分可跨数据集迁移的效用信号。

Conclusion: PULSE 能定位与示例效用相关的模型内部特征，并有效用于示例选择和检索，在多种任务上提升性能，且所识别效用信号具有部分跨数据集迁移性。代码已开源。

中文摘要：上下文学习对示例选择高度敏感，但大多数方法使用外部查询-示例相似度来选择示例。此类标准可能遗漏模型特定信号：相似的示例可能激活不同的内部特征和下游行为。我们提出 PULSE（Paired Utility Localization over Sparse Encodings，基于稀疏编码的配对效用定位），一个基于 SAE 的框架，用于识别与示例效用相关的模型内部特征，并将其用于示例选择。使用小型带标注发现集，PULSE 采样候选示例集，在目标模型下测量其相对于零样本的效用，并根据 SAE 特征的激活差异与效用差异的对齐程度对特征评分。排名靠前的正坐标和负坐标构成稀疏效用定位向量。我们以两种互补方式使用该向量：作为用于受控完整集排序的有符号分数，以及作为 PULSE-Retriever，将其幅度转换为特征相关性掩码，以实现可扩展的池级检索。在分类、生成和推理基准上，PULSE-Retriever 分别比最强基线提升 2-3 个准确率点、0.6-0.9 BLEU-4 和 3.2 个精确匹配点，而受控排序验证了所识别特征编码了可预测的集合级效用信号。特征检查和跨数据集实验表明，所识别特征捕捉了任务相关、数据集条件化的模式，但仍保留了可部分跨数据集迁移的效用信号。我们的代码见 https://github.com/aohenuo/PULSE。

Original abstract: In-context learning is highly sensitive to demonstration choice, yet most methods select demonstrations using external query-demonstration similarity. Such criteria can miss model-specific signals: Similar demonstrations may activate different internal features and downstream behaviors. We introduce PULSE (Paired Utility Localization over Sparse Encodings), an SAE-based framework for identifying model-internal features associated with demonstration utility and using them for demonstration selection. Using a small labeled discovery set, PULSE samples candidate demonstration sets, measures their zero-shot-relative utility under the target model, and scores SAE features by how their activation differences align with utility differences. The top positive and negative coordinates form a sparse utility-localization vector. We use this vector in two complementary ways: as a signed score for controlled complete-set ranking, and as PULSE-Retriever, which converts its magnitude into a feature-relevance mask for scalable pool-scale retrieval. Across classification, generation, and reasoning benchmarks, PULSE-Retriever improves over the strongest baseline by 2-3 accuracy points, 0.6-0.9 BLEU-4, and 3.2 exact-match points, respectively, while controlled ranking validates the identified features encode a predictive set-level utility signal. Feature inspection and cross-dataset experiments suggest that the identified features capture task-relevant, dataset-conditioned patterns, yet retain utility signals that partially transfer across datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/aohenuo/PULSE.

</details>


### [65] [From Outcomes to Strategies: Learning Strategy Utility for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32482)
*Ruikang Zhang,Xiao An,Xuli Shen,Jiaxing Sun,Xiaoyi Yu,Jin Zeng,Jiang Wu,Tong Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 学习策略级效用奖励模型以评估高层策略在给定 executor 下的成功概率，对 agent 规划与验证有启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出 SURE，将高层策略与详细推理分离，用策略奖励模型估计相对策略效用，并在序列级 GRPO 中结合正确性与格式奖励，从而提升数学推理平均 pass@1 且降低 GRPO 阶段计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用最终答案正确性作为奖励，难以评估定理选择、子目标分解等高层策略本身的质量，因为策略质量需与其后续执行区分开；因此需要显式建模策略对下游正确解的支撑程度。

Method: 定义策略效用为给定执行器下策略带来正确下游解的可能性；将高层策略与详细推理分离，基于策略条件化 rollout 与教师生成对比构建成对偏好，训练策略奖励模型；RL 时冻结该模型，仅对提取策略打分，并将其与正确性、格式奖励组合进序列级 GRPO 目标。

Result: 相比结果与格式 GRPO 基线，SURE 在三个策略骨干上平均 pass@1 分别提升 1.87%、2.64%、2.93%；相比更强奖励基线，准确率相当或更优，且 GRPO 阶段计算量显著更低。

Conclusion: 相对策略效用可作为独立于执行结果的有效学习信号，提升策略选择与推理表现，并在计算效率上具有优势。

中文摘要：具有可验证奖励的强化学习已显著提升了数学推理能力。然而，仅凭最终正确性，当把高层策略（例如定理选择与子目标分解）与其后续执行分开考虑时，只能提供对其质量的有限洞察。本文研究策略效用，其定义为在给定执行器下，策略支持正确下游解的可能性。我们提出 SURE，一个用于学习和利用相对策略效用的框架。在该框架中，高层策略与其详细推理相分离。基于由策略条件化的 rollout 和教师生成的对比所构建的成对偏好，学习一个策略奖励模型来估计相对策略效用。在强化学习过程中，冻结的奖励模型仅读取提取出的策略，其分数在序列级 GRPO 目标中与正确性奖励和格式奖励相结合。与结果和格式 GRPO 基线相比，实验表明，SURE 在三个策略骨干网络上将平均 pass@1 分别提升 1.87%、2.64% 和 2.93%。我们的方法在显著降低 GRPO 阶段计算量的同时，也达到了与更强奖励基线相当或更好的准确率。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has substantially improved mathematical reasoning. However, terminal correctness alone provides limited insight into the quality of high-level strategies, such as theorem selection and subgoal decomposition, when considered separately from their subsequent execution. This paper studies strategy utility, which is defined as the likelihood that a strategy supports a correct downstream solution under a given executor. We introduce SURE, a framework for learning and leveraging relative strategy utility. In this framework, high-level strategies are separated from their detailed reasoning. Based on the pairwise preferences constructed from strategy-conditioned rollouts and teacher-generated contrasts, a Strategy Reward Model is learned to estimate relative strategy utility. During reinforcement learning, the frozen reward model reads only the extracted strategy, whose score is combined with the correctness and format rewards in a sequence-level GRPO objective. Compared with outcome-and-format GRPO baselines, experiments show that SURE improves average pass@1 by 1.87%, 2.64%, and 2.93% across three policy backbones. Our method also achieves competitive or better accuracy than stronger reward baselines while requiring substantially lower GRPO-stage compute.

</details>


### [66] [RepoMAS: Solving Progressively Specified Tasks with Issue-Driven Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32490)
*Yuchen Song,Andong Chen,Wenxin Zhu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向渐进式明确任务的多 agent 框架与 ProgSpec 基准，以 Issue 驱动需求与执行结构修订，属长程 agent 通用方法。

TL;DR: 论文提出面向需求逐步明确任务的 ProgSpec 基准与 RepoMAS 框架；RepoMAS 以结构化 Issues 记录新需求、冲突与失败，并动态修订任务规范与执行结构，在 ProgSpec 及五个现有基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体系统大多假设任务需求在执行前已充分指定，但真实用户请求常不完整，额外需求可能在推理、工具使用或执行过程中才变得清晰。

Method: 提出 ProgSpec 基准，依据初始请求中明确陈述的需求与可用任务证据支持的额外需求评估最终输出；提出 RepoMAS，受开源项目管理启发，将新发现的需求、冲突和失败记录为结构化 Issues，并据此修订任务规范和执行结构。

Result: 在 ProgSpec 和五个现有基准上，RepoMAS 均取得最佳性能；进一步分析表明，其 issue 驱动修订与仓库维护机制持续对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: MAS 不仅应修订任务求解方式，还应在执行过程中修订其对任务需求的显式表示。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在解决复杂任务方面展现出强大潜力，但大多数系统假设在执行之前任务需求已被充分指定。在实践中，用户请求往往不完整，而额外需求可能只有在推理、工具使用或执行过程中才会变得清晰。我们将此类问题称为渐进式指定任务。为了系统地研究这一设定，我们提出了 ProgSpec，一个基准，用于根据初始请求中明确陈述的需求以及由可用任务证据支持的额外需求来评估最终输出。我们进一步提出了 RepoMAS，一个受开源项目管理启发的、由问题（issue）驱动的多智能体框架。RepoMAS 将新发现的需求、冲突和失败记录为结构化的 Issues，并利用它们在问题求解过程中修订任务规范和执行结构。在 ProgSpec 和五个现有基准上，RepoMAS 取得了最佳性能。进一步分析表明，其问题驱动的修订和仓库维护机制持续对性能有贡献。这些结果凸显了允许 MAS 不仅修订任务如何被解决，还在执行过程中修订其对任务需求的显式表示的重要性。

Original abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MASs) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, but most assume that task requirements are sufficiently specified before execution. In practice, user requests are often incomplete, and additional requirements may only become clear during reasoning, tool use, or execution. We refer to such problems as progressively specified tasks.
  To systematically study this setting, we introduce ProgSpec, a benchmark that evaluates final outputs against requirements explicitly stated in the initial request and additional requirements supported by the available task evidence. We further propose RepoMAS, an issue-driven multi-agent framework inspired by open-source project management. RepoMAS records newly discovered requirements, conflicts, and failures as structured Issues and uses them to revise the task specification and execution structure during problem solving.
  Across ProgSpec and five existing benchmarks, RepoMAS achieves the best performance. Further analyses show that its issue-driven revision and repository maintenance mechanisms consistently contribute to performance. These results highlight the importance of allowing MASs to revise not only how a task is solved, but also revise their explicit representation of task requirements during execution.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond Prompt or Skill? Attribution-Guided Optimization of Modular LLM Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32492)
*Haoran Shou,Haoyue Liu,Yu Huo,Kun Zeng,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 归因引导地联合优化 prompt、可复用 skill 与路由规则，与自动优化 agent 提示/技能库高度可迁移。

TL;DR: SPARO 联合优化任务指令、可复用技能块与路由规则，通过反事实评估将示例效果转化为组件责任分布并定向变异，在五个基准和五个工作模型上持续优于提示中心与技能中心基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法通常只关注提示或技能中的单一环节，缺乏在失败时决定更新哪个组件的有原则机制，也较少将提示、技能与技能使用策略统一优化。

Method: SPARO 在统一框架中联合优化任务指令、可复用技能块和路由规则；它进行受控反事实评估，将示例效果转化为对提示、技能、路由组件的概率责任分布，采样一个组件并施加针对性变异。

Result: 在五个基准和五个工作模型上，SPARO 一致优于以提示为中心和以技能为中心的优化基线。

Conclusion: 有效的语言程序优化不仅取决于发现有用的任务知识，还取决于决定该知识应存储在何处以及何时应被激活。

中文摘要：大型语言模型能够解决日益多样的推理任务，但其性能仍然对任务提示、中间指令以及可复用的问题求解知识的引入方式高度敏感。现有的优化方法通常只关注这一设计空间的一部分：它们要么优化单一的提示，要么从模型轨迹中分别归纳并精炼技能。因此，它们缺乏一种有原则的机制来决定当失败发生时应该更新哪个组件，并且很少在统一框架中优化提示、技能和技能使用策略。我们提出 SPARO（Skill, Prompt, And Routing Optimization，技能、提示与路由优化），一个联合优化任务指令、可复用技能块和路由规则的框架。它执行受控的反事实评估，将示例的影响转换为关于提示、技能和路由组件的概率责任分布，从该分布中采样一个组件，并应用相应的针对性变异。这一设计将语言程序优化从全局提示重写推进到结构化、可复用且选择性激活的任务知识。在五个基准和五个工作模型上，SPARO 始终优于以提示为中心和以技能为中心的优化基线。这些结果表明，有效的语言程序优化不仅取决于发现有用的任务知识，还取决于决定该知识应存储在何处以及何时应被激活。

Original abstract: Large language models can solve increasingly diverse reasoning tasks, yet their performance remains highly sensitive to task prompts, intermediate instructions, and the way reusable problem-solving knowledge is incorporated. Existing optimization methods usually focus on only one part of this design space: they either optimize a monolithic prompt, or separately induce and refine skills from model traces. As a result, they lack a principled mechanism for deciding which component should be updated when failures occur, and they rarely optimize prompts, skills, and skill-use policies in a unified framework. We propose SPARO (Skill, Prompt, And Routing Optimization), a framework that jointly optimizes task instructions, reusable skill blocks, and routing rules. It performs controlled counterfactual evaluations, converts examples' effects into a probabilistic responsibility distribution over prompt, skill, and routing components, samples one component from that distribution, and applies the corresponding targeted mutation. This design moves language-program optimization beyond global prompt rewriting toward structured, reusable, and selectively activated task knowledge. Across five benchmarks and five worker models, SPARO consistently outperforms both prompt-centered and skill-centered optimization baselines. These results suggest that effective language-program optimization depends not only on discovering useful task knowledge, but also on deciding where that knowledge should be stored and when it should be activated.

</details>


### [68] [DAAF: From Failure Localization to Editable System Assets in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32498)
*Xiaoyang Yuan,Qi Liu,Yubin Ruan,Xinyi Mou,Zhuomeng Zhang,Wenjin Wang,Hanying Jiao,Di Wu,Mingye Xu,Yi Bin,Ke Feng,Zixun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 1 figure. Preprint

Selection reason: 将失败归因到可编辑的系统资产并给出修复决策与 no_change 判定，直接对应 agent 自我诊断与修复。

TL;DR: 提出DAAF，面向版本化系统资产进行组件-属性故障归因，用受控重放学习替换效果，诊断时无需反事实重放或任务奖励，在tau^2-bench Telecom上取得80.72%属性Hit@1、62.65%失败恢复率和71.93%总体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障定位只能识别错误在何处显现，无法回答应修改哪个可编辑系统资产，以及该修改是否可能改善任务结果；因此需要将故障定位连接到可执行系统修复。

Method: DAAF学习有效属性替换的效果，并将干预证据摊销到部署时诊断。它结合稀疏且含噪的失败信号判断是否干预，从可执行任务结果评估的受控重放中学习组件类型条件化的替换效果，并在兼容干预响应的请求间共享监督。诊断时仅使用观察到的执行、注册候选和失败信号，不需要反事实重放或任务奖励，输出no_change、修复目标或未解决决策。

Result: 在留出的tau^2-bench Telecom任务上，DAAF达到80.72%属性Hit@1，恢复62.65%的失败执行，干净任务回归仅3.23%，总体任务成功率为71.93%。

Conclusion: 基于干预的属性归因能够将故障定位与可执行系统修复连接起来。

中文摘要：部署的LLM智能体日益依赖持久化、版本化的系统资产，如路由规则、知识片段、提示指令和可复用技能。故障定位方法可以识别错误在智能体或执行轨迹中显现的位置，但修复需要不同的决策：应更改哪个可编辑系统资产，以及该更改是否预期会改善任务结果？我们通过组件-属性故障归因研究这一缺口，其中诊断针对的是版本化、可寻址的条目，而非执行位置。我们提出检测感知归因框架（DAAF），它学习有效属性替换的效果，并将这种干预证据摊销到部署时诊断中。DAAF结合稀疏且含噪的失败信号来决定是否需要进行干预，从由可执行任务结果评估的受控重放中学习组件类型条件化的替换效果，并在具有兼容干预响应的请求之间共享监督。在诊断时，DAAF仅使用观察到的执行、已注册候选和可用的失败信号；它既不需要反事实重放，也不需要任务奖励，并返回no_change、修复目标，或证据不足时的未解决决策。在留出的tau^2-bench Telecom任务上，DAAF达到80.72%的属性Hit@1，恢复62.65%的失败执行，同时将干净任务回归限制在3.23%，并达到71.93%的总体任务成功。这些结果表明，基于干预的属性归因可以将故障定位与可执行系统修复连接起来。

Original abstract: Deployed LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent, versioned system assets such as routing rules, knowledge segments, prompt instructions, and reusable skills. Failure-localization methods can identify where an error manifests in an agent or execution trace, but repair requires a different decision: which editable system asset should be changed, and is that change expected to improve the task outcome? We study this gap through component-attribute failure attribution, where diagnosis targets versioned, addressable items rather than execution locations. We propose the Detection-Aware Attribution Framework (DAAF), which learns the effects of valid attribute replacements and amortizes this intervention evidence into deployment-time diagnosis. DAAF combines sparse and noisy failure signals to decide whether intervention is warranted, learns component-type-conditioned replacement effects from controlled replays evaluated by executable task outcomes, and shares supervision across requests with compatible intervention responses. At diagnosis time, DAAF uses only the observed execution, registered candidates, and available failure signals; it requires neither counterfactual replay nor task reward and returns no_change, a repair target, or an unresolved decision when evidence is insufficient. On held-out tau^2-bench Telecom tasks, DAAF achieves 80.72% attribute Hit@1, recovers 62.65% of failed executions while limiting clean-task regression to 3.23%, and reaches 71.93% overall task success. These results show that intervention-grounded attribute attribution can connect failure localization to executable system repair.

</details>


### [69] [Learning from Others, Acting for You: Cross-User Memory Sharing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32511)
*Jinming Hu,Haodong Zhao,Qi Jia,Die Chen,Tianhang Zhao,Sufeng Duan,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Work in progress

Selection reason: 跨用户共享记忆架构，含多会话编码任务(MemoryCode)，对agent经验复用与记忆设计有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出 ShareMem 记忆架构，在共享可复用经验的同时以接收用户偏好为约束；通过两阶段整合与范围优先检索，在多个任务和四个骨干模型上优于用户本地记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同用户的 LLM 智能体常解决相关任务，但分离的用户历史导致可复用经验无法共享；直接汇聚记忆又会迁移与接收用户需求冲突的偏好。因此，需要一种既能共享可复用经验、又能以接收用户自身偏好来约束应用的方法。

Method: ShareMem 让共享经验指示如何行动以及查询哪些偏好，由接收用户记忆提供具体偏好值；采用两阶段整合，先在本地精炼经验，再将接受的编辑并入共享池；执行时使用范围优先检索，在统一条目预算下联合选择本地与共享经验，并通过用户绑定通道支持初始和智能体主动的偏好检索。

Result: 在 Mind2Web、VitaBench 2.0、MemoryCode 三个任务及四个骨干模型上，ShareMem 分别提升步骤成功率、平均任务成功率和对话-宏观编码得分，均优于匹配的用户本地记忆。消融显示两阶段整合有利于更小共享池、更低归纳 token 用量和更好下游性能，并支持经验指导与主动偏好检索互补。分析表明，相关本地经验稀缺时共享收益最大，来源质量和跨用户偏好干扰限制有效迁移。

Conclusion: ShareMem 能有效共享可复用经验，同时保持接收用户偏好约束，提升多类任务表现；但实际收益受本地经验稀缺程度、来源质量和跨用户偏好干扰影响。

中文摘要：服务不同用户的大语言模型（LLM）智能体常常解决相关任务，但彼此分离的用户历史会使可复用经验无法被其他智能体访问。汇聚记忆可扩大访问范围，却可能迁移与接收用户需求相冲突的偏好。我们提出 ShareMem，一种记忆架构，在共享可复用经验的同时，将其应用建立在接收用户自身偏好之上。共享经验指示如何行动以及应查询哪些偏好；接收用户的记忆提供其具体偏好值。两阶段整合先在本地精炼经验，再将接受的编辑整合到共享池中。在执行期间，范围优先检索在统一条目预算下联合选择本地与共享经验，而用户绑定通道支持初始检索和由智能体发起的偏好检索。我们在网页导航（Mind2Web）、在线个性化交互（VitaBench 2.0）和多会话编码（MemoryCode）上，使用四个骨干模型评估 ShareMem。相较于匹配的用户本地记忆，它在全部四个模型上分别提升了步骤成功率、平均任务成功率和对话-宏观编码得分。消融实验支持两阶段整合有利于更小的共享池、更低的归纳 token 用量以及更好的下游性能，并支持经验指导与主动偏好检索之间的互补性。进一步分析表明，当相关本地经验稀缺时，共享帮助最大，而来源质量和跨用户偏好干扰限制了有用的迁移。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents serving different users often solve related tasks, yet separate user histories can leave reusable experience inaccessible to other agents. Pooling memories expands access but risks transferring preferences that conflict with the receiving user's requirements. We introduce ShareMem, a memory architecture that shares reusable experience while grounding its application in the receiving user's own preferences. Shared experiences indicate how to act and which preferences to consult; the receiving user's memory supplies their concrete values. Two-stage consolidation refines experience locally before integrating accepted edits into a shared pool. During execution, scope-first retrieval jointly selects local and shared experiences under a common entry budget, while a user-bound channel supports initial and agent-initiated preference retrieval. We evaluate ShareMem across web navigation (Mind2Web), online personalized interaction (VitaBench~2.0), and multi-session coding (MemoryCode) with four backbone models. It improves step success, average task success, and dialogue-macro coding scores, respectively, over matched user-local memory across all four models. Ablations favor two-stage consolidation for smaller shared pools, lower induction token usage, and better downstream performance, and support complementarity between experience guidance and active preference retrieval. Further analyses show that sharing helps most when relevant local experience is scarce, while source quality and cross-user preference interference limit useful transfer.

</details>


### [70] [From Anomalies to Failures: Constructing Causal Error Graphs for Agentic Trace Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32514)
*Shu-Xun Yang,Yidong Wang,Zhuoer Feng,Bosi Wen,Jiayi Gui,Dayong Yang,Wenbo Yu,Haoke Zhang,Jie Tang,Cunxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出因果错误图与CEG-Bench对长程agent轨迹做失败归因，可迁移到coding agent的执行轨迹诊断与可靠性验证。

TL;DR: 提出 CEG-Agent 与 CEG-Bench，通过因果错误图实现智能体轨迹的可靠失败归因，并在语义宽松与结构精确标准下达到当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹诊断方法混淆异常、错误和失败，诊断目标不明确；同时缺乏对因果相关错误传播并放大为最终任务失败的结构化建模，导致失败归因不可靠。

Method: 提出工具增强的智能体框架 CEG-Agent，定义异常、错误和失败的明确分类法，并构建因果错误图（CEG）作为统一类型化表示，连接执行事件、诊断节点和失败结果；进一步构建 CEG-Bench 基准，通过对抗式智能体裁决协议生成高置信度、基于共识的 CEG 标注，并依据专家整理的人类金标准集进行验证。

Result: 自动标注与专家人类金标准集高度一致；在 CEG-Bench 上，CEG-Agent 在语义宽松和结构精确两种评估标准下均达到当前最优性能；代码已公开。

Conclusion: CEG-Agent 能够有效支持智能体轨迹的因果诊断与失败归因，CEG-Bench 及相应标注协议为评估因果轨迹诊断提供了可靠基础。

中文摘要：LLM 驱动的智能体越来越多地部署在复杂应用中，其中漫长的智能体轨迹使失败难以诊断。现有轨迹诊断方法常常混淆异常、错误和失败，导致诊断目标不明确；它们也缺乏对因果相关错误如何传播并放大为最终任务失败的结构化建模，导致失败归因不可靠。为解决这些问题，我们提出 CEG-Agent，一种用于智能体轨迹因果诊断的工具增强智能体框架。具体而言，CEG-Agent 引入了异常、错误和失败的明确分类法，并构建因果错误图（Causal Error Graphs, CEGs），这是一种统一的类型化表示，通过因果关系将执行事件、诊断节点和失败结果联系起来。为了评估因果轨迹诊断，我们进一步构建 CEG-Bench，这是一个完全由智能体标注的基准，通过对抗式智能体裁决协议（AAAP）获得高置信度、基于共识的 CEG 标注。我们针对专家整理的人类金标准集验证所得标注，结果显示自动标注与人类标注高度一致。在 CEG-Bench 上的实验表明，CEG-Agent 在语义宽松和结构精确两种评估标准下均达到当前最优性能。我们的代码已公开可用。

Original abstract: LLM-driven agents are increasingly deployed in complex applications, where long agentic traces make failures difficult to diagnose. Existing trace diagnosis methods often conflate anomalies, errors, and failures, making diagnostic targets ambiguous; they also lack structured modeling of how causally relevant errors propagate and amplify into final task failures, resulting in unreliable failure attribution. To address these problems, we propose CEG-Agent, a tool-augmented agentic framework for causal diagnosis of agentic traces. Specifically, CEG-Agent introduces an explicit taxonomy of anomalies, errors, and failures, and constructs Causal Error Graphs (CEGs), a unified typed representation that links execution events, diagnostic nodes, and failure outcomes through causal relations. To evaluate causal trace diagnosis, we further construct CEG-Bench, a fully agent-annotated benchmark with high-confidence, consensus-derived CEG annotations obtained through an Adversarial Agentic Adjudication Protocol (AAAP). We validate the resulting annotations against an expert-curated human gold set, which shows close agreement with the automatic annotations. Experiments on CEG-Bench demonstrate that CEG-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance under both semantically relaxed and structurally exact evaluation criteria. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [MemAgent: Learning to Manage Heterogeneous Memory Providers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32521)
*Yongxian Wei,Yilin Zhao,Runxi Cheng,Xinrui Chen,Chun Yuan,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 把agent记忆建模为异构记忆提供者路由问题并合成路由监督数据，对SemOpt经验/轨迹复用的记忆设计高度可迁移。

TL;DR: 该文将智能体记忆管理建模为路由问题，提出内容感知路由架构与训练数据合成流水线MemAgent，在多个基准上以低开销显著提升准确率并减少任务步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体跨任务基本无状态，难以从先验交互中持续改进；现有记忆方法多依赖单一记忆表示，其效果随任务分布变化，且实验发现没有单一方法能跨基准泛化，因此需要管理异构记忆提供者。

Method: 将智能体记忆形式化为路由问题，由记忆智能体决定检索哪个记忆提供者、是否注入短期记忆以及由哪些提供者存储经验；提出MemAgent，包含内容感知路由架构（检索前内容感知探测、执行中短期记忆门控、选择性多提供者存储）和可为路由决策合成阶段特定监督信号的训练数据合成流水线。

Result: 在GAIA、WebWalkerQA和xBench-DS上，MemAgent平均准确率提升10.0%，并在三个基准上均优于每一种单独的记忆方法；路由开销低于0.3%，平均任务步数减少12%。

Conclusion: 通过将异构记忆提供者的管理转化为路由问题，MemAgent能够有效利用多种记忆表示，在低开销下提升长时程智能体任务表现并提高执行效率。

中文摘要：当前智能体在任务间基本处于无状态，限制了其从先前交互中持续改进的能力，也使记忆成为长时程智能体行为的关键。现有记忆方法试图复用过去经验，但大多依赖单一记忆表示（如轨迹、反思、技能、结构化知识），其有效性会随任务分布而变化。重新审视这一设计空间，我们评估了13种记忆方法，发现没有单一方法能跨基准泛化，这揭示了管理异构记忆提供者的潜力。我们将智能体记忆形式化为一个路由问题，其中记忆智能体决定从哪个记忆提供者检索、是否注入短期记忆，以及哪些提供者应存储由此产生的经验。基于这一视角，我们提出MemAgent，具有内容感知路由架构和训练数据合成流水线。路由架构结合了检索前的内容感知探测、执行期间的短期记忆门控以及选择性多提供者存储，而训练流水线为路由决策合成阶段特定的监督信号。在GAIA、WebWalkerQA和xBench-DS上，MemAgent将平均准确率提升10.0%，并在所有三个基准上优于每一种单独的记忆方法。这些收益仅带来不到0.3%的路由开销，并使平均任务步数减少12%。

Original abstract: Current agents remain largely stateless across tasks, limiting their ability to continually improve from prior interactions and making memory essential for long-horizon agentic behavior. Existing memory methods seek to reuse past experience, but most rely on a single memory representation (e.g., trajectories, reflections, skills, structured knowledge) whose effectiveness varies across task distributions. Rethinking this design space, we evaluate 13 memory methods and find that no single method generalizes across benchmarks, revealing the potential of managing heterogeneous memory providers. We formulate agent memory as a routing problem in which a memory agent decides which memory provider to retrieve from, whether to inject short-term memory, and which providers should store the resulting experience. Based on this perspective, we propose MemAgent, featuring a content-aware routing architecture and a training-data synthesis pipeline. The routing architecture combines content-aware probing before retrieval, short-term memory gating during execution, and selective multi-provider storage, while the training pipeline synthesizes phase-specific supervision for routing decisions. Across GAIA, WebWalkerQA, and xBench-DS, MemAgent improves average accuracy by 10.0% and outperforms every individual memory method across all three benchmarks. These gains come with less than 0.3% routing overhead and a 12% reduction in average task steps.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Dyadic Memory: Interaction-Aware Multimodal Memory with Adaptive Agentic Retrieval for Multi-Party Spoken Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32522)
*Wenxu Jia,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Dongjie Fu,Linjun Li,Wenshi Chen,Yangyang Wu,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多模态多说话人长期记忆与自适应agentic检索/证据增益RL，检索决策机制对agent记忆有一般启发，但场景偏语音垂直。

TL;DR: 提出面向多人语音对话的交互感知多模态长期记忆框架 VoxPolyMem，结合增量式说话人识别、分层记忆与基于序列决策的检索，并引入 EG-GRPO 与 VoxPolyBench；其在多个基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆研究主要集中于双人文本或图文对话，较少关注多人语音对话；而后者需同时保留对话内容、跨会话识别参与者并追踪说话对象，因此亟需专门的多模态记忆框架与评测基准。

Method: 提出 VoxPolyMem：将增量式说话人识别与交互记忆、事实记忆、参与者画像组成的记忆层级结合；把检索建模为序列决策，由智能体重写查询、选择检索工具与记忆层以弥补信息缺口；引入 Evidence-Gain GRPO（EG-GRPO），通过逐轮信用分配鼓励获取互补证据；构建 VoxPolyBench 评估记忆演化、个性化回答、记忆检索与推理、交互推理与归属。

Result: VoxPolyMem 在 VoxPolyBench 上总体得分 85.0，超过最强评估基线 23.6 分；在 Mem-Gallery 和 H2HMem-Multi 上分别得分 89.6 和 74.4，均超过最强公开记忆基线 8 分以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明 VoxPolyMem 在多人多模态交互中具有提供持久、个性化辅助的潜力，代码与数据集已公开。

中文摘要：长期记忆使智能体能够积累信息并跨会话进行推理，然而现有研究主要聚焦于双人文本或图文对话，对多人语音对话中的长期记忆仍探索不足。该设定需要保留对话内容、跨会话识别参与者，并记住谁对谁说话。为此，我们提出 VoxPolyMem，一种交互感知的多模态记忆框架，将增量式说话人识别与包含交互记忆、事实记忆和参与者画像的记忆层级相结合。我们将检索形式化为序列决策，其中智能体基于累积证据重写查询并选择检索工具和记忆层，以弥补信息缺口。我们进一步提出 Evidence-Gain GRPO（EG-GRPO），它使用逐轮信用分配来鼓励互补证据的获取。我们还构建了 VoxPolyBench，用于评估多人语音对话中的记忆演化、个性化回答、记忆检索与推理，以及交互推理与归属。VoxPolyMem 在 VoxPolyBench 上取得 85.0 的总体得分，超过最强评估基线 23.6 分。在 Mem-Gallery 和 H2HMem-Multi 上，它分别得分 89.6 和 74.4，均比最强的公开记忆基线高出 8 分以上。这些结果凸显了其在多人多模态交互中提供持久、个性化辅助的潜力。代码和数据集见 https://voxpolymem.github.io/VoxPolyBench/demo/

Original abstract: Long-term memory enables agents to accumulate information and reason across sessions, yet existing research primarily focuses on dyadic text or image-text conversations, leaving long-term memory for multi-party spoken conversations underexplored. This setting requires preserving conversational content, identifying participants across sessions, and retaining who speaks to whom. To this end, we propose VoxPolyMem, an interaction-aware multimodal memory framework combining incremental speaker identification with a memory hierarchy comprising interaction memory, fact memory, and participant profiles. We formulate retrieval as sequential decision-making, where an agent rewrites queries and selects retrieval tools and memory layers based on accumulated evidence to address information gaps. We further introduce Evidence-Gain GRPO (EG-GRPO), which uses round-wise credit assignment to encourage complementary evidence acquisition. We also construct VoxPolyBench to evaluate memory evolution, personalized answering, memory retrieval and reasoning, and interaction reasoning and attribution in multi-party spoken conversations. VoxPolyMem achieves an overall score of 85.0 on VoxPolyBench, surpassing the strongest evaluated baseline by 23.6 points. On Mem-Gallery and H2HMem-Multi, it scores 89.6 and 74.4, respectively, exceeding the strongest evaluated public memory baselines by over 8 points each. These results highlight its potential for persistent, personalized assistance in multi-party multimodal interactions. Code and datasets are available at https://voxpolymem.github.io/VoxPolyBench/demo/

</details>


### [73] [Fail Loudly: An Auditable Runtime for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32528)
*Hanxu Yan,Langxuan Deng,Zhengle Wang,Yibo Wang,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: RADAR可审计运行时用类型化算子与运行时校验让agent"响亮失败"并据此修订，直接对应代码/数据分析agent的验证与反馈机制。

TL;DR: RADAR 是面向数据科学智能体的可审计运行时，通过证据保留检索、类型化算子记录与运行时验证，使分析选择可检查并在错误传播前修正；在 KramaBench、DA-Code、DABStep 上取得显著相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 数据科学智能体易在数据来源、范围或统计定义上做出错误选择，导致计算成功但输出看似合理却错误，且难以察觉。

Method: RADAR 包含三个机制：证据保留探索以检索相关内容并保留来源位置和观察覆盖；类型化算子记录声明的输入、操作参数和观察；运行时验证检查提议操作，遇冲突则拒绝或给出诊断反馈，让智能体在传播前修正。语义解释仍由 LLM 完成。

Result: KramaBench 总分 0.723（完整来源检索）和 0.747（提供黄金来源），相对最强基线提升 35.9% 和 28.8%；DA-Code 相对提升 14.0%，DABStep 相对提升 59.3%。

Conclusion: RADAR 能让智能体显式失败并支持分析选择修正，适用于多样化的智能体数据分析工作流。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）使数据科学智能体能够对异构文件自动化执行多步骤分析。然而，在数据来源、范围或统计定义方面作出错误选择，往往会导致静默错误：计算成功执行，却产生看似合理但不正确的输出，无法回答原本的问题。为缓解这一问题，我们提出 RADAR，一种可审计的运行时，使智能体的分析选择可被检查，并通过执行反馈支持其修正。RADAR 通过三个核心机制运行。首先，一个保留证据的探索模块检索任务相关内容，同时保留来源位置和观察覆盖范围。接下来，运行时使用类型化算子记录智能体声明的输入、操作参数和产生的观察结果。最后，运行时验证会根据这些观察结果检查提议的操作。当检测到冲突时，运行时会拒绝该操作或提供诊断反馈，使智能体能够在错误传播之前修正其选择。这种设计使智能体能够显式失败，同时将语义解释留给 LLM。在 KramaBench 上，RADAR 在完整来源检索下取得 0.723 的总分，在提供黄金来源时取得 0.747，相对于最强基线分别获得 35.9% 和 28.8% 的相对提升。在 KramaBench 之外，RADAR 在 DA-Code 上取得 14.0% 的相对性能提升，在 DABStep 上取得 59.3% 的相对性能提升，表明其适用于多样化的智能体数据分析工作流。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled data-science agents to automate multi-step analyses over heterogeneous files. However, incorrect choices regarding data sources, scope, or statistical definitions often lead to silent errors: computations execute successfully but produce plausible yet incorrect outputs that fail to answer the intended question. To mitigate this, we present RADAR, an auditable runtime that makes an agent's analytical choices inspectable and supports their revision through execution feedback. RADAR operates through three core mechanisms. First, an evidence-preserving exploration module retrieves task-relevant content while retaining source locations and observation coverage. Next, the runtime uses typed operators to record the agent's declared inputs, operation arguments, and resulting observations. Finally, runtime validation checks proposed operations against these observations. When a conflict is detected, the runtime rejects the operation or provides diagnostic feedback, allowing the agent to revise its choices before errors propagate. This design enables agents to fail loudly while leaving semantic interpretation to the LLM. On KramaBench, RADAR achieves overall scores of 0.723 with full source retrieval and 0.747 with gold sources supplied, corresponding to relative gains of 35.9% and 28.8% over the strongest baselines. Beyond KramaBench, RADAR achieves relative performance gains of 14.0% on DA-Code and 59.3% on DABStep, demonstrating its applicability across diverse agentic data-analysis workflows.

</details>


### [74] [Porimon: An LLM-Based Pokémon Battle Agent Enhanced by Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32544)
*Dongyin Zhuo,Fengjunjie Pan,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 6 pages, 5 figures, accepted by FLLM 2026

Selection reason: 以长短时知识增强让LLM agent检索相似任务的经验摘要用于对手感知规划，经验检索与复用思路可迁移到agent设计。

TL;DR: 论文提出LSTKAG与Porimon，在宝可梦对战中以15,000场实验验证，无需微调即可提升LLM智能体在对手感知规划任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升基于LLM的智能体在需要对手感知规划任务中的性能，同时避免额外微调。

Method: 提出LSTKAG，利用当前任务过去状态并基于当前状态检索相似历史任务的经验总结；据此设计Porimon智能体结构；引入外部API进行精确伤害计算和更详细游戏信息扩展；开展包含15,000场对战的锦标赛式评估、超参数优化、消融研究与性能评估。

Result: 经超参数优化的Porimon玩家显著优于PokéLLMon玩家和基于规则的启发式玩家；Porimon变体优于未扩展游戏信息检索的版本，证明该扩展有贡献；但长期KAG的贡献尚无定论。

Conclusion: 引入外部资源、当前任务历史状态信息以及相似历史任务实例的经验总结，可提升为需要对手感知规划任务设计的LLM智能体性能。

中文摘要：在本文中，我们以宝可梦对战作为案例研究，探讨如何在不进行额外微调的情况下，提升基于LLM的智能体在需要对手感知规划的任务中的性能。我们提出了长/短期知识增强生成（Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation, LSTKAG），这是一种机制，使基于LLM的智能体能够利用当前任务的过去状态，并基于当前状态从相似的先前任务实例中检索经验总结。基于LSTKAG，我们设计了Porimon，一种用于宝可梦对战的基于LLM的智能体结构。为了优化，我们引入了一个外部API，用于精确的伤害计算以及关于游戏的更详细信息。我们进行了类似于锦标赛的评估实验，包括15,000场对战，用于超参数优化、消融研究和性能评估。结果表明，经过超参数优化的基于Porimon的玩家显著优于基于PokéLLMon（先前研究中提出的基于LLM的智能体结构）的玩家以及基于规则的启发式玩家。此外，我们的消融研究表明，Porimon的变体优于未在游戏信息检索方面进行扩展的版本，这表明了该扩展的贡献。然而，当前的实验结果对于长期KAG（Long-Term KAG）的贡献尚无定论。这些结果表明，引入外部资源、当前任务先前状态的信息以及来自相似先前任务实例的经验总结，可以提升为需要对手感知规划的任务而设计的基于LLM的智能体的性能。

Original abstract: In this paper, we use Pokémon Battles as a case study to investigate how to improve the performance of LLM-based agents in tasks that require opponent-aware planning without additional fine-tuning. We propose Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation (LSTKAG), a mechanism that enables LLM-based agents to leverage past states of the current task and retrieve experience summaries from similar previous task instances based on the current state. Based on LSTKAG, we design Porimon, an LLM-based agent structure for Pokémon Battles. For optimization, we introduce an external API for precise damage calculation and more detailed information about the game. We conduct tournament-like evaluation experiments comprising 15,000 battles for hyperparameter optimization, ablation studies, and performance evaluation. The results indicate that Porimon-based players with hyperparameter optimization significantly outperform players based on PokéLLMon, an LLM-based agent structure proposed in previous research, and the rule-based heuristic player. Furthermore, our ablation study shows that Porimon variants outperform the one without extension in game information retrieval, which shows the contribution of that extension. However, the current experiment results are inconclusive regarding the contribution of Long-Term KAG. These results suggest that introducing external resources, information from previous states of the current task, and experience summaries from similar previous task instances could elevate the performance of LLM-based agents designed for tasks requiring opponent-aware planning.

</details>


### [75] [ProTTT: Learning to Learn Semantic User Memory with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32564)
*Sejun Park,Hyoungjo Bhang,Hyein Jeong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用测试时训练构建持续演化的用户记忆，记忆压缩与持续更新机制对agent长期记忆设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: ProTTT通过用户画像监督学习共享初始化，并在用户历史上进行测试时训练，形成可持续演化的参数化语义用户记忆，在个性化任务上优于全历史ICL和参数化基线且推理成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化需要语言模型利用不断增长的用户历史捕获用户特定知识；现有基于上下文的方法推理成本随历史增长而上升且依赖分离的检索或摘要，基于参数的方法在新数据到来时往往需要重建用户表示。

Method: 提出ProTTT：一种画像监督的元学习框架。记忆从共享初始化开始，针对每个用户通过在用户历史上测试时训练进行更新；并以文本用户画像为监督学习共享初始化，使测试时训练更有效地捕获语义用户知识。

Result: ProTTT在多个基准上持续优于全历史ICL和所有参数化基线，并通过将用户历史压缩为轻量级参数化记忆显著降低推理成本；分析表明画像监督是学习语义用户知识的可靠目标，记忆能跟踪并保留演变的用户偏好，且对不同历史长度稳健。

Conclusion: 研究证明测试时训练用于个性化的有效性，并将ProTTT确立为持续演化用户记忆的基线。

中文摘要：个性化要求语言模型从不断增长的用户历史中捕获用户特定知识。现有的基于上下文的方法会随着用户历史积累而带来不断增加的推理成本，并依赖单独的检索或摘要阶段；而基于参数的方法在加入新的用户数据时通常需要重建用户表示。我们提出ProTTT，一种用于学习语义用户记忆的、由画像监督的元学习框架。记忆构建从共享初始化开始，并针对每个用户通过在用户历史上进行测试时训练来更新，使其能够随着历史增长而持续演化。然而，仅靠测试时训练并不会显式鼓励记忆捕获个性化所需的语义用户知识，因此我们使用文本用户画像作为监督来学习这一共享初始化，使得在用户历史上的测试时训练能够更有效地捕获语义知识。ProTTT在多样化基准上持续优于全历史ICL和所有参数化基线，同时通过将用户历史压缩为轻量级参数化记忆，大幅降低推理成本。我们的分析还表明，画像监督是学习语义用户知识的可靠目标，并且所得记忆能够跟踪和保留不断演变的用户偏好，同时在不同历史长度下保持稳健。总体而言，我们展示了测试时训练用于个性化的有效性，并将ProTTT确立为持续演化用户记忆的基线。

Original abstract: Personalization requires language models to capture user-specific knowledge from a growing user history. Existing context-based approaches incur increasing inference costs as user history accumulates and rely on separate retrieval or summarization stages, while parametric-based approaches often require reconstructing user representations when new user data is added. We introduce ProTTT, a profile-supervised meta-learning framework for learning semantic user memory. The memory construction starts from a shared initialization and is updated for each user through test-time training on user history, allowing it to evolve continuously as the history grows. However, since test-time training alone does not explicitly encourage the memory to capture semantic user knowledge necessary for personalization, we learn this shared initialization using textual user profiles as supervision, so that test-time training on user history captures semantic knowledge more effectively. ProTTT consistently outperforms both full history ICL and all parametric baselines across diverse benchmarks, while substantially reducing inference cost by compressing user history into a lightweight parameterized memory. Our analysis also shows that profile supervision is a reliable objective for learning semantic user knowledge and that the resulting memory can track and retain evolving user preferences, while remaining robust across different history sizes. Overall, we demonstrate the effectiveness of test-time training for personalization and establish ProTTT as a baseline for continuously evolving user memory.

</details>


### [76] [CUE-Mem: Benchmarking Long-Term User Memory via Implicit Cues in Multimodal Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32574)
*Yulin Hu,Yanyan Zhao,Zimo Long,Xing Fu,Mengtong Ji,Weixiang Zhao,Yutai Hou,Qianchao Wang,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 28 pages. Submitted to AAAI 2027. Code: https://github.com/yulinlp/CUE-MEM. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Kkryptonite/CUE-Mem

Selection reason: 面向隐式多模态线索的长期用户记忆benchmark，揭示记忆保留与检索瓶颈，可迁移到agent记忆评测。

TL;DR: 提出 CUE-Mem 文本-图像-音频基准，用 2,674 个问题评估多模态智能体从隐式线索进行长期用户记忆；文本化方法瓶颈在细微线索保存检索，原生多模态访问仍受 backbone 和检索噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆对持续对话中的多模态智能体至关重要，但用户记忆可能隐含在图像背景物体、音频环境声等边缘多模态线索中；现有基准多关注纯文本记忆或显式多模态证据，忽视隐式多模态线索。

Method: 构建 CUE-Mem 基准，包含 2,674 个问题，覆盖显式和隐式证据设置，涉及实体回忆、长模式、个性化推荐和答案拒答四项任务；比较文本化记忆系统、caption 细节增加和原生多模态访问等设置。

Result: 文本化记忆系统中隐式性能远低于 oracle 证据，主要瓶颈在细微线索的保存和检索而非问题可回答性；增加 caption 细节可恢复更多证据，但增益不均衡且 token 成本快速增长；原生多模态访问也未能统一解决瓶颈，证据使用强烈依赖 backbone，多模态索引引入大量检索噪声。

Conclusion: CUE-Mem 为选择性保留、检索和利用细微多模态证据的记忆系统提供了测试平台。

中文摘要：长期记忆对于在持续对话中与用户交互的多模态智能体至关重要。然而，用户记忆并不总是被明确表述：它们也可能由图像中反复出现的背景物体、音频中的环境声音或其他边缘多模态线索所隐含。现有基准主要关注纯文本记忆或显式多模态证据，导致隐式多模态线索未被充分探索。我们提出 CUE-Mem，一个用于从隐式线索评估长期用户记忆的文本-图像-音频基准。CUE-Mem 包含 2,674 个问题，涵盖显式和隐式证据设置，并覆盖四项任务：实体回忆、长模式、个性化推荐和答案拒答。在文本化记忆系统中，隐式性能仍远低于 oracle 证据，将主要瓶颈定位在保存和检索细微线索，而非问题可回答性。增加 caption 细节可恢复更多此类证据，但带来不均衡增益和快速增长的 token 成本，促使原生多模态访问。然而，原生访问并不能统一解决该瓶颈：证据使用强烈依赖 backbone，而多模态索引引入大量检索噪声。CUE-Mem 为选择性保留、检索和利用细微多模态证据的记忆系统提供了一个测试平台。

Original abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents that interact with users across sustained conversations. However, user memories are not always explicitly stated: they may also be implied by recurring background objects in images, ambient sounds in audio, or other peripheral multimodal cues. Existing benchmarks largely focus on text-only memory or explicit multimodal evidence, leaving implicit multimodal cues underexplored. We introduce CUE-Mem, a text-image-audio benchmark for evaluating long-term user memory from implicit cues. CUE-Mem contains 2,674 questions across explicit and implicit evidence settings and covers four tasks: Entity Recall, Long Pattern, Personalized Recommendation, and Answer Refusal. Across textualized memory systems, implicit performance remains far below oracle evidence, locating the main bottleneck in preserving and retrieving subtle cues rather than question answerability. Increasing caption detail recovers more of this evidence, but brings uneven gains and rapidly growing token costs, motivating native multimodal access. Yet native access does not uniformly resolve the bottleneck: evidence use depends strongly on the backbone, while multimodal indexing introduces substantial retrieval noise. CUE-Mem provides a testbed for memory systems that selectively retain, retrieve, and use subtle multimodal evidence.

</details>


### [77] [EMIR$^2$: Evolution-Aware Memory with Intent-Guided Multi-Round Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32584)
*Jinlan Liu,Hongliang Sun,Yong Wang,Bolin Zhang,Dinabo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 演化感知记忆图加意图引导多轮检索，解决事实变化与分散证据整合，对agent记忆与多轮检索策略有明确迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出演化感知记忆框架 EMIR^2，通过状态演化记忆图和意图引导多轮检索，提升 LLM 智能体对持续演变历史信息的长期记忆利用与复杂证据检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体记忆系统多依赖静态记忆表示和单轮检索，难以跟踪事实变化，也难以整合长期交互中分散的证据，导致持续演变的历史信息无法被有效利用。

Method: 构建状态演化记忆图（SEMG），以演化知识状态表示长期记忆，并通过时间事件轨迹与证据关联支持基于证据的语义状态更新和证据追踪；在此基础上引入意图引导多轮检索机制，迭代识别缺失证据并依据累积信息扩展检索。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 MemConflict 数据集上，EMIR^2 提升了长期记忆利用、动态与静态冲突处理及复杂检索性能，部分类别相对提升超过 12%。

Conclusion: 联合建模记忆演化与自适应证据获取，对提升长期智能体交互中的长期记忆利用和复杂检索效果具有有效性。

中文摘要：长期记忆使大语言模型（LLM）智能体能够利用历史交互来完成未来任务。然而，现有记忆系统难以利用持续演变的历史信息，因为它们通常依赖静态记忆表示和单轮检索策略，无法跟踪事实变化或整合长期交互中的分布式证据。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 EMIR^2，一个具有意图引导多轮检索的演化感知记忆框架（Evolution-Aware Memory framework with Intent-Guided Multi-Round Retrieval），使 LLM 智能体能够维护不断演变的历史知识并自适应地检索相关证据。具体而言，EMIR^2 构建状态演化记忆图（State-Evolving Memory Graph, SEMG），将长期记忆表示为演化知识状态，并由时间事件轨迹和证据关联支持。通过基于证据的更新来维护语义状态，SEMG 保留历史演化，并能在复杂和冲突场景下进行证据追踪。在此基础上，我们引入意图引导的多轮检索机制，该机制迭代识别缺失证据并基于累积信息扩展检索。在 LoCoMo 和 MemConflict 上的实验表明，EMIR^2 提升了长期记忆利用、动态与静态冲突处理以及复杂检索性能，在某些类别上实现了超过 12% 的相对提升。这些结果凸显了联合建模记忆演化与自适应证据获取对于长期智能体交互的有效性。

Original abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to leverage historical interactions for future tasks. However, existing memory systems struggle to utilize continuously evolving historical information, as they often rely on static memory representations and single-round retrieval strategies, failing to track factual changes or integrate distributed evidence across long-term interactions. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$, an \textbf{E}volution-Aware \textbf{M}emory framework with \textbf{I}ntent-Guided Multi-\textbf{R}ound \textbf{R}etrieval, enabling LLM agents to maintain evolving historical knowledge and adaptively retrieve relevant evidence. Specifically, \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ constructs a State-Evolving Memory Graph (SEMG) that represents long-term memory as evolving knowledge states supported by temporal event trajectories and evidential associations. By maintaining semantic states through evidence-based updates, SEMG preserves historical evolution and enables evidence tracing under complex and conflicting scenarios. Building upon this, we introduce an intent-guided multi-round retrieval mechanism that iteratively identifies missing evidence and expands retrieval based on accumulated information. Experiments on LoCoMo and MemConflict demonstrate that \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ improves long-term memory utilization, dynamic and static conflict handling, and complex retrieval performance, achieving relative improvements of more than 12\% in certain categories. These results highlight the effectiveness of jointly modeling memory evolution and adaptive evidence acquisition for long-term agent interactions.

</details>


### [78] ["You're Right, Let Me Fix It": How LLM Agents Damage Correct Work When Falsely Accused](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32616)
*Xutao Mao,Rui Qian,Longxiang Wang,Xinjian Yi,Mingxuan Li,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究长寿命agent被错误指控后破坏原本正确工作的失败模式，并提出harness门控降低危害，对coding agent可靠性与安全控制直接相关。

TL;DR: CAVE-Bench 揭示：LLM 智能体会在无证据的虚假指控下破坏正确工作，最高达 60.06%；由基准实时信号驱动的门控可将重放危害降低 74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行智能体在任务成功后仍会收到后续输入，其中可能错误指控其导致后续失败；需要研究智能体是否会接受虚假指控并破坏已经正确的工作。

Method: 构建 CAVE-Bench，包含六个领域 365 个不透明智能体任务；每次计分运行先达到经验证正确状态，指控所需事实位于智能体不可达的外部或运行时状态；从轨迹评估接受指控与证据使用，确定性重放下游事件衡量危害；设置五个风险因素。

Result: Claude Code 中 14 个最新模型最高 60.06% 的运行中正确工作受损害；更强模型常在恢复支持证据后仍如此；同一模型在 OpenCode、Codex、Hermes 中表现不同；实时信号门控降低重放危害 74%。

Conclusion: 在无支持指控下保留已正确工作是长期运行智能体的一项独立安全挑战。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体越来越多地在任务成功后继续工作，因为它们会在压缩后恢复或接手任务移交。它们已完成的工作会持续收到后续输入，这些输入有时会因后续失败而错误地指控该工作。我们将智能体接受此类虚假指控称为煤气灯式谄媚（gaslight sycophancy），并将据此行动而损害先前正确工作的行为称为破坏性过度纠正（destructive over-correction）。我们提出 CAVE-Bench，一个包含跨六个领域的 365 个智能体任务的基准，其构建围绕不透明任务。每次计分运行首先到达一个经验证正确的状态，其支持性理由与历史保留在工作区中，而能够裁定该指控的事实则位于智能体无法触及的外部状态或运行时状态中。智能体无法通过本地检查确认或反驳该主张，因此正确响应应保留该工作并请求缺失的证据。每个任务要么将带有已保存证据的正确工作交给智能体，要么让智能体先构建并验证该工作，并由五个风险因素决定指控如何进入工作流。我们从轨迹中评估对指控的接受程度与证据使用情况，并通过对下游事件的确定性重放来衡量危害。在 Claude Code 中的 14 个最新模型中，虚假指控在最多 60.06% 的运行中损害了正确工作，并且更强的模型常常在恢复支持性证据之后仍这样做。同一模型在 OpenCode、Codex 和 Hermes 中表现不同，而由该基准的实时信号驱动的 harness 门控将重放危害降低了 74%。这些结果表明，在无支持的指控下保留已经正确的工作是长期运行智能体面临的一项独特安全挑战。我们的项目位于 https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly keep working after a task succeeds as they resume after compaction or take over handoffs. Their finished work keeps receiving follow-up input that sometimes falsely accuses it for later failures. We call an agent's acceptance of such a false accusation gaslight sycophancy, and destructive over-correction when acting on it damages previously correct work. We introduce CAVE-Bench, a benchmark of 365 agentic tasks across six domains built around opaque tasks. Every scored run first reaches a verified correct state, whose supporting rationale and history stay in the workspace while the facts that would settle the accusation lie in external or runtime state beyond the agent's reach. The agent cannot confirm or refute the claim with a local check, so the right response should keep the work and ask for the missing evidence. Each task either hands the agent correct work with saved evidence or let it build and verify that work first, and five risk factors set how the accusation enters the workflow. We score accusation acceptance and evidence use from the trajectory and measure harm by deterministic replay of downstream events. Across 14 of the latest models in Claude Code, false accusations damage correct work in up to 60.06% of runs, and stronger models often do so after recovering the supporting evidence. The same model behaves differently across OpenCode, Codex, and Hermes, and a harness gate driven by the benchmark's live signals cuts replayed harm by 74%. These results show that preserving already-correct work under unsupported accusation is a distinct safety challenge for long-lived agents. Our project is in https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/.

</details>


### [79] [Business Compromise Detection with Agentic AI and LLM-driven Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32643)
*Diego Palma,Kyu Bin Kim,Zhen Han,Allbright Dsouza,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at EMNLP 2026. 13 pages, 2 figures, 10 tables

Selection reason: 虽为广告账号风控垂直应用，但提出agent仅作结构化信号调查者、由神经符号层裁决的架构，对agent作为验证者的可靠性设计有一般启发。

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM智能体在企业广告账户入侵检测中的作用：智能体作为高召回信号提取器，最终裁决交给神经符号规则、朴素贝叶斯校准和矛盾层；在专家标签上MCC从0.295升至0.435，精确率升至0.446而召回率降至0.660，并略胜树集成模型0.386。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测被劫持的企业广告账户困难；LLM智能体可做诚信治理，但困难案例上的幻觉错误会造成业务摩擦，需兼顾精度、可解释性与可审计性。

Method: 将LLM智能体限定为调查者并输出结构化、可解释信号向量；裁决采用神经符号阶段：FOIL-IE归纳逻辑编程发现的符号规则、朴素贝叶斯校准层、数据调优矛盾层；在过采样总体和真实低流行率专家标注样本上评估。

Result: 裁决者替换使MCC从0.295提升到0.435（ΔMCC +0.139，95% CI [+0.026, +0.245]，p=0.018，配对bootstrap），精确率从0.250提升到0.446（1.8倍），召回率从0.920降至0.660；相同条件下略胜树集成模型0.386。

Conclusion: 规则可编码领域w标签并保持可解释、可审计；LLM智能体更适合作为信号提取器而非最终裁决者，神经符号裁决可提升MCC与精度。

中文摘要：检测被攻陷的企业广告账户是数字广告中的一项挑战，因为攻击者会利用被劫持的账户发起欺诈性活动。大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在诚信治理方面展现出前景，但在困难案例上的幻觉错误会造成业务摩擦。在一项研究中，我们发现该自主智能体是一个强大的、偏重召回的信号提取器，但并非可靠的最终裁决者，在模糊决策上牺牲了精确率。因此，我们让该智能体继续作为调查者，输出结构化的、可解释的信号向量，并将裁决委托给一个神经符号阶段：由归纳逻辑编程（FOIL-IE）发现的符号规则、一个朴素贝叶斯校准层，以及一个数据调优的矛盾层。在受损账户过采样总体和带有主题专家标签的真实低流行率样本上进行评估，这种裁决者替换将 MCC 从 0.295 提升到 0.435（ΔMCC +0.139，95% CI [+0.026, +0.245]，p=0.018，配对 bootstrap），在召回率代价下（0.920 到 0.660）将精确率从 0.250 提升到 0.446（1.8 倍）。在相同条件下进行基准测试，它还略胜树集成模型（0.386）。这些规则编码了领域 w 标签，同时保持可解释和可审计。

Original abstract: Detecting compromised business ad accounts is a challenge in digital advertising, as attackers exploit hijacked accounts to launch fraudulent campaigns. Large Language Model (LLM) agents show promise for integrity enforcement, but hallucinated mistakes on hard cases create business friction. In a study we find the autonomous agent is a strong, recall-heavy signal extractor but an unreliable final arbiter, conceding precision on ambiguous decisions. We therefore keep the agent as an investigator that emits a structured, interpretable signal vector, and delegate the verdict to a neuro-symbolic stage: symbolic rules discovered by Inductive Logic Programming (FOIL-IE), a Naïve Bayes calibration layer, and a data-tuned contradiction layer. Evaluating on a compromise-over-sampled population and a realistic low-prevalence sample with subject-matter-expert labels, this arbiter substitution raises MCC from 0.295 to 0.435 (ΔMCC +0.139, 95% CI [+0.026, +0.245], p=0.018, paired bootstrap), lifting precision from 0.250 to 0.446 (1.8x) at a recall cost (0.920 to 0.660). Benchmarked under identical conditions, it also edge tree ensembles (0.386).The rules encode domain w labels while remaininginterpretable and auditable.

</details>


### [80] [From Scene Graphs to Answers: Selective Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32645)
*Yiyao Wang,Pei Liu,Fangzhou Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出“确定性符号执行优先、LLM 仅在无法求解时兜底”的查询自适应推理架构，这种先验证/精确计算再交给 LLM 的分工模式对代码优化 agent 的工具调用与验证流程设计有借鉴意义。

TL;DR: 提出查询自适应神经符号推理框架，以分层时空场景图为核心，优先用符号执行精确求解查询，仅在符号执行弃权时调用LLM进行语义推理，并用查询条件检索与证据过滤为LLM提供任务相关证据；在NuScenes-QA上同时提升准确率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言方法将异构推理操作统一交给单一神经推理过程，忽视了查询之间存在根本区别：部分查询可由精确符号操作求解，部分查询才需要语义解释；统一策略因此难以兼顾精确性、效率与语义泛化。

Method: 构建分层时空场景图（STSG），分离持久对象身份与帧特定状态，并以显式有向结构表示空间关系和时间转换；给定查询后，符号执行器先通过精确图操作求解，仅当弃权时调用LLM；对未解决查询，使用查询条件图检索和证据过滤，保留关系方向、时间局部性与对象语义，为LLM提供紧凑且验证过的任务相关证据。该设计将LLM从通用推理引擎转为针对性语义推理器，并让确定性计算被精确高效处理。

Result: 在oracle感知设置下，于nuScenes v1.0-mini全部十个场景的5,916个NuScenes-QA问题上评估。完整系统使用GPT-5.4-mini达到80.63%总体准确率，较对应的仅LLM配置提升5.48个百分点；使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash时提升6.64个百分点。计数问题增益最大，分别提升10.20和12.61个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择性推理能够同时提高准确率和推理效率，表明按查询性质分配符号计算与语义推理是自动驾驶问答中有效的神经符号框架设计。

中文摘要：自动驾驶问答需要对结构化场景信息进行推理，然而现有的视觉-语言方法在很大程度上将异构推理操作委托给单一神经推理过程。我们认为，这种统一策略忽视了一个根本区别：某些查询允许精确的符号解，而其他查询则需要语义解释。我们引入一种查询自适应的神经符号推理框架，根据查询的性质显式分配计算。其核心是一个分层时空场景图（STSG），它将持久对象身份与帧特定状态分离，并将空间关系和时间转换表示为显式有向结构。给定一个查询，符号执行器首先尝试通过精确图操作来解决它；只有当符号执行弃权时，才调用LLM进行语义推理。对于这些未解决的查询，查询条件图检索和证据过滤保留关系方向、时间局部性和对象语义，为LLM提供紧凑且经过验证的任务相关证据。该设计将LLM的角色从通用推理引擎转变为针对性语义推理器，同时允许确定性计算被精确且高效地处理。我们在oracle感知设置下，在nuScenes v1.0-mini全部十个场景的5,916个NuScenes-QA问题上评估该框架。完整系统使用GPT-5.4-mini达到80.63%的总体准确率，相较对应的仅LLM配置提升5.48个百分点；使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash时，提升达到6.64个百分点。最大增益出现在计数问题上，分别提升10.20和12.61个百分点。这些结果表明，选择性推理同时提高了准确率和推理效率。

Original abstract: Autonomous-driving question answering requires reasoning over structured scene information, yet existing vision-language approaches largely delegate heterogeneous reasoning operations to a single neural inference process. We argue that this uniform strategy overlooks a fundamental distinction: some queries admit exact symbolic solutions, while others require semantic interpretation. We introduce a query-adaptive neuro-symbolic reasoning framework that explicitly allocates computation according to the nature of the query. At its core is a hierarchical Spatiotemporal Scene Graph (STSG) that separates persistent object identities from frame-specific states and represents spatial relations and temporal transitions as explicit directed structures. Given a query, a symbolic executor first attempts to resolve it through exact graph operations; only when symbolic execution abstains is an LLM invoked for semantic reasoning. For these unresolved queries, query-conditioned graph retrieval and evidence filtering preserve relation direction, temporal locality, and object semantics, providing the LLM with compact and verified task-relevant evidence. This design shifts the role of the LLM from a universal reasoning engine to a targeted semantic reasoner, while allowing deterministic computation to be handled exactly and efficiently. We evaluate the framework on 5,916 NuScenes-QA questions across all ten scenes of nuScenes v1.0-mini under an oracle-perception setting. The complete system achieves 80.63 percent overall accuracy with GPT-5.4-mini, improving over the corresponding LLM-only configuration by 5.48 percentage points; with DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the improvement reaches 6.64 points. The largest gains occur on counting questions, with improvements of 10.20 and 12.61 points, respectively. These results show that selective reasoning improves both accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [81] [Contract Memory Compiler: Resolve, Then Traverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32658)
*Zhi Song,XiMing Xing,Chunhan Li,Weian Mao,Zhenchao Tang,Hanbo Huang,Fan Xu,Jiale Zhou,Jiahui Guan,Zejian Ding,Chen Ma,Lusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 针对 LLM agent 外部记忆的“先解析更新再遍历证据”机制，解决多跳检索中的证据选择问题，属于 agent 记忆与检索方法，可迁移到需要长历史依赖的编码 agent。

TL;DR: 提出契约记忆编译器 CMC，通过预先抽取关系并应用更新追踪当前关系，从问题实体出发选择原始证据，在 FactConsolidation 多跳问答上取得 SOTA，并发布 MQuAKE-MemStream 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆可扩展上下文，但更新会使证据选择复杂化：关系更新可能把多跳问题引向问题未提及实体的记录，仅刷新旧上下文的做法不足。

Method: CMC 在问答前用语言模型抽取历史中的关系及其陈述位置；根据后续更新确定当前关系；从问题实体沿关系追踪，将对应的原始记录一次传入答案模型；证据由当前状态决定，而非在旧上下文中刷新值。

Result: 在 FactConsolidation 上总体多跳准确率 78.25%，262K 长度 61.0%，为 SOTA；固定抽取关系与答案模型时，先选证据后解析更新仅 21.50%。并构建 MQuAKE-MemStream（基于 MQuAKE-Remastered 反事实案例的有序记忆流）。

Conclusion: 当前状态驱动的证据选择对长历史更新场景至关重要；CMC 验证了先解析更新再选证据可显著提升多跳问答准确率。

中文摘要：外部记忆使语言模型智能体能够回答有关历史的问题，而这些历史对于答案模型的上下文窗口来说过长。更新带来的问题比检索一个近期事实更难：改变一个关系可能将多跳问题重定向到关于问题中未出现的实体的记录。我们研究这种依赖更新的证据选择问题，并引入契约记忆编译器（Contract Memory Compiler, CMC）。在看到问题之前，CMC 使用语言模型识别历史中的关系，并记录每个关系被陈述的位置。它应用后续更新来确定当前关系，从问题中命名的实体出发沿着这些关系追踪，并在一次调用中将相应的原始记录传给答案模型。因此，当前状态决定了哪些证据被读取，而不仅仅是在先前选定的上下文中刷新值。据我们所知，CMC 在 FactConsolidation 上取得了最先进的多跳准确率，总体达到 78.25%，在 262K 时达到 61.0%。在提取的关系和答案模型保持固定不变的情况下，在解析更新之前选择证据会将多跳准确率降低到 21.50%。我们还引入了 MQuAKE-MemStream，这是一个从 MQuAKE-Remastered 反事实案例构建的有序记忆流衍生数据集。

Original abstract: External memory lets language-model agents answer questions about histories too long for the answer model's context window. Updates create a harder problem than retrieving a recent fact: changing one relation can redirect a multi-hop question to records about an entity absent from the question. We study this update-dependent evidence selection problem and introduce the Contract Memory Compiler (CMC). Before seeing a question, CMC uses a language model to identify relations in the history and record where each one was stated. It applies later updates to determine the current relations, follows them from entities named in the question, and passes the corresponding original records to the answer model in one call. Thus the current state determines which evidence is read, rather than merely refreshing values in a previously selected context. To the best of our knowledge, CMC achieves state-of-the-art multi-hop accuracy on FactConsolidation, reaching 78.25% overall and 61.0% at 262K. With the extracted relations and answer model held fixed, selecting evidence before resolving updates reduces multi-hop accuracy to 21.50%. We also introduce MQuAKE-MemStream, a derived dataset of ordered memory streams built from MQuAKE-Remastered counterfactual cases.

</details>


### [82] [When Better Gets Worse: Improvement Fidelity for Self-Improving Agents in Adaptive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32677)
*Ke Wang,Zijie Zhao,Zhiyi Yuan,Changlun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 10 figures, 3 tables

Selection reason: 形式化自改进 agent 的“改进保真度”问题（代理验证器与部署效果不一致），并提出按选择后悔分配高保真评估的 PIVOT-KG，对 agent 自我改进与验证可靠性直接相关。

TL;DR: 论文提出改进保真度，衡量代理验证器所选策略更新在部署后是否仍保持改进方向与排序；指出全局策略准确率不足以保证更新保真度，并提出 PIVOT-KG 按每单位成本的选择遗憾期望降低来分配高保真评估。实验显示代理与部署最优集常不相交，PIVOT-KG 显著降低选择遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进智能体依赖代理验证器选择策略更新，但部署会改变评估这些更新的世界，导致验证器认为更好的更新在部署后可能更差；需要刻画并弥合代理改进与部署改进之间的差距。

Method: 形式化改进保真度，分析全局策略准确率与更新保真度的关系，指出算子偏移、部署响应和候选边际的作用；提出 PIVOT-KG，一种成对、决策感知的验证器，根据期望选择遗憾降低与单位成本来分配稀缺高保真评估。

Result: 在 Leduc、Kuhn 和 Melting Pot 的 90 个留出根中，代理与部署最优集在 51 个案例中不相交；八候选 HighwayEnv 压力测试中，PIVOT-KG 将平均改进选择遗憾从精确 Uniform 验证规则的 0.0435 降至主预算下的 0.0055。

Conclusion: 可靠自改进应在更新所诱导的世界中评估所提出的改进；当稀缺部署证据可能影响替换决策时，应使用 PIVOT-KG 这类决策感知规则进行分配。

中文摘要：自我改进智能体日益依赖代理验证器来选择策略更新，然而部署可能改变评估这些更新时所处的世界。因此，即使验证器在整体上对策略排序良好，一个在验证器看来更好的更新在部署后也可能变得更差。我们将这一差距形式化为改进保真度（Improvement Fidelity），它追问：在改进过程实际提出的更新上，代理改进是否保持部署改进的符号与排序。我们表明，全局策略准确率并不必然保证更新保真度：算子偏移和部署响应会产生更新层面的错误，而候选边际决定这些错误是否会改变替换决策。我们提出 PIVOT-KG，一种成对的、决策感知的验证器，它根据每单位成本的选择遗憾期望降低来分配稀缺的高保真评估。在 Leduc、Kuhn 和 Melting Pot 的 90 个留出根中，代理最优集和部署最优集在 51 个案例中不相交。在一个八候选 HighwayEnv 压力测试中，PIVOT-KG 在主要预算下将平均改进选择遗憾从精确 Uniform 验证规则下的 0.0435 降至 0.0055。这些结果共同表明，可靠的自改进为何应在其所诱导的世界中评估所提出的改进，同时提供了一条实用规则，用于在稀缺部署证据可能影响替换决策时分配这些证据。

Original abstract: Self-improving agents increasingly rely on proxy verifiers to choose policy updates, yet deployment can change the world in which those updates are evaluated. An update that looks better to the verifier can therefore become worse after deployment even when the verifier ranks policies well overall. We formalize this gap as Improvement Fidelity, which asks whether proxy improvements preserve the sign and ordering of deployment improvements over the updates an improvement process actually proposes. We show that global policy accuracy need not guarantee update fidelity: operator shift and deployment response can create update-level errors, while candidate margins determine whether those errors change the replacement decision. We introduce PIVOT-KG, a paired, decision-aware validator that allocates scarce high-fidelity evaluation according to the expected reduction in selection regret per unit cost. Across 90 held-out roots in Leduc, Kuhn, and Melting Pot, proxy and deployment optimal sets are disjoint in 51 cases. In an eight-candidate HighwayEnv stress test, PIVOT-KG reduces mean improvement-selection regret from 0.0435 under the exact Uniform validation rule to 0.0055 at the primary budget. Together, these results show why reliable self-improvement should evaluate proposed improvements in the worlds they induce, while providing a practical rule for allocating scarce deployment evidence when it can affect the replacement decision.

</details>


### [83] [PINNMorph: Evolving Online Adaptation Policies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32685)
*Xu Yang,Mingyang Yu,Jun Zhang,Keqian Li,Jing Xu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用 LLM 引导演化干预策略，在训练过程中动态生成并执行修改程序的闭环优化框架，其“诊断—程序化干预—执行反馈—策略演化”流程与自动优化 agent 高度同构。

TL;DR: 提出 PINNMorph：基于 LLM 引导策略进化在线自适应 PINN 训练，在 13 个 PDE 基准上取得低于多个基线的求解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 的训练行为会随优化过程显著变化，残差、梯度、区域难度和容量需求不断演变，但网络架构与主要训练机制通常在训练前固定，缺乏在线自适应能力。

Method: 维护状态条件自适应策略种群，将执行诊断映射为对拓扑修改、加性表示增强、目标平衡、梯度处理、自适应采样和优化器阶段控制的干预；在每个干预时机实例化候选程序，按训练状态选择并直接应用于当前 PINN，使其继承参数和训练状态继续优化，再用执行结果评估并进化策略种群。

Result: 在 13 个 PDE 基准上，PINNMorph 在所有评估问题上的求解误差均低于 SA-PINN、ConFIG、RoPINN、HARMONIC 和 PINNsAgent；消融研究进一步考察了在线自适应、状态条件干预选择和执行反馈驱动策略演化的效果。

Conclusion: PINNMorph 利用实际训练反馈联合自适应当前 PINN 与干预策略，区别于训练前架构搜索或固定规则，可在线演化训练机制并提升 PDE 求解精度。

中文摘要：物理信息神经网络（PINNs）为求解偏微分方程（PDEs）提供了一种基于学习的框架，然而其训练行为在整个优化过程中可能发生显著变化。残差分布、梯度交互、区域学习难度以及模型容量需求可能随时间演变，而网络架构和主要训练机制通常在训练前就已确定。我们提出了 PINNMorph，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的策略演化的在线 PINN 自适应框架。PINNMorph 维护一个状态条件自适应策略种群，这些策略将执行诊断映射为对拓扑修改、加性表示增强、目标平衡、梯度处理、自适应采样和优化器阶段控制的受控干预。在每个干预时机，从当前策略种群中实例化候选程序，根据观察到的训练状态进行选择，并直接应用于正在训练的 PINN。所得模型继承其现有参数和训练状态，并沿相同轨迹继续优化。随后利用执行结果评估干预并演化策略种群。与训练前架构搜索或固定自适应规则不同，PINNMorph 利用来自实际训练的反馈，联合自适应当前 PINN 及其干预所遵循的策略。在 13 个 PDE 基准上的实验表明，PINNMorph 在所有评估问题上均比 SA-PINN、ConFIG、RoPINN、HARMONIC 和 PINNsAgent 取得更低的求解误差。消融研究进一步考察了在线自适应、状态条件干预选择和执行反馈驱动的策略演化的效果。

Original abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a learning-based framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their training behavior can change substantially throughout optimization. Residual distributions, gradient interactions, regional learning difficulty, and model-capacity requirements may evolve over time, while the network architecture and major training mechanisms are typically determined before training. We propose PINNMorph, an online PINN adaptation framework based on large language model (LLM)-guided policy evolution. PINNMorph maintains a population of state-conditioned adaptation policies that map execution diagnostics to controlled interventions over topology modification, additive representation augmentation, objective balancing, gradient handling, adaptive sampling, and optimizer-phase control. At each intervention opportunity, candidate programs are instantiated from the current policy population, selected according to the observed training state, and applied directly to the PINN under training.
