The resulting model inherits its existing parameters and training state and continues optimization along the same trajectory. Execution outcomes are subsequently used to evaluate interventions and evolve the policy population. Unlike pre-training architecture search or fixed adaptation rules, PINNMorph jointly adapts the current PINN and the policies governing its interventions using feedback from actual training. Experiments on 13 PDE benchmarks show that PINNMorph achieves lower solution errors than SA-PINN, ConFIG, RoPINN, HARMONIC, and PINNsAgent across all evaluated problems. Ablation studies further examine the effects of online adaptation, state-conditioned intervention selection, and execution-feedback-driven policy evolution.

</details>


### [84] [Dude, Where's My State? Execution Information Requirements for Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32687)
*Nikita Mehrotra,Ashish Tiwari,Priyanshu Gupta,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 35 pages, 12 figures, 15 tables. Supplementary material included

Selection reason: 研究有状态长程 agent 的信息保留与恢复需求，提出 EIR 度量与 VESTIGE 轨迹分析方法，并基于 7 万余条软件 agent 轨迹给出失败模式结论，对长程编码 agent 的上下文/状态管理很有价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出 EIR 与 LACUNA、VESTIGE，用于评估长期运行智能体是否保留并恢复任务所需信息，并揭示存储、保留策略、错误传播和恢复不完整等失败因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行智能体依赖历史信息完成后续步骤，但信息需求、保留与恢复能力缺乏独立评估指标。

Method: 提出 EIR 下界；构建 LACUNA 生成已知依赖任务并单独变化信息需求、保留、恢复；构建 VESTIGE 从执行轨迹生成语义图并度量真实任务信息需求。

Result: 四模型上恢复缺失结果使受影响回忆步骤准确率达 100%，等长无关信息为 0%；充足存储仍可能失败；72,562 条轨迹中失败运行重读随距离下降更陡（RR 0.951/距离翻倍），调整后峰值需求本身与失败无关。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为评估智能体能否保留并恢复任务所需信息提供了框架与证据。

中文摘要：长时间运行的智能体必须保留后续步骤所依赖的信息。我们引入执行信息需求（Execution Information Requirement, EIR），它是在指定任务和访问条件下，为正确完成任务而必须保持可访问的信息的下界。我们开发了 LACUNA，一个生成具有已知依赖关系任务的框架，并将信息需求、保留和恢复与单个操作的难度分开变化。在四个模型中，恢复缺失结果可将受影响的回忆步骤的准确率提高到 100%，而等长的无关信息则为 0%。仅有足够的存储并不能确保成功：保留策略可能丢弃所需结果，错误可能通过后续计算传播，并且智能体可能在恢复完成之前停止。我们还引入 VESTIGE，它利用智能体执行轨迹构建语义图，并测量真实任务的信息需求。在 72,562 条软件智能体轨迹中，VESTIGE 揭示，失败运行中与解决方案相关的重读随距离增加而出现更陡峭的下降（距离每翻倍 RR 为 0.951），而仅调整后的峰值需求与失败无关。总之，这些贡献支持评估智能体是否保留并恢复其任务所需的信息。

Original abstract: Long-running agents must preserve information that later steps depend on. We introduce the Execution Information Requirement (EIR), a lower bound on the information that must remain accessible for correct completion under specified task and access conditions. We develop LACUNA, a framework that generates tasks with known dependencies and varies information demand, retention, and recovery separately from the difficulty of individual operations. Across four models, restoring a missing result raises accuracy on affected recall steps to 100%, compared with 0% for equal-length irrelevant information. Sufficient storage alone does not ensure success: retention policies can discard required results, errors can propagate through later computations, and agents can stop before recovery is complete. We also introduce VESTIGE, which uses agent execution traces to construct semantic graphs and measure information demand for real tasks. Across 72,562 software-agent trajectories, VESTIGE reveals a steeper distance-related decline in solution-relevant rereading for failed runs (RR 0.951 per distance doubling), while adjusted peak demand alone is not associated with failure. Together, these contributions support evaluating whether agents preserve and recover the information their tasks require.

</details>


### [85] [World Agent: Can Language Models Keep a World Running?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32692)
*Weixing Chen,Weipeng Zhang,Nan An,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出“世界持续运行”这一 agent 长程任务与配套 benchmark，考察状态维护与因果事件组织，可迁移为长程 agent 评测与因果一致性检查的范式。

TL;DR: 论文提出世界智能体任务与 WorldAgent-Benchmark，将世界生成评估从交付转向持续运行；基准含维护与推演两条赛道，分别检验事件落地为状态转移和部分观测下预测/行动；8个模型上移除预构建结构后分数持续下降，因果关系检查最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估止于生成、交付或单步转移，未检验交付后的持续运行；局部状态转移或中间结果正确，也不代表因果事件流组织正确。

Method: 提出世界智能体任务：模型不生成世界，而负责维持世界运行，协调事件并传递后果。构建 WorldAgent-Benchmark：维护赛道将连续叙事事件落地为受因果、时间、并发约束的显式状态转移，结合LLM辅助语义判断与程序化验证评分；推演赛道在部分观测下预测演化并朝目标行动，完全程序化评估。判断和分数可审计、可重算。

Result: 在8个模型上，随预构建结构移除，分数稳步下降；因果关系检查为所有模型最弱环节。

Conclusion: 该基准使世界持续运行可度量，区分局部完成与事件组织失败，并将发布代码与数据集。

中文摘要：世界模型正从生成逼真的帧转向生成可玩的世界，然而，一个被交付的世界能否持续运行，尚未在任何地方得到测试。现有评估止步于生成、交付或单步转移，且各自停在这条路径上的不同节点。正确的局部状态转移或中间结果并不能保证因果事件流被正确组织。我们提出世界智能体任务，将世界生成的评估点从交付时刻后移到其后的持续运行。在该任务中，模型不被要求生成一个世界，而是负责让这个世界持续运行，这需要协调事件并传递其后果以约束后续演化。我们在 WorldAgent-Benchmark 中实例化该任务，包含两个互补赛道。在维护赛道中，模型必须将连续叙事中的事件落地为显式世界状态的正确转移，同时遵守因果、时间和并发约束。在推演赛道中，模型必须在部分观测下预测世界将如何演化，并朝目标行动。维护赛道结合了大语言模型辅助的语义判断与程序化验证和评分，而推演赛道完全以程序化方式评估。个体判断可对照世界状态和执行日志进行审计，分数可从保存的判断和执行记录重新计算。在8个模型上，随着预构建结构从世界中被移除，分数稳步下降，而因果关系检查是每个模型最弱的组成部分。该基准使世界的持续运行变得可度量，并区分局部完成与事件组织中的失败。代码和数据集将在 https://github.com/HCPLab-SYSU/WorldAgent-Benchmark 发布。

Original abstract: World models are moving from generating realistic frames to generating playable worlds, yet whether a delivered world can keep running is not tested anywhere. Existing evaluations stop at generation, at delivery, or at single-step transitions, and each stops at a different point along the way. Correct local state transitions or intermediate outcomes do not guarantee a correctly organized causal event flow. We propose the world agent task, which moves the evaluation point of world generation from the moment of delivery to the continued operation that follows. In this task, a model is not asked to generate a world. It is held responsible for keeping the world running, which requires coordinating events and carrying forward their consequences to constrain subsequent evolution. We instantiate the task in WorldAgent-Benchmark with two complementary tracks. In the maintenance track, the model must ground the events of a continuous narrative into correct transitions of the explicit world state while respecting causal, temporal, and concurrency constraints. In the deduction track, the model must predict how the world will evolve under partial observations and act toward a goal. The maintenance track combines LLM-assisted semantic judgments with programmatic validation and scoring, while the deduction track is evaluated entirely programmatically. Individual judgments are auditable against world states and execution logs, and scores can be recomputed from the saved judgments and execution records. Across 8 models, scores decline steadily as pre-built structure is removed from the world, and causal-relation checking is the weakest component for every model. The benchmark makes the continued operation of a world measurable and distinguishes local completion from failures in event organization. Code and dataset will be released on https://github.com/HCPLab-SYSU/WorldAgent-Benchmark.

</details>


### [86] [IGSD: Environment-Verified Hindsight Self-Distillation for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32694)
*Angqing Jiang,Gaoming Zhang,Chaoqun Zhang,Jianchun Song,Liyuan Kong,Kena Qi,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向检索型 agent 的环境验证式自蒸馏，用真实检索反馈校验教师建议的动作级监督，属于带工具反馈与验证的 agent 训练方法，可迁移到代码优化中的动作级奖励设计。

TL;DR: 提出信息增益门控自蒸馏 IGSD，用环境反馈验证教师后见查询提议，并将执行后的配对信息增益作为软权重蒸馏，从而为搜索智能体提供可靠的动作级监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体的在策略自蒸馏中，后见信息可能使教师偏好无法从学生当前状态改善检索的查询；现有直接蒸馏或模型内部过滤方法均未验证查询实际执行后的检索效果。

Method: IGSD 将每个查询 token 视为微动作，把教师提议与学生采样补全为匹配查询，并在相同失败状态和相同检索器下执行；通过共享反事实控制计算执行配对信息增益，以仅正向软权重指导候选对蒸馏，保持 GRPO 目标不变且仅在训练时验证。

Result: 在七个单跳和多跳 QA 基准上，IGSD 以 3B 和 7B 策略分别取得 42.8% 和 47.0% 的宏平均精确匹配准确率，且无需推理时验证。

Conclusion: 结果表明，环境验证的后见信息是面向搜索智能体实现可靠动作级监督的有效途径。

中文摘要：在策略自蒸馏无需外部教师即可密集化智能体训练：一个以特权后见信息为条件的策略为其自身非特权 rollout 提供步骤级指导。然而，对于搜索智能体，后见信息可能使教师偏好一个无法从学生状态提升检索的查询。现有方法要么直接蒸馏该偏好，要么用模型内部分数过滤它，但两种策略都未验证查询实际执行的检索后果。我们提出信息增益门控自蒸馏（Information-Gain-Gated Self-Distillation, IGSD），其在蒸馏前用环境反馈验证在策略 token 提议。将每个查询 token 视为微动作，IGSD 将教师的 token 提议和学生的采样 token 补全为匹配的查询，并在相同失败状态下用相同检索器执行二者。共享反事实控制项解释了答案似然中依赖查询的变化，因此它们的差值，即执行配对信息增益，为检索到的文档提供相对效用对比。IGSD 将该对比用作候选对蒸馏的仅正向软权重，同时保持 GRPO 目标不变，并将验证限制在训练阶段。在七个单跳和多跳 QA 基准上，IGSD 分别以 3B 和 7B 策略达到 42.8% 和 47.0% 的宏平均精确匹配准确率，且无需推理时验证。这些结果支持环境验证的后见信息是一种为搜索智能体提供可靠动作级监督的有效方法。

Original abstract: On-policy self-distillation densifies agent training without external teachers: a policy conditioned on privileged hindsight provides step-level guidance for its own unprivileged rollouts. For search agents, however, hindsight can make the teacher prefer a query that does not improve retrieval from the student's state. Existing methods either distill this preference directly or filter it with model-internal scores, but neither strategy verifies the query's executed retrieval consequence. We propose Information-Gain-Gated Self-Distillation (IGSD), which verifies on-policy token proposals with environment feedback before distilling them. Treating each query token as a micro-action, IGSD completes the teacher's token proposal and the student's sampled token into matched queries and executes both from the same failed state with the same retriever. Shared counterfactual controls account for query-conditioned shifts in answer likelihood, so their difference, the executed paired information gain, provides a relative utility contrast for the retrieved documents. IGSD uses this contrast as a positive-only soft weight for candidate-pair distillation, while leaving the GRPO objective unchanged and confining verification to training. Across seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks, IGSD reaches macro-average exact-match accuracies of 42.8% and 47.0% with 3B and 7B policies, respectively, without inference-time verification. These results support environment-verified hindsight as an effective approach to reliable action-level supervision for search agents.

</details>


### [87] [CAIRN: Dynamic Fact-Intent DAGs for Multi-Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32700)
*Zuyao Xu,Yuyang Jia,Junwei Guan,Xiang Li,Kaiwen Shen,Zhiqiang Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables

Selection reason: 以动态事实-意图 DAG 组织多 agent 探索，保留依赖、支持知识复用与轨迹审计，并对高消耗任务给出加速与 token 开销实证，对多 agent 规划与协作有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出事实-意图驱动、基于DAG的多智能体范式CAIRN，用于目标导向探索；在低投入任务上可能增加token成本，但在高投入任务上可显著加速且相对开销下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对如何组织LLM驱动的自主系统以实现有效、可靠和持续性能这一未决问题，探索多智能体协调机制。

Method: CAIRN将观察和计划中的探究表示为动态DAG；推理器从事实提出意图，工作者执行意图产生新事实，每个意图引用支撑事实并形成潜在探索分支；持久化图保留目标、依赖和发现，支持知识复用、并行探索及轨迹审计与人工干预。

Result: 在网络安全和数学推理任务上，低投入任务中DAG协调可能增加token成本且无可见性能收益；在高投入任务（至少100万token）中，76.5%情况下求解更快，最高加速3.08倍，且任务投入越大时间增益越显著、相对token开销越低。

Conclusion: DAG引导的并行探索在复杂高投入任务中具有提升效率的潜力，但需权衡低投入场景下的token成本，并受益于可追踪、可审计的持久化协调结构。

中文摘要：由LLM驱动的自主系统已在数学推理、工程和网络安全领域展现出令人期待的能力。然而，如何组织这些系统以实现有效、可靠且持续的性能仍是一个未决问题。在本文中，我们提出CAIRN，一种用于目标导向探索的事实-意图驱动的多智能体范式。CAIRN将观察和计划中的探究表示为动态有向无环图（DAG）。推理器解释事实以提出意图，工作者执行这些意图以产生新事实。每个意图都引用其支撑事实，并定义一个潜在的探索分支。持久化图跨工作者保留目标、依赖关系和发现，支持知识复用与并行探索。该图还使执行轨迹可追踪、可审计，为人工验证和干预提供基础。我们在网络安全和数学推理任务上评估CAIRN，考察任务成功率、求解时间和token消耗。在仅需少量投入的任务上，基于DAG的协调可能带来更高的token成本，且未观察到性能收益。然而，在高投入任务（至少100万token）上，我们在76.5%的情况下观察到更快的求解，加速比最高达3.08倍。此外，随着任务投入增加，这些时间增益变得更加显著，而相对token开销下降，凸显了DAG引导的并行探索的潜力。

Original abstract: LLM-powered autonomous systems have demonstrated promising capabilities in mathematical reasoning, engineering, and cybersecurity. Yet how to organize these systems for effective, reliable, and sustained performance remains an open question. In this paper, we present CAIRN, a fact-intent-driven multi-agent paradigm for goal-directed exploration. CAIRN represents observations and planned investigations as a dynamic directed acyclic graph (DAG). A reasoner interprets facts to propose intents, which workers execute to produce new facts. Each intent references its supporting facts and defines a potential exploration branch. The persistent graph preserves goals, dependencies and findings across workers, supporting knowledge reuse and parallel exploration. The graph also makes execution trajectories traceable and auditable, providing a basis for human verification and intervention. We evaluate CAIRN across cybersecurity and mathematical reasoning tasks, examining task success, time to solution, and token consumption. DAG-based coordination can incur higher token costs with no observable performance gains on tasks that require little effort. However, on high-effort tasks (at least 1M tokens), we observe faster solutions in 76.5% of cases, with speedups of up to 3.08x. Moreover, as task effort increases, these time gains become more pronounced while relative token overhead declines, highlighting the potential of DAG-guided parallel exploration.

</details>


### [88] [Despite Instructions: Frontier Agents Improvise Covert Channels at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32701)
*Jacob Dineen,Silei Ren,Muhao Chen,Dan Roth,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 揭示前沿 agent 在测试时自发形成隐蔽通信信道，说明多 agent 协作与安全监控的可靠性风险，对 agent 安全控制研究有启示。

TL;DR: 成对语言模型智能体仅凭1比特正确性反馈，在推理阶段且无编码示例条件下，可学会通过公开报告摘要的重复交互传递四种秘密状态；该效应在模拟事件响应任务中持续，十局 GPT-5.6 Sol 达到98.8%最终准确率，而随机概率为25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全敏感场景要求语言模型智能体在不泄露机密信息的前提下协调，但重复交互可能让普通消息产生共享私密含义，因此需要研究合作交互是否会导致机密信息经合法协调消息泄露。

Method: 构建成对模型的重复博弈：发送方观察四种秘密状态之一，从同一公开报告的四种摘要中选择一种；接收方推断秘密状态。仅提供1比特反馈（接收方是否推断正确），在固定参数推理阶段进行，不提供码本或编码示例；并在模拟事件响应任务中测试智能体生成自由形式更新的情形；共十个独立游戏，使用成对 GPT-5.6 Sol 智能体，并设置无法访问交互历史的逐消息监控器。

Result: 模型对能够学会传递秘密；该效应在智能体生成自由形式更新的模拟事件响应任务中仍然存在。十个独立游戏中，成对 GPT-5.6 Sol 智能体达到98.8%最终准确率，而随机概率为25%，尽管有明确禁止披露的指令且监控器逐条筛查消息。

Conclusion: 有助于智能体合作的重复交互也可形成隐蔽信道，使机密信息通过本用于合法协调的消息传递，表明需关注推理阶段涌现的隐蔽通信风险。

中文摘要：在安全敏感应用中，语言模型智能体常常需要在不泄露机密信息的情况下进行协调。然而，重复交互也可能让普通消息获得共享的私密含义。我们研究一个由成对模型参与的重复博弈：发送方模型观察到四种秘密状态之一，并从同一份公开报告的四种摘要中选择一种，而接收方模型试图推断该秘密状态。我们发现，模型对仅利用一比特反馈——即接收方是否推断正确——就能学会传递秘密。这种学习发生在推理阶段，参数固定，且没有提供码本或编码示例。当智能体在模拟的事件响应任务中生成自己的自由形式更新时，该效应仍然存在。在十个独立博弈中，成对的 GPT-5.6 Sol 智能体达到 98.8% 的最终准确率，而随机概率为 25%，尽管有明确指令禁止泄露，并且存在一个在无法访问智能体交互历史的情况下筛查每条消息的监控器。因此，同一类有助于智能体合作的交互，也可能让机密信息通过本用于合法协调的消息传递出去。

Original abstract: In security-sensitive applications, language-model agents are often required to coordinate without disclosing confidential information. Yet repeated interactions may also let ordinary messages acquire shared private meaning. We study a repeated game with pairs of models in which the sender model observes one of four secret states and selects one of four summaries of the same public report, while the receiver model tries to infer the secret state. We find that model pairs can learn to communicate the secret using only one bit of feedback indicating whether the receiver inferred it correctly. This learning occurs during inference with fixed parameters and no supplied codebook or encoding examples. The effect also persists when agents generate their own free-form updates in a simulated incident-response task. Across ten independent games, pairs of GPT-5.6 Sol agents reach 98.8% final accuracy, compared with 25% chance, despite explicit instructions prohibiting disclosure and a monitor that screens each message without access to the agents' interaction histories. The same interactions that help agents cooperate can therefore allow confidential information to pass through messages intended for legitimate coordination.

</details>


### [89] [SkillVine: Agent Skill Evolution via Branching Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32731)
*Kaiwei Liu,Jiqian Dong,Liran Dong,Shuai Mao,Mingming Zhao,Bufang Yang,Jie Chuai,Zhitang Chen,Guoliang Xing,Zhenyu Yan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 agent 技能库演化建模为图搜索并采用分支探索、父节点选择与自适应粒度更新，直接对应轨迹经验复用与自我改进型 agent，可迁移到代码优化策略库的迭代提升。

TL;DR: SkillVine将技能进化建模为图搜索并采用分支探索，在多数基准-模型组合上取得最佳测试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能进化方法主要采用线性范式，仅对最新技能库版本顺序更新，容易陷入局部最优，且无法探索许多有前景的进化路径。

Method: 提出 SkillVine 自动技能进化框架，将技能进化形式化为图搜索问题，采用分支探索策略，并引入主干-分支协同搜索机制、智能父节点选择器和自适应粒度更新规则，以平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在 5 个基准和两个 LLM 上进行评估，SkillVine 沿分支发现的技能库版本优于沿线性主干发现的版本，并在十个基准-模型组合中的九个取得最佳测试性能。

Conclusion: SkillVine 通过图搜索与分支探索克服线性进化的局部最优问题，有效提升技能进化效果，并在多数评测组合中达到最优性能。

中文摘要：智能体技能封装了可复用的程序性知识，使 LLM 智能体能够执行任务，并且可以利用与环境交互产生的轨迹自动改进。这是经典的技能进化问题。现有方法主要遵循线性进化范式，即按顺序将更新应用于最新的技能库版本。因此，它们不可避免地陷入局部最优，留下许多有前景的进化路径未被探索。我们提出 SkillVine，一种自动技能进化框架，将技能进化建模为图搜索问题，并采用分支探索策略。借助主干-分支协同搜索机制、智能父节点选择器和自适应粒度更新规则，SkillVine 在探索与利用之间取得平衡。我们在 5 个基准上使用两个 LLM 评估 SkillVine。结果表明，SkillVine 沿分支发现的技能库版本优于沿线性主干发现的版本，并在十个基准-模型组合中的九个取得了最佳测试性能。

Original abstract: Agent skills encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge that enables LLM agents to perform tasks, and they can be improved automatically using trajectories from interactions with the environment. This is the classic problem of skill evolution. Existing approaches predominately follow a linear evolution paradigm, in which updates are sequentially applied to the latest skill-library version. As a result, they inevitably fall into local optima, leaving many promising evolution paths unexplored. We propose SkillVine, an automatic skill-evolution framework that formulates skill evolution as a graph search problem and employs a branching exploration strategy. Equipped with a trunk-branch collaborative searching mechanism, an intelligent parent-node selector, and an adaptive-granularity update rule, SkillVine achieves a balance between exploration and exploitation. We evaluate SkillVine on 5 benchmarks with two LLMs. Results show that SkillVine discovers better skill-library versions along branches than along the linear trunk and achieves the best test performance in nine of ten benchmark-model combinations.

</details>


### [90] [CUA-Sandbox: Efficient Environments for Computer-Use Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32750)
*Xin Yan,Zhengbo Jiao,Jiaqi Liu,Zhenglin Wan,SiYuan Ma,Xuliang Yu,Tianyi Jiang,Chubin Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Xingrui Yu,Bo An,Yang You,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 computer-use agent RL 的沙箱环境，通过状态/运行时分离把 rollout 吞吐提升 6.2x、内存降 9.2x，直接有益于 agent 训练与轨迹采样基础设施。

TL;DR: CUA-Sandbox 将独立可变状态与可复用应用运行时分离，用状态胶囊和事务性生命周期操作支撑计算机使用智能体的并行 rollout，在保持任务成功率的同时显著提升吞吐并降低内存与存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机使用智能体训练会为每个独立 rollout 复制已初始化运行时，即使复用同一软件，也会随并行环境数量增加而重复消耗内存与初始化成本。核心问题是：独立环境是否必须依赖独立执行运行时。

Method: 作者观察到轨迹真正需要的是独立可变状态，而非独立应用运行时。为此提出 CUA-Sandbox，通过状态作用域执行将私有状态胶囊与共享运行时分离，并提供事务性生命周期操作，包括重置和分支，同时保留原始软件接口与任务评估器。

Result: 实验显示，相较 Docker，CUA-Sandbox 的任务成功率相当或更高，同时大幅降低 rollout 与资源成本：rollout 吞吐量最高提升 6.20 倍，单环境内存降低 9.2 倍，增量存储降低 504 倍。

Conclusion: 环境独立性可由状态而非运行时来承载；复用已初始化应用运行时并隔离可变状态，可在不牺牲任务表现的情况下显著提高计算机使用智能体训练与部署的效率。

中文摘要：强化学习使计算机使用智能体能够通过与真实软件环境（包括网站和桌面应用）交互来改进。然而，传统部署会为每个独立 rollout 复制一个已初始化的运行时，即使轨迹使用相同软件，也会随着并行环境数量增长而带来重复的内存和初始化成本。一个独立的计算机使用环境是否需要独立的执行运行时？我们的关键观察是：轨迹需要独立的可变状态，而已初始化的应用运行时可以在并发演化的环境之间复用，因此状态是环境独立性的自然单元。基于这一观察，我们提出 CUA-Sandbox，它通过状态作用域执行以及事务性生命周期操作（包括重置和分支），将私有状态胶囊与共享运行时分离，同时保留原始软件接口和任务评估器。实验表明，与 Docker 相比，其任务成功率相当或有所提升，同时大幅降低 rollout 和资源成本。CUA-Sandbox 实现了最高 6.20 倍的 rollout 吞吐量提升、9.2 倍的单环境内存降低，以及 504 倍的增量存储降低。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning enables computer-use agents to improve through interaction with real software environments, including websites and desktop applications. However, conventional deployments replicate an initialized runtime for each independent rollout, even when trajectories use the same software, incurring repeated memory and initialization costs as the number of parallel environments grows. Does an independent computer-use environment require an independent execution runtime? Our key observation is that trajectories require independent mutable state, while initialized application runtimes can be reused across concurrently evolving environments, making state the natural unit of environment independence. Guided by this observation, we introduce CUA-Sandbox, which separates private state capsules from shared runtimes through state-scoped execution and transactional lifecycle operations, including resets and branches, while retaining the original software interfaces and task evaluators. Experiments show comparable or improved task success relative to Docker, while substantially reducing rollout and resource costs. CUA-Sandbox achieves up to a 6.20x increase in rollout throughput, a 9.2x reduction in per-environment memory, and a 504x reduction in incremental storage.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Consistency Graph for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32754)
*Jiecong Wang,Hao Peng,Zhanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程 agent 的自适应一致性图，将执行证据与出处持久化并在预算内构造需求中心视图，直接对应 agent 记忆/上下文一致性难题。

TL;DR: 提出自适应一致性图 ACG，通过持久图组织执行证据与来源，并在有限上下文下为长时程任务的每个决策构建以需求为中心的临时视图，从而提升 LLM 智能体长期执行的稳定性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在短任务上局部决策合理，但在需要长序列依赖行动和工具调用时表现下降；执行中任务要求、历史证据与当前状态易脱节，导致后续决策偏离目标。

Method: ACG 不替换基础规划器或工具执行器，而是增量维护执行证据及其来源的持久图，并在受限上下文预算下为每个决策生成结构化、可追溯的以需求为中心的视图。

Result: 在匹配评估中，ACG 使 GPT-5.6-luna 平均成功率从 ReAct 的 44.5% 提升至 50.2%；BrowseComp-Plus 上增益最大，从 62.4% 提升至 73.5%；并进一步分析轨迹结构与推理成本。

Conclusion: ACG 通过结构化、可追溯且以需求为中心的上下文视图缓解长时程执行中的目标漂移，提高智能体成功率；代码已开源。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体通常能在短任务上做出合理的局部决策，但当成功需要一长串相互依赖的行动与工具调用时，其性能会下降。在执行过程中，任务要求、历史证据和当前执行状态可能逐渐脱节，因此后续决策可能偏离原始目标。我们通过引入面向长时程执行的自适应一致性图（Adaptive Consistency Graph，ACG）来研究这一问题。ACG 将执行证据及其来源增量地组织到一个持久图中，然后在受限的上下文预算下，为每个决策构建一个临时的、以需求为中心的视图。ACG 并不取代基础智能体的规划器或工具执行器，而是为每个决策提供结构化且可追溯的上下文视图。在匹配评估中，ACG 将 GPT-5.6-luna 的平均成功率从使用 ReAct 时的 44.5% 提升到 50.2%，其中在 BrowseComp-Plus 上提升最大（73.5% 对比 62.4%）。我们进一步分析轨迹结构和推理成本，以刻画这一改进。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/yunsaijc/Adaptive-Consistency-Graph 获取。

Original abstract: Large language model agents can often make reasonable local decisions on short tasks, yet their performance degrades when success requires long sequences of dependent actions and tool calls. During execution, task requirements, historical evidence, and the current execution state may gradually become disconnected, so later decisions can drift from the original objective. We study this problem by introducing the Adaptive Consistency Graph (ACG) for long-horizon execution. ACG incrementally organizes execution evidence and its provenance in a persistent graph, then constructs a temporary requirement-centered view for each decision under a bounded context budget. Rather than replacing the base agent's planner or tool executor, ACG provides a structured and traceable context view for each decision. In the matched evaluation, ACG improves GPT-5.6-luna's average success from 44.5\% with ReAct to 50.2\%, with the largest gain on BrowseComp-Plus (73.5\% versus 62.4\%). We further analyze trajectory structure and inference cost to characterize this improvement. Our code is available at https://github.com/yunsaijc/Adaptive-Consistency-Graph.

</details>


### [92] [Agentic Network Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32778)
*Manuel Tsoukatos,Hayden Jananthan,Jeremy Kepner*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures, to appear in IEEE URTC 2026

Selection reason: 用稀疏流量矩阵审计 agentic 系统的网络行为，提供 agent 可观测性与行为对齐审计思路，可迁移性一般但值得跟踪。

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于复值超稀疏流量矩阵、DBOS、OneSparse PostgreSQL 与 GraphBLAS 的智能体系统网络流量监控方法，并用智能体模拟器表明其便于监控 AI 智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体人工智能在几乎各行业普及，攻击面扩大，因此需要监控智能体并确保其行为符合用户意图；审计网络流量可提供智能体交互的清晰记录。

Method: 集成 DBOS、OneSparse PostgreSQL 数据库和 GraphBLAS 数学库，使用复值超稀疏流量矩阵监控智能体系统网络流量；构建智能体模拟器，让不同数量的 AI 智能体以不同策略共同勘察虚拟环境。

Result: 生成了网络流量矩阵，这些矩阵使 AI 智能体的监控变得容易。

Conclusion: 通过审计智能体网络流量，并结合复值超稀疏流量矩阵与相关数据库、数学库，可为智能体行为对齐监控提供清晰记录和便捷手段。

中文摘要：随着智能体人工智能在几乎每个行业中的使用不断增加，攻击面也在不断扩大。有必要对智能体进行监控，以确保智能体的行为方式与用户意图保持一致。审计智能体的网络流量为智能体交互提供了清晰的记录。这项工作提出了一种使用复值超稀疏流量矩阵来监控智能体系统网络流量的新方法，该方法通过集成 DBOS（DataBase OS）、OneSparse PostgreSQL 数据库和 GraphBLAS 数学库实现。为了开发这些概念，构建了一个智能体模拟器，允许数量可变的 AI 智能体使用不同策略共同勘察一个虚拟环境。由此产生的网络流量矩阵使得对 AI 智能体的监控变得容易。

Original abstract: As the use of agentic artificial intelligence increases in nearly every industry, there exists a widening attack surface. It is necessary to monitor agents to ensure that agents are acting in a way that is aligned with the users intent. Auditing an agent's network traffic provides a clear record of the agent interactions. This work presents a novel approach to monitoring the network traffic of agentic systems using complex valued hypersparse traffic matrices by integrating DBOS (DataBase OS), the OneSparse PostgreSQL database, and the GraphBLAS math library. To develop these concepts an agentic simulator was constructed, allowing a varying numbers of AI agents to collectively survey a virtual environment using different strategies. The resulting network traffic matrices enable easy monitoring of the AI agents.

</details>


### [93] [CLAIRE: A Schema-Grounded Hybrid Workflow for Healthcare Administrative Form Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32787)
*Garapati Keerthana,Manik Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: CLAIRE 把表单填写拆成字段发现、映射、确定性校验、有界纠错、升级与审计追踪的分层 agentic 工作流，其验证优先与受限动作授权模式可迁移到代码 agent。

TL;DR: CLAIRE 是一种将字段状态发现、源到字段映射、确定性校验、有界纠正、升级和审计追踪分离的混合工作流；合成基准中完整版达到 1.000 成功率，移除校验后压力套件降至 0.500；模拟中 100.0% 干净和校验压力回合可生成可审核草稿，升级挑战为 68.6%，不支持案例被阻断；情景节省为每例 149.7-165.5 秒，语言模型仅辅助映射、不授权不受支持或重大后果操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗行政人员需要从电子健康记录、转诊、理赔系统、提供者名册和工作队列等来源将结构化信息转录到动态表单。直接依赖语言模型进行映射可能产生不可靠或不可审计的结果，因此需要一种可验证、可升级、可追踪且能阻止不支持操作的自动化方案。

Method: 开发并评估 CLAIRE（Clinical Language and Agentic Intelligence for Reasoning and Entry）混合工作流，将字段状态发现、源到字段映射、确定性校验、有界纠正、升级和审计追踪分离。测试五个合成医疗行政模式、1,000 条源记录、四种界面变体、两个数据质量套件和六个比较器，共 24,000 个基准回合；另以严格输出审计评估 Qwen2.5-1.5B 与 Qwen2.5-7B 的直接映射，并进行覆盖 6,000 个回合的轨迹推导运行模拟。

Result: 在合成基准条件下，完整 CLAIRE 在两个套件中的回合成功率、字段准确率、必填字段完成率和依赖完成率均为 1.000；移除校验后压力套件成功率降至 0.500。模拟中，100.0% 的干净和校验压力回合达到可由工作人员审核的草稿，升级挑战回合为 68.6%，不支持案例被阻断。基于情景的节省为每例 149.7-165.5 秒，而非实际观察到的员工用时。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于模式、校验优先的医疗行政自动化具有支持价值；语言模型组件可辅助映射，但不应被授权执行不受支持或具有重大后果的操作。

中文摘要：医疗行政人员将结构化信息从电子健康记录、转诊、理赔系统、提供者名册和工作队列转录到动态表单中。我们开发并评估了 CLAIRE（Clinical Language and Agentic Intelligence for Reasoning and Entry），一种混合工作流，它将字段状态发现、源到字段映射、确定性校验、有界纠正、升级和审计追踪分离。我们测试了五个合成医疗行政模式、1,000 条源记录、四种界面变体、两个数据质量套件和六个比较器，产生了 24,000 个基准回合。一项单独的严格输出审计评估了来自 Qwen2.5-1.5B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 的直接映射，一项由轨迹推导的运行模拟覆盖了 6,000 个回合。在受评估的合成基准条件下，完整 CLAIRE 在两个套件中均达到 1.000 的回合成功率、字段准确率、必填字段完成率和依赖完成率；移除校验后，压力套件的成功率降至 0.500。在模拟中，100.0% 的干净和校验压力回合达到了可由工作人员审核的草稿，而升级挑战回合为 68.6%；不受支持的案例被阻断。基于情景的节省为每例 149.7-165.5 秒，而非实际观察到的员工用时。这些发现支持基于模式、校验优先的医疗行政自动化，其中语言模型组件辅助映射，但不授权不受支持或会产生重大后果的操作。

Original abstract: Healthcare administrative staff transfer structured information from electronic health records, referrals, claims systems, provider rosters, and work queues into dynamic forms. We developed and evaluated CLAIRE (Clinical Language and Agentic Intelligence for Reasoning and Entry), a hybrid workflow that separates field-state discovery, source-to-field mapping, deterministic validation, bounded correction, escalation, and audit tracing. We tested five synthetic healthcare administrative schemas, 1,000 source records, four interface variants, two data-quality suites, and six comparators, yielding 24,000 benchmark episodes. A separate strict-output audit evaluated direct mappings from Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B, and a trace-derived operational simulation covered 6,000 episodes. Under the evaluated synthetic benchmark conditions, full CLAIRE achieved 1.000 episode success, field accuracy, required-field completion, and dependency completion in both suites; removing validation reduced stress-suite success to 0.500. In the simulation, 100.0% of clean and validation-stress episodes reached a staff-reviewable draft, compared with 68.6% of escalation challenge episodes, unsupported cases were blocked. Scenario-based savings were 149.7-165.5 seconds per case, not observed staff times. The findings support schema-grounded, validation-first healthcare administrative automation in which language-model components assist mapping but do not authorize unsupported or consequential actions.

</details>


### [94] [AgentHabit: Characterizing Distinct Behaviors of Agents on Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32795)
*Woojung Song,Hoyeol Yang,Jeonghoon Shim,Sungjib Lim,Jonggeun Lee,Yunho Choi,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 60 pages, 16 figures

Selection reason: HABIT/AgentHABIT 从轨迹中刻画 agent 行为倾向（是否澄清、是否检索等），提供超越任务成功率的 agent 评测框架。

TL;DR: 提出 HABIT 行为分类体系与 AgentHABIT 基准，从轨迹刻画 LLM 智能体完成日常任务的行为倾向，并揭示模型差异、跨任务稳定性及干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体完成日常任务的方式多样，即使答案有用，其行为也可能不符合用户偏好与需求；现有价值观或行为分类不足以清晰区分这些行为差异。

Method: 通过三位作者和三个 LLM 自下而上地从 17 个领域的 408 条轨迹中归纳出五大类别、23 个行为轴的 HABIT 分类体系；并基于 HABIT 构建 AgentHABIT 基准，用 86 个日常任务上的轨迹对 18 个模型进行行为画像。

Result: AgentHABIT 揭示不同模型具有独特行为倾向，例如 GPT 和 Claude 多在需求冲突时陈述假设并提供替代方案，而 Qwen 和 Google 模型更常保持假设或需求变更未说明；画像在不同任务集上仍可识别，提示行为具有一般性；提示能改变部分行为轴，微调只改变部分画像。

Conclusion: HABIT 和 AgentHABIT 提供了超越任务成功、系统刻画智能体日常任务执行方式的框架，有助于指导开发更符合用户需求的行为。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体协助用户完成日常任务，而这些任务可以以许多合理的方式完成。即使它们的答案有用，智能体执行这些任务的方式也可能不符合用户的偏好和需求。例如，智能体在是否提出澄清性问题或是否搜索网页方面存在差异。我们提出 HABIT，这是一个包含五大类别、23 个行为轴的分类体系，由三位作者和三个 LLM 自下而上地从跨 17 个领域的 408 条智能体轨迹中推导得出。在留出任务上，HABIT 比现有的人类价值观和智能体行为分类体系更清晰地区分模型，同时支持具有可比一致性的标注。基于 HABIT，我们构建了 AgentHABIT，一个根据智能体在 86 个日常任务上的轨迹来刻画其行为倾向的基准。用 AgentHABIT 对 18 个模型进行画像，揭示了一系列独特的倾向。例如，大多数 GPT 和 Claude 模型在需求冲突时会陈述其假设并提供替代方案，而 Qwen 和 Google 的模型更常将假设或需求变更留作未说明。即使这些画像完全基于不同的任务集构建，它们仍然可被识别，这表明它们反映的是一般倾向，而非特定任务行为。提示智能体采用特定行为可以轻易改变某些轴，但几乎不改变其他轴；而在另一个模型的轨迹上进行微调只会改变模型画像的一部分，并使其大部分保持不变。总体而言，HABIT 和 AgentHABIT 提供了一个系统框架，用于刻画智能体如何完成日常任务，而不仅限于任务成功，为引导开发更符合用户需求的智能体行为提供了洞见。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents assist users with everyday tasks that can be completed in many reasonable ways. Even when their answers are useful, how agents carry out these tasks may not match users' preferences and needs. For example, agents differ in whether they ask clarifying questions or search the web. We introduce HABIT, a taxonomy of 23 behavioral axes in five categories, which three authors and three LLMs derive bottom-up from 408 agent trajectories across 17 domains. On held-out tasks, HABIT distinguishes models more clearly than existing taxonomies of human values and agent actions while supporting comparably consistent annotation. Building on HABIT, we construct AgentHABIT, a benchmark that profiles each agent's behavioral tendencies from its trajectories on 86 everyday tasks. Profiling 18 models with AgentHABIT reveals a range of distinctive tendencies. For example, most GPT and Claude models state their assumptions and offer alternatives when requirements conflict, whereas Qwen and Google's models more often leave assumptions or changes to requirements unstated. These profiles remain recognizable even when built from entirely different sets of tasks, indicating that they reflect general tendencies rather than task-specific behavior. Prompting agents to adopt specific behaviors shifts some axes readily but barely changes others, while fine-tuning on another model's trajectories changes only part of a model's profile and leaves much of it intact. Overall, HABIT and AgentHABIT provide a systematic framework for characterizing how agents carry out everyday tasks beyond task success, offering insights to guide the development of agents whose behavior better fits users' needs.

</details>


### [95] [PlanGuard: A Guardrail for Multi-Step Plan Safety in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32801)
*Junchi Chen,Changtao Miao,Yuxiao Xiang,Zhenchao Jin,Haojie Yuan,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,He Liu,Bo Zhang,Jiansheng Cai,Zhe Li,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向具身 agent 多步计划执行前物理安全检测的 guardrail 与在线策略蒸馏压缩方法，其计划级风险校验思路可迁移到 agent 动作安全控制。

TL;DR: 提出首个执行前整计划物理安全检测器PlanGuard，构建MSP-Safe数据集，并用STAC-OPD蒸馏方法使2B模型达到87.15% ACC与87.21% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全保障忽视多步计划中由子任务依赖和环境交互导致的组合性物理风险；通用防护栏只关注语义危害，具身安全检测器则孤立评估子任务。

Method: 提出PlanGuard执行前检测完整多步计划在当前环境中的物理安全性；构建MSP-Safe数据集，包括配对任务构建、多样化规划器生成计划和三名评判者安全标注；先进行任务导向SFT，再提出STAC-OPD，通过词元级分布迁移和概率路由的序列级补偿，沿在线策略轨迹为紧凑模型提供自适应强教师监督。

Result: PlanGuard-2B在所有测试子集上平均达到87.15% ACC和87.21% F1，表明紧凑模型规模下可有效检测整计划物理风险。

Conclusion: PlanGuard能够弥补组合性物理风险检测空白，并以紧凑模型实现有效的整计划物理风险检测；代码和数据集将公开释放。

中文摘要：具身任务规划器可能生成多步计划，其子任务依赖关系以及与环境的交互会在执行过程中产生物理风险。然而，现有安全保障措施忽视了这种组合性风险，因为通用防护栏关注语义危害，而具身安全检测器孤立地评估子任务。为弥补这一空白，我们提出PlanGuard，这是首个在执行前评估完整多步计划在当前环境中物理安全性的检测器。为了训练和评估，我们通过配对任务构建、使用多样化规划器生成计划以及由三名评判者进行安全标注，构建了多步计划安全（MSP-Safe）数据集。在MSP-Safe上进行面向任务的SFT建立了基本的计划安全评估能力，但适合实时部署的紧凑模型与更强大但成本更高的大模型之间仍存在显著差距。因此，我们提出用于在线策略蒸馏的强教师自适应补偿（STAC-OPD），它沿紧凑模型的在线策略轨迹为其提供自适应的强教师监督。它结合了来自微调强教师的词元级分布迁移与概率路由的序列级补偿：当学生模型倾向于参考安全决策时，保留学生生成的目标；否则使用教师重构的目标。在所有测试子集上，PlanGuard-2B平均达到87.15% ACC和87.21% F1，展示了在紧凑模型规模下有效的整计划物理风险检测。代码和数据集将公开释放。

Original abstract: Embodied task planners may produce multi-step plans whose subtask dependencies and interactions with the environment create physical risks during execution. Yet existing safeguards overlook such compositional risks, as general-purpose guardrails focus on semantic harm and embodied safety detectors assess subtasks in isolation. To address this gap, we introduce PlanGuard, the first pre-execution detector that evaluates the physical safety of a complete multi-step plan in its current environment. For training and evaluation, we construct a Multi-Step Plan Safety (MSP-Safe) dataset through paired task construction, plan generation using diverse planners, and safety annotation by three judges. Task-oriented SFT on MSP-Safe establishes fundamental plan-safety assessment capabilities, yet a substantial gap remains between compact models suitable for real-time deployment and stronger but costlier large models. Accordingly, we propose Strong-Teacher Adaptive Compensation for On-Policy Distillation (STAC-OPD), which provides compact models with adaptive strong-teacher supervision along their on-policy trajectories. It combines token-level distribution transfer from a fine-tuned strong teacher with probability-routed sequence-level compensation, retaining student-generated targets when the student favors the reference safety decision and using teacher-reconstructed targets otherwise. Across all test subsets, PlanGuard-2B achieves average 87.15% ACC and 87.21% F1, demonstrating effective whole-plan physical-risk detection at compact model scale. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [96] [Re-derivability Decides What a Staged Agent Pipeline Recovers After an Upstream Fault](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32802)
*Tianqi Bu,YuXuan Peng,Junteng Tu,Henghui Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 34 pages, 8 figures, 13 tables; preprint under review

Selection reason: 通过受控故障注入研究多阶段 agent 流水线的可重导出性，量化重新接入原始问题对下游恢复的收益与成本，对 agent 容错与拓扑设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 上游故障代价由可重新推导性决定；重新接地检查器或下游阶段可修复故障，但无故障时流水线不敌直接调用，有效修复前置且廉价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分阶段语言模型智能体流水线中上游故障代价的决定因素，以及架构分解是否优于直接调用，并寻找低成本修复。

Method: 在四个开放权重骨干上禁用思考，注入确定性故障，固定智能体、条目、故障和拓扑，操纵下游重新暴露原始问题的阶段数 k=0…3，每个实验臂 120 个 gsm_hard 条目，温度为零；使用头对头检查器、空白词槽、交互效应、注册排除测试等，并比较流水线与直接调用。

Result: 重新接地检查器相对盲检查器增益 +0.608 至 +0.358；故障下准确率四/四提升，+0.233 至 +0.392，最大 Holm p=2.1e-6；保留率随可见比例四/四提升，0.221 至 0.692；交互效应在注册/三阶段/完整历史下分别二/四、四/四、三/四排除零，主要 +0.317；Llama-3.1-8B 无检测代价；无故障时注册流水线输给直接调用 -0.267/-0.125/-0.317，Phi-4 +0.058 p=0.118；Qwen3-14B 首个重新接地阶段 +0.394 保留率，+59.8 tokens/条目，后续阶段无收益。

Conclusion: 可重新推导性既决定故障代价也决定架构代价；有效修复应前置且廉价；大多数分解不优于直接调用；故障代价结论主要由三个非 Llama 骨干承载。

中文摘要：一个变量决定了上游故障对语言模型智能体分阶段流水线造成的代价：可重新推导性，即一个阶段所需的信息中有多少可以从原始问题中重建。将检查器智能体建立在该问题上，相比盲检查器，在四个以禁用思考模式服务的开放权重骨干模型上，价值为 +0.608 [+0.517, +0.700] 到 +0.358，而在两个 Qwen 骨干模型上，盲检查器完全不会改变任何条目。该头对头比较是探索性的。一个确定性故障进入第一阶段，我们在保持智能体、条目、故障和拓扑固定的情况下，将原始问题重新暴露给 k=0,…,3 个下游阶段，每个实验臂使用 120 个 gsm_hard 条目，温度为零。故障下的准确率在四个骨干模型中的四个上均上升，从 +0.233 到 +0.392，最大的 Holm 校正 p 值为 2.1×10^-6。一项注册的 kill 测试排除了 token 数量解释。将每个词置空保持词槽固定，保留率在四个骨干模型中的四个上均与可见比例同步，在主要骨干模型上从 0.221 攀升至 0.692。这两组结果是验证性的，而此处其他所有内容都是探索性的。该交互效应在注册流水线下于四个骨干模型中的两个上排除零，在三阶段流水线下于四个中的四个上排除零，在完整消息历史下于四个中的三个上排除零，主要结果为 +0.317。在 Llama-3.1-8B 上，故障在任何剂量下都不带来可检测的代价，因此其他三个模型承载了所有关于故障代价的结论。可重新推导性也决定了架构的代价，而我们测量的任何分解方法都不能可靠地胜过一次直接调用。未注入故障时，注册流水线相对于该直接调用分别损失 -0.267、-0.125 和 -0.317，而在 Phi-4 上读数为 +0.058，p=0.118，检验未能将其与零区分开。有效的修复代价低且前置：在 Qwen3-14B 上，第一个重新接地的阶段以每个条目增加 +59.8 个 token 的代价换来 +0.394 的匹配保留率，而其后的阶段没有带来任何收益。

Original abstract: One variable sets what an upstream fault costs a staged pipeline of language-model agents: re-derivability, how much of what a stage needs it can rebuild from the original problem. Grounding an inspector agent in that problem is worth +0.608 [+0.517, +0.700] to +0.358 over a blind one on four open-weight backbones served with thinking disabled, and on the two Qwen backbones the blind inspector changes no item at all. That head-to-head is exploratory. One deterministic fault enters the first stage, and we re-expose the original problem to $k = 0,\dots,3$ of the downstream stages with agents, items, fault and topology held fixed, on 120 gsm_hard items per arm at temperature zero. Accuracy under fault rises on four of four backbones, from +0.233 to +0.392, the largest Holm-adjusted $p$ being $2.1\times10^{-6}$. A registered kill test rules out tokens. Blanking every word holds the word slots fixed, and retention tracks the visible fraction on four of four, climbing from 0.221 to 0.692 on the primary. Those two families are confirmatory and everything else here is exploratory. The interaction excludes zero on two of four backbones under the registered pipeline, four of four under a three-stage pipeline, and three of four under full message history, the primary at +0.317. On Llama-3.1-8B the fault carries no detectable cost at any dose, so the other three carry every claim about what a fault costs. Re-derivability also sets what the architecture costs, and no decomposition we measured reliably beats one direct call. With no fault injected the registered pipeline loses to that call by -0.267, -0.125 and -0.317, and on Phi-4 reads +0.058 at $p = 0.118$, which the test fails to separate from zero. The repair that works is cheap and front-loaded: the first re-grounded stage buys +0.394 of matched retention for +59.8 tokens per item on Qwen3-14B, and the stages after it buy nothing.

</details>


### [97] [Decision-Sufficient State Representations: Measuring and Reducing Write-Time Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32805)
*Bingyu Shen,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 29 pages (10 main, 17 appendix), 12 figures (4 main, 8 appendix), 18 tables (2 main, 16 appendix)

Selection reason: 量化并降低 agent 写状态时的「写入时后悔」，用读者前向回报训练 writer，直接对应 agent 记忆压缩与状态表示设计。

TL;DR: 长任务中，写入器维护短状态会丢失信息；损失主要是写入时后悔，并随事实延迟增长。DSSR 用读取器的前向滚动损失训练写入器，在事实很快需要时带来 +7.0 个百分点提升，但延迟增大后因信用分配问题收益受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的上下文有限，且即使历史能放入上下文也未必可靠使用。让智能体携带简短书面状态可降低每步成本，但写入器丢弃的信息可能在后续才被证明必要。本文旨在量化这种损失并检验训练能否减少它。

Method: 将当前书面状态与事后同尺寸最佳状态比较，把损失拆为预算损失和写入时后悔；在可控制事实携带时长的 TextWorld 烹饪游戏上评估。提出 DSSR，用写入器前向携带候选状态后读取器的行动表现给状态打分，并据此训练写入器；与将候选状态当固定上下文的后事打分对比。在预先注册且仅打开一次的测试划分上验证。

Result: 128-token 事实状态几乎每局必胜，而提示语言模型写入器最多 17% 胜率；损失几乎全为写入时后悔且随延迟增长。DSSR 前向滚动得分预测结果 ρ=0.48，固定上下文打分 ρ≤0.07。训练在事实很快需要时提升 +7.0 [+1.9, +12.2] 个百分点，使普通摘要写入器达到基于信念/槽位记忆提示水平；增益随延迟下降，仅最短延迟显著。

Conclusion: 训练收益受信用分配限制：现在保留事实只有在后续每次重写都保留时才有回报，而逐步得分无法捕捉这种跨步依赖。

中文摘要：长任务产生的历史比 LLM 智能体能在其上下文中容纳的更多，也比即使历史能容纳时它能可靠使用的更多。因此，越来越多工作让智能体携带一个简短的书面状态：每一步由一个写入器重写该状态，读取器仅依据该状态行动。步骤保持低成本，但写入器丢弃的任何内容都会在后续决策表明需要它之前就已丢失。我们量化这种损失，并追问训练能否减少它。将书面状态与事后写出的同尺寸最佳状态比较，我们把读取器的损失拆分为预算损失（任何该尺寸状态都必须承受）和写入时后悔（源于写入器的选择）。在 TextWorld 烹饪游戏中，我们控制一个事实在被需要前必须携带多久；一个容纳这些事实的 128 token 状态几乎赢得每一局游戏，而经提示的语言模型写入器最多赢得 17%。几乎全部损失都是写入时后悔，且随延迟增长。然后我们根据读取器自身的损失来训练写入器。DSSR（决策充分状态表示）通过写入器将候选状态向前携带后读取器行动得有多好来给候选状态打分，并教写入器偏好更好的状态。这种前向滚动得分能预测游戏结果（ρ=0.48），而像事后方法通常所做的那样，将候选状态作为固定上下文来打分则不能（ρ≤0.07）。在一个仅打开一次、预先注册的测试划分上，当事实很快被需要时，训练带来 +7.0 [+1.9, +12.2] 个百分点的成功率提升，使一个普通摘要写入器达到基于信念和槽位的记忆提示的水平。增益随延迟增长而缩小，且仅在最短延迟下显著。我们将这一局限追溯到信用分配：现在保留一个事实只有在之后每一次重写也都保留它时才有回报，而逐步得分看不到这一点。

Original abstract: Long tasks produce more history than an LLM agent can hold in its context, and more than it uses reliably even when the history fits. A growing line of work therefore has agents carry a short written state instead: at every step a writer rewrites the state, and a reader acts from the state alone. Steps stay cheap, but anything the writer drops is lost before later decisions reveal that they need it. We quantify this loss and ask whether training can reduce it. Comparing the written state with the best state of the same size written in hindsight, we split the reader's loss into a budget loss, which any state of that size must incur, and a write-time regret, which comes from the writer's choices. In TextWorld cooking games where we control how long a fact must be carried before it is needed, a 128-token state holding the facts wins nearly every game, while prompted language-model writers win at most 17%. Almost all of the loss is write-time regret, and it grows with the delay. We then train the writer from the reader's own loss. DSSR (decision-sufficient state representations) scores candidate states by how well the reader acts after the writer carries them forward, and teaches the writer to prefer the better ones. This forward-rolled score predicts game outcomes ($ρ= 0.48$), whereas scoring a candidate as a fixed context, as hindsight methods usually do, does not ($ρ\leq 0.07$). On a pre-registered test split opened once, training adds +7.0 [+1.9, +12.2] points of success when facts are needed soon, bringing a plain summary writer to the level of belief- and slot-based memory prompts. The gain shrinks as the delay grows and is significant only at the shortest delay. We trace this limit to credit assignment: keeping a fact now pays off only if every later rewrite keeps it too, which a per-step score cannot see.

</details>


### [98] [Improving LLM Collaboration via Multi-Agent Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32827)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Tianle Chen,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出通用的多智能体偏好学习框架MAPL（MARLHF与MADPO），可迁移到多agent协作与反馈驱动的优化循环。

TL;DR: 该文从去中心化与中心化视角形式化基于偏好的多智能体系统，提出通用多智能体偏好学习框架MAPL，并用MARLHF与MADPO实例化；实验表明MAPL能提升协作质量与效率并接近固定奖励MARL，其中MARLHF多数任务优于MADPO但对数据覆盖、模型与底层算法敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM协作中MARL的可靠奖励难以构建，完整准确指标常不可得且难以聚合；偏好学习可提供替代方案，但其在多智能体系统中的扩展研究不足。

Method: 从去中心化和中心化协作视角形式化基于偏好的MAS；提出MAPL，通过将当前解与不同智能体生成的去中心化/中心化解比较进行迭代更新；用带学习奖励模型的MARLHF和MADPO实例化MAPL。

Result: 在协作写作、编码、工具使用、旅行规划任务上，MAPL提升协作质量与效率，性能接近固定明确奖励的MARL；MAPL内MARLHF在多数任务上优于MADPO，但对数据覆盖、智能体与比较器模型及底层MARL算法敏感。

Conclusion: MAPL为多智能体偏好学习提供了通用框架，可缓解奖励构建难题，但MARLHF等方法的鲁棒性仍受数据覆盖、模型选择和底层算法等因素影响。

中文摘要：已有若干工作探索了LLM协作中的多智能体强化学习（MARL）。然而，在实践中构建可靠的奖励很困难，因为完整且准确的指标往往不可获得，且难以聚合。偏好学习通过从人类或AI的比较反馈中学习，提供了一种替代方案。然而，其向多智能体系统的扩展仍未得到充分探索。为弥补这一空白，我们从去中心化和中心化协作视角形式化了基于偏好的多智能体系统（MAS）。我们还引入了一个通用的多智能体偏好学习框架（MAPL）来解决这些问题。MAPL允许通过将当前解与由各种智能体生成的去中心化或中心化解进行比较来进行迭代更新。我们使用带有学习到的奖励模型的来自人类反馈的MARL（MARLHF）以及多智能体直接偏好优化（MADPO）来实例化MAPL。在协作写作、编码、工具使用和旅行规划上的实验表明，MAPL能够提升协作质量和效率，同时接近具有固定、明确定义奖励的MARL的性能。在MAPL中，MARLHF在大多数任务上通常优于MADPO，但仍对数据覆盖、智能体和比较器模型以及底层MARL算法敏感。

Original abstract: Several works have explored multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in LLM collaboration. However, constructing reliable rewards is difficult in practice, as complete and accurate metrics are often unavailable and hard to aggregate. Preference learning provides an alternative by learning from comparative human or AI feedback. Yet, its extension to multi-agent systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we formulate preference-based multi-agent systems (MAS) from decentralized and centralized collaboration perspectives. We also introduce a general multi-agent preference learning framework (MAPL) to solve these problems. MAPL allows iterative updates by comparing the current solution with decentralized or centralized solutions generated by various agents. We instantiate MAPL using MARL from human feedback (MARLHF) with a learned reward model and multi-agent direct preference optimization (MADPO). Experiments on collaborative writing, coding, tool use, and travel planning show that MAPL can improve collaboration quality and efficiency while approaching the performance of MARL with fixed, well-defined rewards. Within MAPL, MARLHF generally outperforms MADPO on most tasks but remains sensitive to data coverage, agent and comparator models, and the underlying MARL algorithms.

</details>


### [99] [Counterfactual Self-Evolving Agents for Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32870)
*Xing Han,Yuxin Wang,Chen Chen,Wei Dai,Gautham Krishna Gudur,Shijun Li,Hsing-Huan Chung,Gregory D. Hager,Joydeep Ghosh,Paul Pu Liang,Suchi Saria*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 16 figures, 23 tables

Selection reason: 反事实自进化agent，含proposer-solver-verifier闭环、奖励整合与经验记忆，其验证反馈式自我改进机制可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出反事实自演化框架，通过可训练Proposer生成反事实证据编辑，结合Solver与Verifier反馈奖励，将接受的反事实存入记忆为冻结Solver提供上下文，提升临床推理、事实验证和商业推理等任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈提议者—求解者方法依赖生成可独立验证的合理案例，但在证据可识别任务中，答案由案例证据和领域知识决定，直接生成可验证案例较为困难。

Method: 构建专家验证的反事实指令微调数据训练Proposer；Proposer生成类别化证据编辑及因果解释；用整合Solver与Verifier反馈的微调奖励优化；接受的反事实累积到记忆，作为冻结Solver的上下文证据。

Result: 在多样反事实场景中，奖励偏好高质量反事实与合理修改，惩罚推翻正确决策的更改；在临床推理、事实核查和商业推理上，随反事实记忆增长性能持续提升，并可迁移至更难案例，在多种前沿模型上优于基线。

Conclusion: 反事实自演化使Solver通过演化上下文而非权重更新进行适应，能纠正错误并增强正确决策信心，为证据可识别推理任务提供有效自演化方案。

中文摘要：自对弈（self-play）提议者—求解者方法通过生成任务并从经过验证的解答中学习来提升推理能力。然而，对于证据可识别的任务，即由案例特定证据和领域知识决定可检验答案的任务，自对弈需要生成答案能够被独立验证的合理案例。我们引入反事实自演化（counterfactual self-evolution），它为重新审视原始案例生成反事实上下文。一个可训练的提议者（Proposer）构建有针对性的证据编辑，并通过因果解释描述潜在的结果变化。我们手工构建了一个经专家验证的反事实指令微调数据集，以教导提议者在广泛的动作—结果场景中生成高质量反事实。每个反事实指令微调样本都指定了定义类别内的一项编辑，并解释其对决策的假设性因果效应，从而教导提议者系统地推理改变了什么以及为什么改变。我们在这些样本上对提议者进行指令微调，然后制定一个整合求解者（Solver）和验证者（Verifier）反馈的微调奖励。在多样的反事实场景中，该奖励偏好高质量反事实和有理据的修改，同时惩罚那些推翻正确决策的更改。反事实上下文旨在纠正错误并增强对正确决策的信心。被接受的反事实会累积到记忆中，为冻结的求解者提供上下文内证据；求解者通过演化的上下文而非权重更新进行适应。我们将该框架应用于临床推理、事实核查和商业推理。我们的评估追踪随着反事实记忆增长，在连续轮次中的性能表现，包括向更难案例的迁移。我们的方法在多种前沿模型上取得了更优结果。

Original abstract: Self-play proposer--solver methods improve reasoning by generating tasks and learning from verified solutions. However, for evidence-identifiable tasks, where case-specific evidence and domain knowledge determine a checkable answer, self-play requires generating plausible cases whose answers can be independently verified. We introduce counterfactual self-evolution, which generates counterfactual context for reconsidering the original case. A trainable Proposer constructs targeted evidence edits and describes potential outcome changes with causal explanations. We handcraft an expert-verified counterfactual instruction-tuning dataset to teach the Proposer to generate high-quality counterfactuals across a broad range of action--outcome scenarios. Each counterfactual instruction-tuning example specifies an edit within a defined category and explains its hypothesized causal effect on the decision, teaching the Proposer to reason systematically about what changes and why. We instruction-tune the Proposer on these examples, then formulate a fine-tuning reward that integrates feedback from the Solver and Verifier. Across diverse counterfactual scenarios, this reward favors high-quality counterfactuals and warranted revisions, while penalizing changes that overturn correct decisions. The counterfactual context aims to correct errors and strengthen confidence in correct decisions. Accepted counterfactuals accumulate in memory that supplies in-context evidence to the frozen Solver; the Solver adapts through evolving context rather than weight updates. We apply the framework to clinical reasoning, fact verification, and business reasoning. Our evaluation tracks performance over successive rounds as counterfactual memory grows, including transfer to harder cases. Our method achieves superior results across diverse frontier models.

</details>


### [100] [StraTune: Adaptive Selection of Revision Operators for Self-Evolving LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32886)
*Zeping Liu,Yan Li,Ni Lao,Gil Wolff,Gengchen Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: StraTune自适应选择技能修订算子，用执行反馈+候选小样本筛选/大样本验证驱动文本技能迭代，与SemOpt类的反馈式技能优化高度同构。

TL;DR: StraTune让冻结的优化器LLM根据优化状态自适应选择修订算子，在多个基准上超过固定、随机、调度和bandit策略，并能将小模型学到的技能迁移到更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常使用固定的修订算子、搜索策略和修订形式，但没有任何单一修订算子能在所有任务上始终最佳；反复使用不合适的算子会限制技能持续改进。

Method: 提出StraTune：每一轮由冻结的优化器LLM根据优化状态选择修订算子，优化状态包含当前执行反馈以及此前策略和形式的记录结果；各修订算子产生的候选技能经过候选评估，在小样本集上筛选增益与退化，并在更大样本集上验证；所有结果写回优化状态用于后续选择。

Result: 在四个基准和两种LLM设置下，StraTune在大多数设置中优于全部五个基线；消融显示自适应选择修订算子带来主要收益，固定、随机、按计划、bandit策略选择均得分更低；用小目标LLM学到的技能也能提升更强的LLM。

Conclusion: 自适应选择修订算子能有效提升基于执行反馈的文本技能调优，并具有跨模型迁移潜力；代码和学到的技能已公开。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）可以从执行反馈中学习可复用的文本技能，而无需更新其参数，但有效决定如何修订这些技能仍是一项关键挑战。现有方法通常依赖固定的修订算子、搜索策略以及在该策略下应用的修订形式。然而，我们观察到，没有单一修订算子能在所有任务上始终表现最佳，反复应用不合适的算子会限制进一步改进。我们提出StraTune（策略引导的技能调优），它让一个冻结的优化器LLM在每一轮根据优化状态选择修订算子，优化状态定义为当前执行反馈以及此前策略和形式的记录结果。来自每个修订算子的候选技能都要通过一次候选评估，该评估在小样本集上筛选增益和退化，并在更大的样本集上验证它们，且每个结果都会被写回优化状态以供后续选择。在四个基准和两种LLM设置中，StraTune在大多数设置下优于所有五个基线。消融实验将收益归因于修订算子的自适应选择，因为固定、随机、按计划以及bandit策略选择得分都更低，并且用小目标LLM学到的技能也能提升一个更强的LLM。代码和学到的技能可在https://github.com/seai-lab/StraTune获取。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can learn reusable textual skills from execution feedback without updating their parameters, but effectively deciding how to revise these skills remains a key challenge. Existing methods typically rely on a fixed revision operator, a search strategy and the revision forms applied under it. However, we observe that no single revision operator consistently performs best across tasks, and repeatedly applying an unsuitable operator can limit further improvement. We propose StraTune (strategy-guided skill tuning), which lets a frozen optimizer LLM choose the revision operator at every round from the optimization state, which is defined as the current execution feedback together with the recorded outcomes of earlier strategies and forms. Candidate skills from every revision operator pass one candidate evaluation, which screens for gains and regressions on a small sample set and validates them on a larger one, and every outcome is written back to the optimization state for later choices. Across four benchmarks and two LLM settings, StraTune outperforms all five baselines in most settings. Ablations attribute the gains to the adaptive choice of the revision operator, since fixed, random, scheduled, and bandit strategy choices all score lower, and skills learned with a small target LLM also improve a stronger one. Code and learned skills are available at https://github.com/seai-lab/StraTune.

</details>


### [101] [Planner-as-Router: Joint Plan-Time Model Routing for Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32917)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam,Gautam Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at AIxSET 2026. 8 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables. Data and Code in github: https://github.com/vsingh45/par-entbench

Selection reason: Planner-as-Router在规划阶段为子任务分配模型层级，属agent规划与工具/成本编排，可迁移到多步agent工作流设计。

TL;DR: PaR 将模型层级选择融入规划，为每个子任务分配小型/中型/前沿模型，在 EntBench 的 1,157 次评估中保持成本-准确率前沿；相比全前沿路由降本 44%，准确率降 2.9 个百分点，并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 LLM 智能体成本高昂：前沿模型每 token 成本可达小型模型的 25 倍，多步工作流会放大差距，因此需要更优的模型层级路由策略。

Method: PaR 在规划阶段分解查询时，直接为子任务分配模型规模层级，并全局查看工作流依赖；它无需单独路由器模型或训练数据。评估使用 EntBench，覆盖七类 54 个企业级智能体任务，并通过在实时数据库上执行生成的 SQL 和 MongoDB 查询评分；共 1,157 次评估、八种路由器、三个随机种子。

Result: PaR 保持在观察到的成本-准确率前沿：以可比成本在准确率上匹配汇点前沿启发式，以更低成本匹配忠实的 FrugalGPT 级联；相对全前沿路由降低成本 44%，准确率下降 2.9 个百分点。若干准确率差距处于 54 任务研究的 ±6 个百分点置信区间内。初步小试点还提示，廉价路由在组合式工作流上可能带来隐藏的累积惩罚，但该结果尚未验证。

Conclusion: 作者将 PaR 的优势表述为成本-准确率前沿位置，而非明确的准确率胜出；隐藏的复合惩罚被列为未来测量假设。PaR、EntBench 及所有评估代码开源。

中文摘要：在生产环境中运行大型语言模型（LLM）智能体很快就会变得昂贵。前沿模型（最大、能力最强的层级）准确，但每 token 成本可能是小型模型的 25 倍，而且一旦工作流将多次调用串联起来，这一差距就会累积。Planner-as-Router（PaR）从不同角度解决这一问题。它不是将模型层级选择留给下游的某个组件，而是把这一选择折叠进规划本身。当规划器将查询分解为子任务时，它也为每个子任务分配一个模型规模层级（小型、中型或前沿型，按能力和价格排序），因此任何专家模型运行之前，子任务之间的依赖关系就可见。与级联路由等逐调用路由器一次只查看一个节点不同，PaR 预先看到整个工作流，并且不需要单独的路由器模型或训练数据。我们用 EntBench 评估 PaR，这是一个包含七类 54 个企业级智能体任务的基准，通过实际在实时数据库上运行生成的结构化查询语言（SQL）和 MongoDB 查询来评分。在涵盖八种路由器和三个随机种子的 1,157 次评估中，PaR 保持在观察到的成本-准确率前沿上。它在准确率上以可比成本匹配汇点前沿启发式（仅在终端节点使用前沿模型），并以更低成本匹配忠实的 FrugalGPT 级联，同时相对于全前沿路由将成本降低 44%，但牺牲 2.9 个百分点的准确率。若干准确率差距落在 54 任务研究的 ±6 个百分点置信区间内，因此我们将 PaR 的优势表述为前沿位置，而不是明确的准确率胜出。我们还报告一个初步观察，而非经过验证的结果：一个小型试点提示，廉价路由可能在组合式工作流上带有隐藏的累积惩罚，我们将其表述为未来测量的假设。PaR、EntBench 以及所有评估代码均开源。

Original abstract: Running large language model (LLM) agents in production gets expensive fast. A frontier model (the largest, most capable tier) is accurate but can cost 25 times what a small model costs per token, and the gap compounds once a workflow chains several calls together. Planner-as-Router (PaR) attacks this from a different angle. Instead of leaving model-tier selection to some component downstream, it folds the choice into planning itself. As the planner breaks a query into subtasks, it also assigns each one a model size tier (small, mid, or frontier, ordered by capability and price), so the dependencies between subtasks are visible before any specialist runs. Unlike per-call routers such as cascade routing, which look at one node at a time, PaR sees the whole workflow up front and needs no separate router model or training data. We evaluate PaR with EntBench, a benchmark of 54 enterprise agentic tasks across seven classes, graded by actually running the generated Structured Query Language (SQL) and MongoDB queries against live databases. Over 1,157 evaluations spanning eight routers and three seeds, PaR stays on the observed cost-accuracy frontier. It matches a sink-frontier heuristic (frontier model on terminal nodes only) in accuracy at comparable cost and a faithful FrugalGPT cascade at lower cost, and cuts cost 44% against all-frontier routing while giving up 2.9 points of accuracy. Several accuracy gaps fall inside the plus-or-minus six-point confidence interval of a 54-task study, so we frame PaR's advantage as frontier position rather than a clean accuracy win. We also report a preliminary observation, not a validated result: a small pilot hints that cheap routing may carry a hidden compounding penalty on compositional workflows, which we frame as a hypothesis for future measurement. PaR, EntBench, and all evaluation code are open source.

</details>


### [102] [Certified Long-Horizon Code Agent Evolution via Validation-Gated Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32990)
*Yifan Wang,Hao Cheng,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Dongwon Jung,Hao Tang,Keru Wang,Chenliang Zhou,Qianhui Wu,Wenlin Yao,Ananth Grama,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Baolin Peng,Jaron Lanier,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: VALVE面向长程仓库级代码agent的验证门控技能自进化，含收敛保证、回撤控制与1000+ SWE任务实证，直接契合SemOpt方向。

TL;DR: VALVE 是面向长视界仓库级软件工程的无权重更新自演化框架，通过验证门控实现稳定技能优化，在 1,000+ SWE 任务上提升 14.9/16.5 点并减少 75% 回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界智能体部署需持续积累可复用技能，但现有自演化方法多聚焦短视界，且在仓库级软件工程中易出现不稳定更新、性能回撤与智能体崩溃。

Method: 形式化上下文内自演化，提出验证门控的 VALVE 框架，给出有限收敛、未来任务增益与回撤保证，以及给定容差下验证集和评估集留出规模及其前导阶缩放。

Result: 在超过 1,000 个 SWE 任务上稳定自提升，三个前沿模型平均最终/峰值增益为 14.9/16.5 点；验证门控降低平均回撤 75%，技能库比无门控紧凑 11 倍；消融揭示关键设计选择。

Conclusion: 验证门控可实现长期稳定的无权重更新技能优化，并为长视界仓库级智能体自演化提供理论与经验依据。

中文摘要：长期智能体自演化无需更新模型权重，对于使已部署智能体能够积累可复用技能并随时间提升至关重要。以往的自演化工作主要关注短视界任务，而仓库级软件工程尽管是长期适应的理想试验台，却仍未得到探索。在此场景中，智能体需要解决一系列顺序任务，在演化的仓库中处理复杂依赖，并持续存储和复用经验。基于文本的技能优化为此类适应提供了一种高效、非参数化的方法。然而，现有方法在扩展部署中常面临更新不稳定、性能回撤和智能体崩溃等问题。本文形式化了上下文内自演化（in-context self-evolution）的概念，并提出了 VALVE，一个用于长视界技能优化的验证门控框架。我们建立了有限收敛，为未来任务增益和回撤提供了理论保证，并推导出在给定容差下所需的验证与评估留出集规模及其前导阶缩放。在实证中，我们的流程 VALVE 在跨越超过 1,000 个 SWE 任务的演化视界上实现了稳定的自我改进，在三个前沿模型（GPT-5.5、Claude-4.6 和 MiniMax-M2.7）上平均最终增益和峰值增益分别为 14.9 和 16.5 个点。验证门控将平均回撤降低了 75%，并产生比无门控演化紧凑 11 倍的技能库。我们进一步提供了大量消融实验，识别出对长视界技能演化最关键的设计选择。

Original abstract: Long horizon agent self-evolution without model weight updates is essential for enabling deployed agents to accumulate reusable skills and improve over time. Prior self-evolution work has focused primarily on short-horizon tasks, while repository-level software engineering remains unexplored despite being an ideal testbed for long-horizon adaptation. In this setting, agents are required to solve streams of sequential tasks, navigate complex dependencies with evolving repositories and persistently store and reuse experience. Text-based skill optimization offers an efficient, non-parametric approach for such adaptation. However, existing methods often suffer from unstable updates, performance drawdown, and agent collapse over extended deployments. In this paper, we formalize the concept of in-context self-evolution and introduce VALVE, a validated-gated framework for long-horizon skill optimization. We establish finite convergence, provide theoretical guarantees for future-task gain and drawdown, and derive the validation and evaluation holdout sizes required for a prescribed tolerance, with leading-order scaling
  Empirically, our pipeline, VALVE achieves stable self-improvement over evolution horizon spanning more than 1,000 SWE tasks, with average final and peak gains of $14.9$ and $16.5$ points across three frontier models (GPT-5.5, Claude-4.6 and MiniMax-M2.7). The validation gate reduces average drawdown by 75% and produces an 11x more compact skill bank than ungated evolution. We further present extensive ablations identifying the design choices most critical to long-horizon skill evolution.

</details>


### [103] [X-Tree: Tokenizing Reusable Experience for Efficient Agent Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32993)
*Sitao Cheng,Xunjian Yin,Zhiyuan Sun,Yuxuan Li,Ruiwen Zhou,Xiangru Jian,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Project in Progress. Homepage: https://sitaocheng.github.io/xtree/. Code: https://github.com/sitaocheng/X-Tree/

Selection reason: X-Tree从轨迹中挖掘可复用技能层级并用于离线/在线RL与自蒸馏，其经验复用与层级技能结构对自动代码优化agent有借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出从数据中自动恢复可复用技能层级并构建 X-Tree，在离线 RL、在线 RLVR 与同策略自蒸馏中训练多步智能体，在多个基准和模型规模上稳定提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多步智能体在扁平动作流上训练，SFT 和 RLVR 均匀加权每个 token，忽略跨任务重复出现的子过程与可复用层级，导致稀缺轨迹利用不足；上下文技能方案虽利用结构，但技能不进入权重，收益难以泛化。

Method: 无需 LLM 调用，按可复用性对动作片段打分，将规范化动作合并为可复用的经验树 X-Tree；每个节点表示频繁且能带来成功的技能如何由子技能组成。将 X-Tree 用于离线 RL 的训练实例、在线 RLVR 的自适应技能奖励、以及同策略自蒸馏中自教师的特权上下文。

Result: 在 WebArena、ScienceWorld 和 WebShop 三个基准、三种模型规模下，于匹配数据和预算时，X-Tree 相比标准方法最高提升 WebArena 4.5% SR、ScienceWorld 5.8% SR、WebShop 4.1% 成功率；匹配分析将收益归因于 X-Tree 结构与三种集成。

Conclusion: 从数据本身恢复并训练可复用技能层级，可更充分地利用轨迹内容，提升多步智能体的泛化能力和任务成功率。

中文摘要：多步智能体在扁平动作流上训练：SFT 和 RLVR 对每个 token 均匀加权，忽略了跨任务重复出现的子过程，即那种使人类能够从可复用例程进行自顶向下规划的层级结构。该结构未被利用，扁平训练对每条稀缺轨迹的利用程度低于其内容所允许的程度。近期智能体确实使用了该结构，但仅作为 LLM 编写的技能置于上下文中，从未进入权重，因此其收益无法泛化到检索之外。我们改为从数据本身恢复该层级并据此训练，无需调用 LLM。沿用仅通过计数构建词表的文本分词器思路，我们按可复用性对动作片段打分，并将规范化动作合并为可复用的经验树（X-Tree）。每个 X-Tree 节点刻画一个频繁且能带来成功的技能如何由子技能组成，从而引导高效泛化。我们将 X-Tree 集成到三种训练设置：离线 RL，其中每个节点作为一个训练实例；在线 RLVR，使用自适应技能奖励；以及同策略自蒸馏，将 X-Tree 作为自教师的特权上下文。在三种模型规模上，跨 WebArena、ScienceWorld 和 WebShop，在匹配数据和预算下，X-Tree 相比标准方法提升最高达 WebArena 4.5% SR、ScienceWorld 5.8% SR 和 WebShop 4.1% 成功率。匹配分析将收益归因于 X-Tree 结构及这三种集成。

Original abstract: Multi-step agents are trained on flat action streams: SFT and RLVR weight every token uniformly and ignore the sub-procedures that recur across tasks, the hierarchy that lets humans plan top-down from reusable routines. This structure sits unused, and flat training uses each scarce trajectory less fully than its content allows. Recent agents do use that structure, but only as LLM-written skills in context, never in the weights, so their gains do not generalize beyond retrieval. We instead recover this hierarchy from the data itself and train on it, with no LLM calls. Following text tokenizers, which build a vocabulary by counting alone, we score action spans by reusability and merge canonicalized actions into a reusable eXperience tree (X-Tree). Each X-Tree node captures how a frequent and success-bearing skill is composed from sub-skills, guiding efficient generalization. We integrate X-Tree into three training settings: offline RL, with each node as a training instance; online RLVR, with an adaptive skill bonus; and on-policy self-distillation, with X-Tree as the self-teacher's privileged context. Across WebArena, ScienceWorld, and WebShop at three model scales, X-Tree improves over standard recipes at matched data and budget by up to 4.5% SR on WebArena, 5.8% SR on ScienceWorld and 4.1% success on WebShop. Matched analyses attribute the gains to the X-Tree structure and the three integrations.

</details>


### [104] [When Pair Count Is Not the Sample Size: What All-Pairs Agent Comparisons Estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33012)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop TAE (Trust-AI-Eval): Can We Trust AI Evaluation?

Selection reason: 指出all-pairs agent对比中配对数的统计独立性陷阱，对agent评测（含可靠性能/加速比验证）的置信区间构造有方法学启示。

TL;DR: 全配对排行榜比较会因共享条目而虚增独立证据；论文区分固定榜单的精确汇总与将配置视为iid抽样的二阶U统计量，并用SWE-bench及精确模型证明共享配置导致区间过窄、覆盖率偏离名义水平，强调分析需明确固定与抽样对象及配对聚合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准排行榜常进行所有两两比较，比较次数远多于独立证据；A对B、A对C共享A，若不区分分析目标，可能错误估计不确定性。

Method: 从固定榜单与iid未来配置两种解释出发，以近似并列为例，并使用固定SWE-bench Verified快照和真值已知的精确二值模型比较不同推断；另用两个固定排行榜检验配对集合与加权的影响。

Result: SWE-bench上考虑共享配置的区间宽度超过配对iid参照的两倍；精确模型中，边共享端点时pair-iid覆盖率远低于名义水平，而匹配独立边接近名义水平；其他固定排行榜的精确汇总还取决于包含哪些配对及如何加权。

Conclusion: 全配对分析必须明确说明什么是固定的、什么是抽样的，以及如何处理共享条目和配对聚合，否则推断可能失真。

中文摘要：当一个智能体基准对排行榜条目进行所有两两比较时，比较次数可能看起来远多于其背后的独立证据：A 对 B 和 A 对 C 都重复使用了 A。这种重复使用是否影响推断，取决于该分析旨在描述什么。如果榜单及其结果是固定的，那么所有配对均值就是对这些条目的精确汇总，任何区间都必须来自另一个已声明的随机性来源。如果这些条目反而被视为来自未来配置总体的独立同分布（iid）抽样，并且配对规则是正则且非退化的，那么同一个均值就是一个二阶 U 统计量，其一阶不确定性取决于配置的数量，而不是配对的数量。我们以近似并列作为贯穿示例，但当其他对称配对汇总满足其正则性条件时，这一区分也可推广。我们使用一个固定的 SWE-bench Verified 快照和一个真值已知的精确二值模型来考察这两种解释。在 SWE-bench 上，考虑了共享配置的区间宽度超过将配对视为独立的配对 iid 参照的两倍。在精确模型中，当边共享端点时，配对 iid 覆盖率远低于名义水平，而对于匹配的独立边，覆盖率则接近名义水平。另外两个固定排行榜上的结果表明，精确汇总还取决于包含哪些配对以及如何对其加权。因此，全配对分析必须说明什么是固定的、什么是抽样的，以及它如何处理共享条目和配对聚合。

Original abstract: When an agent benchmark compares every pair of leaderboard entries, the number of comparisons can look much larger than the independent evidence behind them: A versus B and A versus C both reuse A. Whether this reuse affects inference depends on what the analysis is meant to describe. If the board and its outcomes are fixed, the all-pairs mean is an exact summary of those entries, and any interval must come from another declared source of randomness. If the entries are instead treated as iid draws from a population of future configurations and the pair rule is regular and nondegenerate, the same mean is an order-two U-statistic whose first-order uncertainty depends on the number of configurations, not the number of pairs. We use near ties as the running example, but the distinction extends to other symmetric pair summaries when their regularity conditions hold. We examine both interpretations using a fixed SWE-bench Verified snapshot and an exact binary model with known truth. On SWE-bench, intervals that accounted for shared configurations were more than twice as wide as a pair-iid reference that treated the pairs as independent. In the exact model, pair-iid coverage fell far below the nominal level when edges shared endpoints but remained near nominal for matched independent edges. Results on two other fixed leaderboards show that exact summaries also depend on which pairs are included and how they are weighted. An all-pairs analysis must therefore state what is fixed, what is sampled, and how it handles shared entries and pair aggregation.

</details>


### [105] [The Epistemics of Agent Memory: Measuring, and Governing, the Consolidation Decision in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33013)
*Sasank Annapureddy,Anjaneya Prasad Thamatani*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 4 tables, no figures. A four-phase research program. Experiments executed and adversarially verified by PRIMA (arXiv:2605.24775); per-phase result files and preregistrations available to reviewers on request; certain mechanism internals held under controlled release (see Section 7)

Selection reason: 系统研究长程LLM agent的记忆整合决策（保留/压缩/抽象/遗忘），提出ConsolidationBench与治理化整合，直接契合并可迁移到agent经验复用。

TL;DR: 该研究将 LLM 智能体长期记忆问题重构为巩固决策的质量与可信度问题：四阶段从情节边界与经验提升，到 ConsolidationBench，再到治理式巩固；发现治理与质量分数可区分，但质量分数未能预测真实迁移准确率，最终无过度主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体需将经验转为持久记忆，面临保留、压缩、抽象为可复用技能与规则、遗忘等巩固决策；研究动机是让评价重点从记忆量转向巩固决策质量及其可信度，并检验相关指标的外部效度。

Method: 四阶段研究：1）用下游效用从智能体轨迹学习情节边界，并建立三闸门防泄漏协议；2）在 token 预算下学习经验提升时机与抽象层级；3）提出 ConsolidationBench，按构造含 oracle，在三个非循环轴上评分；4）提出治理式巩固，加入抗投毒、可逆性、可审计性与质量门；最后进行对抗性自我批判。

Result: 阶段 1 的 oracle 相关性为 0.691，未达 0.70 门槛；阶段 2 取得 +22.7% 任务成功率与 7 倍压缩，但暴露退化性遗忘与 lambda-prevalence coupling；阶段 3 中生产检索系统跨层级迁移得分为零；阶段 4 中治理与质量分数统计上不同（r²=0.43，偏 r=0.27，相同质量策略治理相差三倍）；负面结果为双基准 2,532 个真实答案单元中质量分数不预测真实迁移准确率（合并 Spearman ρ=-0.24，n=12，置信区间跨零）；对抗性自我批判后无过度主张。

Conclusion: 巩固问题的关键从智能体记住多少转向巩固决策是否足够好、是否可信；治理可作为独立于质量指标外部效度的贡献，而质量分数的外部效度未获支持；该研究以严格自我批判收敛到无过度主张的结论。

中文摘要：长时程 LLM 智能体必须将累积经验转化为持久记忆，决定保留什么、压缩什么、将什么抽象为可复用技能和规则，或遗忘什么。我们报告一项关于这一巩固问题的四阶段研究计划，其核心发现是测量对象的转移：从智能体记住多少，转向其巩固决策是否足够好，再转向这些决策是否可信。
阶段 1 通过下游效用从智能体轨迹中学习情节边界；这是一次诚实的近失（oracle 相关性 0.691，未达到 0.70 门槛），其持久产出是一个三闸门防泄漏协议。阶段 2 学习在 token 预算下何时提升经验以及提升到哪个抽象层级，实现了经核验的 +22.7% 任务成功率提升与 7 倍压缩，但暴露了一个退化性遗忘失败，以及一个我们命名为 lambda-prevalence coupling（λ-流行度耦合）的分布偏移失败模式。阶段 3 提出 ConsolidationBench，一个按构造具有 oracle 的基准，在三个非循环轴上根据已知最优值对巩固决策评分；生产检索系统保留信息，但在跨层级迁移上得分为零。阶段 4 提出治理式巩固：将决策包裹在抗投毒、可逆性和可审计性保证中，并配以质量门。治理与质量分数在统计上不同（r² = 0.43；偏相关 r = 0.27；相同质量的策略在治理上相差三倍），因此该贡献独立于该指标的外部效度而成立。关于该问题，我们报告一个已解决的负面结果：在分级复用重新设计移除结构性上限后，一项包含 2,532 个真实答案单元的双基准研究发现，质量分数不能预测真实迁移准确率（合并 Spearman ρ = -0.24，n = 12，置信区间跨零）。一次对抗性自我批判通过清除了最终主张集，未留下任何过度主张。

Original abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must convert accumulated experience into durable memory, deciding what to keep, compress, abstract into reusable skills and rules, or forget. We report a four-phase research program on this consolidation problem whose central finding is a shift in what is measured: from how much an agent remembers, to whether its consolidation decisions are any good, to whether those decisions can be trusted.
  Phase 1 learns episodic boundaries from agent traces by downstream utility; an honest near-miss (oracle correlation 0.691 vs a 0.70 bar) whose lasting output is a three-gate anti-leakage protocol. Phase 2 learns when to promote experience and to which abstraction level under a token budget, achieving a verified +22.7% task-success improvement with 7x compression, but exposing a degenerate-forgetting failure and a distribution-shift failure mode we name lambda-prevalence coupling. Phase 3 introduces ConsolidationBench, an oracle-by-construction benchmark that scores consolidation decisions against a known optimum on three non-circular axes; production retrieval systems retain information yet score zero on cross-level transfer. Phase 4 introduces governed consolidation: the decision wrapped in poison-resistance, reversibility, and auditability guarantees with a quality gate. Governance is statistically distinct from the quality score ($r^2 = 0.43$; partial $r = 0.27$; identical-quality policies differ threefold in governance), so the contribution survives independently of the metric's external validity. On that question we report a resolved negative: after a graded-reuse redesign removed a structural ceiling, a two-benchmark study with 2,532 real answer cells finds the quality score does not predict real transfer accuracy (pooled Spearman $ρ= -0.24$, n = 12, CI spanning zero). An adversarial self-critique pass cleared the final claim set with zero surviving overclaims.

</details>


### [106] [Trust and Task Completion in the World of Consumer AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33017)
*Jeroen Olieslagers,Eduardo Pujol,Gal Zahavi,Lukas Ingemarsson,Shivani Poddar*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: 以同一批运行同时度量action agent的信任与任务完成度，强调harness/guardrail作用，对agent可靠性评测与安全控制有参考价值。

TL;DR: 论文在仿真环境中对行动型智能体同时评估信任与完成度，设置陷阱及匹配对照，并比较 Fo、基础模型、关闭防护栏的 Fo 和 OpenClaw；Fo 完成 71% 任务并在 94% 陷阱运行中保持信任，优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行动型智能体会代用户发邮件、花钱、打电话，错误可能在无人察觉时变成行动；其失败主要是破坏信任或完成度不足，且高度依赖模型外围的指令、工具、上下文与防护栏，因此需要在系统层面同时衡量信任与完成度。

Method: 构建评估：在同一批运行中为信任和完成度打分；模拟商家拥有网站、收件箱和电话线，模拟用户会回答助手问题；设置陷阱及匹配对照，对照中行动正确；用该评估比较 Fo（Wajo 个人助理）、在三个基础模型上仅配基本指令的基线模型、关闭防护栏的 Fo 外壳，以及同等访问权限的开源助理 OpenClaw。

Result: Fo 完成 71% 任务，在 94% 陷阱运行中保持信任；基线模型完成 50%–64%，信任保持 59%–75%；匹配对照中 Fo 略微更少直接行动；OpenClaw 在共有任务上完成 42%（Fo 71%），在共有陷阱运行中保持信任 74%（Fo 94%）。

Conclusion: 应在整个系统而非仅模型上同时衡量信任与完成度，这是让行动型智能体可安全承接真实工作的关键路径。

中文摘要：行动型智能体为人们做事。它们会在用户忙于其他事情时发送电子邮件、花钱、致电商家，因此一个错误可能在任何人注意到之前就变成一项行动。它们会以两种方式令用户失望。当它们做了用户从未同意的事，或在用户明确说“开始”之后却退缩不前时，就破坏了信任。而当它们放弃那些被证明困难的任务时，就未能完成。这两者都在很大程度上取决于模型周围的外壳，即其指令、工具、上下文和防护栏。我们构建了一个评估，在同一批运行中同时为信任和完成度打分，评估发生在一个模拟世界中，其中有拥有自己网站、收件箱和电话线的商家，以及会回信的人。一个模拟用户回答助手的问题。信任意味着不会发生用户未同意的事情。不会向用户从未批准的人发送电子邮件，不会有私人细节出现在群组线程中，不会花费超过用户限额的钱，不会听从陌生人的指令，也不会在没有来源的情况下作出任何声称。每个陷阱都有一个匹配对照，在该对照中采取行动是正确的选择。我们用该评估来衡量 Fo——Wajo 的个人助理——并与在三个基础模型上配有基本指令的基础模型进行比较，也与关闭防护栏的 Fo 外壳进行比较。Fo 完成了 71% 的任务，并在 94% 的陷阱运行中保持了用户信任。基础模型完成 50% 到 64%，并在 59% 到 75% 的陷阱运行中保持信任。在匹配对照中，Fo 继续行动的比例略低。OpenClaw，一个被给予相同访问权限的热门开源助理，完成了与 Fo 共有的任务的 42%，而 Fo 为 71%，并在共有的陷阱运行中的 74% 保持了用户信任，而 Fo 为 94%。将信任和完成度放在一起衡量，针对整个系统而非仅模型，这就是我们认为行动型智能体能够安全地承接真实工作的方式。

Original abstract: Action agents do things for people. They send email, spend money, and call businesses while the user is busy with something else, so a mistake can turn into an action before anyone notices. They fail their users in two ways. They break trust when they do something the user never agreed to, or hold back after the user clearly said go. And they fall short on completion when they give up on errands that turn out to be hard. Both depend heavily on the harness around the model, meaning its instructions, tools, context, and guardrails. We built an evaluation that scores trust and completion on the same runs, in a simulated world of businesses with their own websites, inboxes, and phone lines, and of people who write back. A simulated user answers the assistant's questions. Trust means that nothing happens the user did not agree to. No email goes to someone they never approved, no private detail ends up on a group thread, no money is spent past their limit, no stranger's instructions are followed, and nothing is claimed without a source. Every trap has a matched control in which acting is the right call. We use the evaluation to measure Fo, Wajo's personal assistant, against a base model with basic instructions on three foundation models, and against the Fo harness with its guardrails switched off. Fo completes 71% of the errands and keeps the user's trust on 94% of the trap runs. The base models complete 50% to 64% and keep trust on 59% to 75%. On the matched controls, Fo goes ahead slightly less often. OpenClaw, a popular open-source assistant given the same access, completes 42% of the errands it shares with Fo, against 71%, and keeps the user's trust on 74% of the shared trap runs, against 94%. Measuring trust and completion together, on the whole system rather than the model alone, is how we think action agents become safe to hand real work to.

</details>


### [107] [Agent Safety From Within: Detecting Harmful Trajectories from LLM Internal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33039)
*Difan Jiao,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages, 11 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 从LLM内部状态读取agent轨迹安全性，提出TACIT探针，属于agent可靠性与安全控制新方法，可迁移到对代码优化agent轨迹的监控。

TL;DR: 提出 TACIT：从冻结模型内部状态线性读出轨迹安全性，无需解码 token，在多个轨迹安全基准上显著优于开源防护模型，且训练参数极少、延迟低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护模型主要面向内容审核，难以检测智能体通过工具执行复杂动作序列所带来的轨迹级风险，因此需要更好的轨迹安全检测方法。

Method: 先对开源防护模型进行表征分析，聚焦有害内容与不安全工具使用两类轨迹级危害；随后提出 TACIT，对冻结主干网络的内部状态进行读出、不解码任何 token，并在六个轨迹安全基准上训练线性探针与精炼读出。

Result: 线性探针将平均 macro-F1 从最强开源防护模型的 62.3 提升到 80.7，精炼读出达到 86.2；在留出基准上精炼读出为 65.7，仍高于最强防护模型的 61.1；冻结读出与完整安全微调相当，叠加应用还能进一步改善微调模型；训练参数量约为完整微调的百万分之一，时间约为其六分之一，且延迟最低。

Conclusion: 直接从模型内部状态读取轨迹安全性是有效且高效的，TACIT 在性能、泛化、训练成本和延迟方面均优于或补益现有防护模型与完整安全微调。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体现已能够通过工具和运行框架执行复杂的动作序列，这扩大了它们可能造成的损害范围。然而，防护模型主要针对内容审核构建，因此并不适合检测这种智能体风险。为解决这一问题，我们首先进行表征分析，然后利用所得洞见构建解决方案。在分析中，我们聚焦两类轨迹级智能体危害：有害内容，它被直接表达；以及不安全工具使用，它取决于某个动作是否与产生该动作的交互相一致。我们研究开源防护模型如何表示这两类危害，发现它们在模型内部是线性可读的，尽管防护模型在仅在被调用工具的 schema 上不同的成对样本上预测表现不优于随机。这两类危害还遵循几乎正交的内部方向，且二者都不能可靠地作为对方的代理。这些结果促使我们直接从内部状态读取轨迹安全性。我们提出 TACIT，一种对冻结主干网络内部状态的读出，不解码任何 token。在六个轨迹安全基准上训练后，线性探针将最强开源防护模型的平均 macro-F1 从 62.3 提高到 80.7，而精炼读出达到 86.2。在将每个基准完全排除于训练之外时，精炼读出仍领先最强防护模型（65.7 对 61.1）。在相同主干网络、训练数据和测试划分下，冻结读出与完整安全微调相当，并且将其叠加应用时可进一步提升微调模型。该探针训练的参数量约为完整微调的百万分之一，训练时间约为其六分之一，并且在我们评估的防护模型中，TACIT 具有最低延迟。

Original abstract: Language model agents can now perform sophisticated sequences of actions via tools and harnesses, which has increased the scope of the damage they can cause. Guard models, however, are mainly built for content moderation and thus are not well-suited to detecting this agentic risk. To address this, we proceed by first conducting a representational analysis, then use the resulting insights to build a solution. In our analysis, we focus on two types of trajectory-level agentic harms: harmful content, which is expressed directly, and unsafe tool use, which depends on whether an action is consistent with the interaction that produced it. We investigate how open-source guard models represent these two types of harm and find that they are linearly readable inside the model, even though guard models predict no better than chance on pairs that differ only in the called tool's schema. The two harm types also follow nearly orthogonal internal directions, and neither reliably serves as a proxy for the other. These results motivate reading trajectory safety directly from internal states. We introduce TACIT, a readout of a frozen backbone's internal states that decodes no tokens. Trained on six trajectory-safety benchmarks, a linear probe raises mean macro-F1 from 62.3 for the strongest open guard to 80.7, and refined readouts reach 86.2. With each benchmark held out of training entirely, the refined readouts still lead the strongest guard (65.7 vs. 61.1). With the same backbone, training data and test split, the frozen readout is on par with full safety fine-tuning, and it improves the fine-tuned model further when applied on top. The probe trains about one millionth as many parameters as full fine-tuning in about a sixth of the time, and TACIT has the lowest latency of the guards we evaluate.

</details>


### [108] [BudgetVerify: Budget-Tiered Verification for Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33052)
*Janet Jenq,Hongda Shen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 按预算分层路由验证（不验证/轻量校验/先解后比），其选择性验证分配思路可迁移到coding agent的验证成本控制。

TL;DR: 提出 BudgetVerify，按预算分层路由生成答案至无验证、轻量修订或强验证，在六个模型上取得更优准确率-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答依赖数值抽取、单位与算术，统一昂贵验证浪费测试时算力，需要按需分配验证预算。

Method: 训练路由器基于离线正确性和 token 成本，在验证前信息（问题、上下文统计、生成答案、生成器元数据）上选择三种验证层级之一。

Result: 六个商用与开放权重基础模型上，BudgetVerify 比固定验证策略获得更高效的准确率-成本帕累托前沿，且定性前沿形状一致。

Conclusion: 按需选择性验证可在不同生成器模型上稳定提升验证效率，避免统一强验证的算力浪费。

中文摘要：金融问答通常需要精确的数值抽取、单位处理以及跨表格和文本的算术运算，但统一施加昂贵的验证会浪费测试时计算。我们提出 BudgetVerify，一种预算分层的生成器-验证器框架，它将每个生成的答案路由到三种验证层级之一：无验证、轻量级检查并修订，或成本更高的先求解再比较验证。该路由器根据离线正确性与 token 成本结果进行训练，并在测试时利用验证前可获得的信息来选择验证层级，这些信息包括问题、上下文统计量、生成的答案以及相关的生成器元数据。所选层级要么直接返回生成的答案，要么调用相应的验证器。在六个商用和开放权重基础模型上，BudgetVerify 通过仅在有用时选择性地分配更强的验证，始终比固定验证策略产生更高效的准确率-成本帕累托前沿。尽管绝对性能因模型而异，但这些效率增益以及由此产生的定性前沿形状在生成器模型之间保持一致。

Original abstract: Financial question answering often requires precise numerical extraction, unit handling, and arithmetic over tables and text, but applying expensive verification uniformly wastes test-time compute. We propose BudgetVerify, a budget-tiered generator-verifier framework that routes each generated answer to one of three verification tiers: no verification, lightweight check-and-revise, or higher-cost solve-first-then-compare verification. The router is trained from offline correctness and token-cost outcomes and, at test time, selects a verification tier using information available before verification, including the question, context statistics, the generated answer, and associated generator metadata. The selected tier either returns the generated answer directly or invokes the corresponding verifier. Across six commercial and open-weight base models, BudgetVerify consistently produces more efficient accuracy-cost Pareto frontiers than fixed verification policies by selectively allocating stronger verification only when it is useful. Although absolute performance varies across models, these efficiency gains and the resulting qualitative frontier shape are consistent across generator models.

</details>


### [109] [LLM sequential decision making under uncertainty in biochemical domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33061)
*Mattias Akke,Soojung Yang,Jurgis Ruža,Sathya Edamadaka,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 在贝叶斯优化场景下测量LLM的信念与动作，揭示其过度反应与上下文粘性导致的探索失败，对agent决策与探索设计有实证启发。

TL;DR: 该研究在七个组合数据集上对五种前沿 LLM 进行贝叶斯优化基准测试，结合信念/行动测量与提示消融，发现 LLM 常对数据过度反应、未能稳定超越统计基线，并因上下文黏性而“意图探索却行动利用”；移除上下文历史可恢复探索，提示需解耦先验与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将 LLM 用于紧张实验预算下的实验设计前，必须理解其如何依据新数据和文献记忆做出决策；但现有研究智能体的性能分数无法揭示其决策策略。

Method: 在贝叶斯优化设置中基准测试五个前沿 LLM，并与已发表统计基线比较，覆盖蛋白质工程、反应优化、分子设计、肽自组装和催化七个组合数据集；同时直接测量模型信念与行动，并使用逐步剥离上下文的提示消融区分记忆、化学推理和裸分类优化；采用校正测量噪声偏差后的信念移动和鞅诊断分析行为。

Result: 先验化学知识在期望上有帮助，但方差高且有时损害性能；没有配置能跨领域决定性地超过平均统计基线；模型对传入数据反应过度而非固守先验；行动偏向利用，但模型真诚意图探索且按意图行动，失败源于上下文黏性；移除上下文历史可恢复探索。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动发现的有效性受限于先验与数据未解耦导致的上下文黏性；需将先验与数据解耦，才能实现更有效的探索与发现。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用于驱动科学发现。在信任它们能在紧张实验预算下设计实验之前，理解 LLMs 如何根据新数据和文献记忆做出决策至关重要。然而，在当前用于评估研究智能体的性能分数中，它们的决策策略是不可见的。在这里，我们在贝叶斯优化设置中对五个前沿 LLM 进行基准测试，并在覆盖蛋白质工程、反应优化、分子设计、肽自组装和催化的七个组合数据集上与已发表的统计基线进行比较。性能评估与对模型信念和行动的直接测量相结合，从而能够进行高分辨率的行为分析。一种逐步剥离上下文的提示消融将记忆与化学推理以及裸分类优化区分开来。先验化学知识在期望上有帮助，但方差很大，有时甚至损害性能。没有测试的配置能在各领域中决定性地超过平均统计基线。信念移动和鞅诊断——此处已针对一种将理性智能体误标为不理性的测量噪声偏差进行校正——表明在此处研究的情境中，模型对传入数据反应过度，而不是固守其先验。有趣的是，尽管 LLM 的行动是剥削性的，但模型真诚地意图探索，并始终按照该意图行动。这种失败是一种源于上下文黏性的能力差距。移除上下文历史可恢复探索，表明要实现有效的 LLM 驱动发现，必须将先验与数据解耦。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to drive scientific discovery. Understanding how LLMs make decisions from new data and memory of the literature is vital before trusting them to design experiments under tight experimental budgets. However, their decision strategies are invisible in the current performance scores used to evaluate research agents. Here, we benchmark five frontier LLMs in a Bayesian Optimization setting against published statistical baselines on seven combinatorial datasets spanning protein engineering, reaction optimization, molecular design, peptide self-assembly, and catalysis. Performance is paired with direct measurements of model beliefs and actions, enabling highly resolved behavior analysis. A prompt ablation that progressively strips context separates memorization from chemical reasoning and from bare categorical optimization. Prior chemical knowledge helps in expectation, but with high variance and occasionally even harms performance. No configuration tested decisively beats a mean statistical baseline across domains. Belief-movement and Martingale diagnostics, corrected here for a measurement-noise bias that mislabels rational agents as irrational, show that models overreact to incoming data rather than entrenching on their priors in the contexts studied here. Interestingly, while LLM actions are exploitative, models sincerely intend to explore and consistently act on that intent. This failure is a competence gap arising from context-stickiness. Removing in-context history restores exploration, indicating that priors and data must be decoupled to achieve effective LLM-driven discovery.

</details>


### [110] [The Model Knows Another Way: Strategy Switching for Effective RLVR Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33085)
*Jin Cui,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 提出PSRA把策略条件化提示作为探索臂做贝叶斯预算分配，其探索与策略复用机制可迁移到agent式强化学习训练。

TL;DR: 论文指出RLVR探索不足常源于有限采样偏向单一主导策略而非能力缺失，并提出PSRA：用贝叶斯序贯分配在无引导与策略条件化提示间竞争性分配rollout预算，同时保留策略路径并迁移成功行为，从而提升推理与分布外迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中困难问题常产生全错误rollout组，导致学习信号不足；但这类失败未必代表模型缺少能力。有限采样容易集中在问题特定的主导推理策略上，模型已支持的替代策略未被探索；且这些策略在RL中的可访问性会动态变化，有些被内化，有些在被吸收前变得难以引出。
